We introduce ORBIT (Omni-Range Bootstrap Incremental Training), a training paradigm that makes this distribution explicit and controllable. ORBIT combines Bootstrap Multi-Level Sampling, which controls dataset exposure and samples records, target variables, context windows, and prediction horizons, with Omni-Range Incremental Training, which varies context lengths and prediction horizons throughout a single training stage. Under ORBIT, we train Falcon-2.0, a simple univariate encoder-only Transformer with missingness-aware triple-channel patch tokenization and parallel patch prediction. We further introduce Rank-Guided Cross-Depth Alignment, a training objective that uses late-layer representations as stop-gradient teachers for shallow layers without additional inference cost. Evaluations on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench demonstrate strong zero-shot forecasting performance across diverse domains and frequencies.

</details>


### [234] [EEG Decoding Using CNN and LSTM Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13285)
*Athanasios Karagounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合CNN与双向长短期记忆网络（bi-LSTM）的混合深度学习架构，用于从原始运动想象脑电信号（MI-EEG）中自动学习特征并进行分类。CNN负责提取高层空间和时间表示，bi-LSTM建模特征间的时间依赖关系。在公开数据集和私有采集数据集上均验证了该方法的有效性，在二分类和三分类运动想象任务中表现出稳健性能，并显示出有前景的跨被试解码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动想象脑机接口（MI-BCI）对神经系统疾病患者具有重要应用价值，然而MI-EEG信号包含大量噪声，且与底层脑活动之间存在复杂且弱相关的映射关系，导致可靠的解码十分困难。虽然深度学习能从脑电信号中直接学习表征，但其在MI-EEG特征学习中的应用仍然有限，因此亟需开发更有效的深度学习架构来提升解码性能。

Method: 提出一种CNN与bi-LSTM相结合的混合深度学习架构。CNN从原始MI-EEG记录中学习高级空间和时间表征；bi-LSTM对CNN提取的特征进一步建模时间依赖关系与特征间的关联。模型在公开数据集和自建EEG采集系统获取的私有数据集上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该CNN&bi-LSTM混合架构在二类和三类运动想象分类任务中均表现出稳健的性能，并在所评估的方法中展现出有前景的跨被试（subject-independent）解码能力。

Conclusion: CNN与bi-LSTM的混合架构能够有效利用CNN的空间/时间特征提取能力和bi-LSTM的时间序列建模优势，为MI-EEG解码提供了一种稳健且具有泛化能力的方案，对运动想象BCI的实际应用具有积极意义。

Abstract: Motor imagery (MI) brain--computer interfaces (BCIs) have emerged as a promising approach for establishing flexible communication pathways between the human brain and external devices , particularly for individuals affected by stroke or neurodegenerative disorders. Reliable decoding of motor-imagery electroencephalography (MI-EEG) remains challenging because EEG recordings contain substantial noise and exhibit complex, weakly informative relationships with the underlying brain activity. Although deep learning provides an effective means of learning representations directly from EEG signals, its application to MI-EEG feature learning remains comparatively limited. This study introduces a hybrid deep-learning architecture that integrates a convolutional neural network (CNN) with a bidirectional long short-term memory (bi-LSTM) network. The CNN is used to learn high-level spatial and temporal representations directly from raw MI-EEG recordings, whereas the bi-LSTM models temporal dependencies and relationships among the extracted features. The proposed approach is evaluated using both a publicly available dataset and a privately acquired dataset obtained with an EEG acquisition system. The experimental results indicate that the CNN\&bi-LSTM architecture provides robust performance for both two- and three-class motor-imagery classification and demonstrates promising subject-independent decoding capability across the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [235] [Large-scale Testing Global Optimization Methods with Black-box Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13296)
*Wojciech Zarzecki,Jarosław Arabas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将黑盒对抗攻击（BBAA）任务用作高维全局优化基准，以替代规模有限、基于老式解析函数的传统基准套件，并验证了多种进化算法和元启发式算法在BBAA问题上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局优化基准套件规模中等且基于少量可追溯到1970年代的解析函数，可能导致全局优化方法开发产生偏差。需要更贴近现代机器学习挑战的新型基准。

Method: 论证BBAA任务可作为高维全局优化基准，并通过实验证明多种进化算法和其他元启发式算法在求解示例BBAA问题上的效率。

Result: 多种进化算法和元启发式算法能够在示例BBAA问题上高效求解，展示了BBAA作为全局优化基准的可行性和价值。

Conclusion: 该工作推动了全局优化方法与现代机器学习领域挑战和需求的融合。

Abstract: Existing global optimization benchmark suites are of a moderate size and are based on a small number of analytical functions that date back even to the 1970s. This causes a risk of biasing the development of global optimization methods. We argue that the tasks related to the black-box adversarial attack (BBAA) can serve as valuable global optimization benchmark in many-dimensional space. We demonstrate the efficiency of several types of evolutionary algorithms and other metaheuristics in solving example BBAA problems. Thus, we take a step towards convergence of global optimization methods to the challenges and needs that arise in the modern machine learning field.

</details>


### [236] [The Time Value of Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13297)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lineage-Value Policy Gradients（LVPG），一个基于长时域信用分配（lineage value）的actor-critic框架，用于自动化交易策略搜索。LVPG通过bootstrap的critic头估计多步突变树中有限时域谱系潜力价值，actor头动态调节剩余搜索预算下的突变强度，在90组配对实验中验证了路径级信用分配相比即时回报优化能显著加速有限预算搜索，验证集best-so-far AUC提升0.394 Sharpe单位，且减少临时回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在进化搜索中，弱小的后代可能是通往高适应度区域的宝贵祖先，而即时回报（immediate-return）控制对此类延迟效用是盲目的，会惩罚那些虽在当前代表现不佳但能开启有前途的后续谱系的突变。作者将这一隐藏动力学形式化为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程中的"时间价值"，旨在利用延迟回报来改进自动化交易策略发现。

Method: LVPG是一个长时域actor-critic框架，将搜索控制解耦为共享生成骨干上的专用策略头：bootstrap的critic头从多步突变树估计有限时域谱系潜力价值，actor头在剩余搜索预算内动态调节突变强度。在匹配算子、谱系监督、fold、种子和预算条件下，与即时回报优化进行了90组配对实验。

Result: 路径级信用分配显著加速有限预算搜索，验证集best-so-far AUC提升0.394 Sharpe单位。LVPG产生的临时回归比即时回报优化更少且恢复率更高，有限时域谱系价值带来更具选择性的非单调搜索，在相同资源约束下产生更强策略。

Conclusion: 长时域信用分配（谱系价值）是进化搜索中优于即时回报优化的重要机制，能够利用弱子代的延迟效用，在有限预算内实现更高效、更稳健的策略发现，对自动化交易策略搜索具有显著价值。

Abstract: In evolutionary search, a weak child can be a valuable ancestor that makes high-fitness regions reachable. Immediate-return control is blind to this delayed utility, penalizing mutations through their immediate offspring even when they open productive future lineages. We formalize this hidden dynamic as the time value of evolution within a finite-horizon Markov decision process. To exploit it, we introduce Lineage-Value Policy Gradients (LVPG), a long-horizon actor-critic framework for automated trading policy discovery. Our architecture decouples search control into specialized policy heads over a shared generative backbone: a bootstrapped critic head estimates the value of finite-horizon lineage potential from multi-step mutation trees, while an actor head dynamically modulates mutation intensity over the remaining search budget. We isolate the impact of long-horizon credit assignment against immediate-return optimization across 90 paired runs under matched operators, lineage supervision, folds, seeds, and budgets. Path-based credit assignment substantially accelerates finite-budget search, increasing validation best-so-far AUC by 0.394 Sharpe units. LVPG also produces fewer temporary regressions than immediate-return optimization and recovers from them more often. Finite-horizon lineage value yields more selective non-monotonic search and stronger policies within identical resource constraints.

</details>


### [237] [A Probe Direction Is a Property of Its Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13329)
*Valentin Noël*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出"评估感知探针"（evaluation-awareness probes）这一标准工具存在一个未披露的自由参数——即选择哪个提示词（prompt）来宣称"正在被评估"。研究发现，仅改变提示词选择而不改变任务文本，报告的分数甚至其随模型规模变化的趋势方向都会随之改变，导致两篇已发表且结论相反的研究可以在同一设计下仅靠提示词选择而复现。模型本身在该测量中仅贡献少量方差，大多数方差来自模型-提示词交互，且这些探针的划分很大程度上可由表面形式（surface form）单独分离。因此单提示词设计无法支撑跨模型比较，论文给出了可辩护比较所需的提示词数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的模型评估依赖模型在测试时表现一致，但若模型感知到被评估而行为不同，评估就失效。近期的研究方向是从模型激活中直接读出这种"被评估感"，然而该探针方法的有效性依赖于其测量设计是否可靠，本文旨在审视这一设计。

Method: 作者将"宣布评估的提示词"视为测量设计中的一个可变化的面（facet）而非实现细节，通过固定任务文本、仅变化提示词选择，进行方差分解分析，检验探针分数、趋势方向对提示词的敏感性；同时用仅含表面形式信息的无信息方向（direction）来复现已发表的探针分数，检验划分是否可由表面形式单独分离。

Result: 报告的探针分数甚至其随模型规模变化的趋势方向都随提示词选择而变，而非由模型决定；两篇关于趋势符号结论矛盾的研究可在同一设计下仅靠提示词复现。模型本身仅解释少量方差，大部分方差来自模型-提示词交互，增加评估条目无法修复测量，而变化提示词可以。无评估信息的表面形式方向仍可复现已发表分数的大部分。

Conclusion: 单提示词设计的评估感知探针无法支撑跨模型比较，测量需要多个提示词；作者给出了可辩护比较所需的提示词数量。提示词应被视作测量设计的一个维度而非实现细节。

Abstract: A model that behaves differently when it senses it is being tested would undermine the evaluations we rely on, so recent work has sought to read that sense directly from a model's activations. The standard instrument contrasts activations on prompts that announce an evaluation against prompts that do not, and reports how well the resulting direction separates held-out cases. That number is then compared across models and correlated with scale. We observe that the instrument has a free parameter its readings do not disclose: "a prompt that announces an evaluation" is not a prompt but a choice among many, and nothing in the method fixes which. Holding the task text fixed and varying only that choice, we find that the reported score, and even the direction in which it trends with model size, follows the prompt rather than the model; two published studies that disagree about the sign of that trend are both reproducible from a single design, by choice of prompt alone. Treating the prompt as a facet of a measurement design rather than an implementation detail, we find the model under study accounts for a small share of the variance in the number reported about it, and most of the rest lies in how each model responds to each prompt: collecting more evaluation items cannot repair the measurement, while varying prompts can. A further check finds that the split these probes are scored on is largely separable from surface form alone, so a direction carrying no information about evaluation at all still reproduces a substantial fraction of each published score. We conclude that a single-prompt design cannot support comparison between models, and we give the number of prompts a defensible comparison requires.

</details>


### [238] [Training AI Scientists to Replicate Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13331)
*Damon Falck,Samer Sabri,Anja Surina,Thom Foster,Anya Sims,Sam Devlin,Dylan Rogers,Tantum Collins,Kaloyan Aleksiev,Louis Kirsch,Edward Hughes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Replica，一个可扩展的论文复现任务空间，并训练了27B参数的AI科学家代理Faraday，其在复现任务上超越了Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-5.5，展示了AI在长时程科学创新方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文复现是科学知识的基石，但现有方法缺乏可扩展的复现任务空间和可靠的奖励信号。复现行为本质上类似开放式的假设驱动探索，需要开发能够自动化此过程的方法。作者旨在构建一个无需复杂框架的、能够进行长期科学探索的AI代理。

Method: 1) 构建Replica，一个可扩展的论文复现任务空间；2) 引入自动生成的基于rubric的评判器，具有低噪声且与人类评估结果一致；3) 后训练27B参数的Faraday AI科学家代理，利用编码代理作为工具来执行复现任务。

Result: Faraday在留出的复现任务上超越了Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-5.5。定性分析显示Faraday在单次rollout中采用了更科学、更有原则的探索方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为无需复杂harness即可实现长时程科学创新的AI代理提供了基础，标志着AI在科学研究自动化方面迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: The replicability of papers is a cornerstone of scientific knowledge, ensuring the reliability of existing results and providing a base for further experiments. The act of replication typically illuminates details that were previously underspecified, and thus requires similar hypothesis-driven exploration to open-ended research. In this work, we develop Replica, a scalable task space for paper replication. To provide reward signal, we introduce an auto-generated rubric-based judge that has low noise and agrees with human assessment of replication quality. We post-train Faraday, a 27B-parameter "AI Scientist" agent that leverages coding agents as tools, surpassing the performance of Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-5.5 on held-out replication tasks. Qualitative analysis of individual rollouts reveals that Faraday adopts a more scientifically-principled approach. We believe that our results provide a stepping stone towards AI agents capable of long-horizon scientific innovation without requiring complex harnesses.

</details>


### [239] [Neural Quadratic Forms: A Unified Minimal Model for Sudden Learning and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13335)
*Liu Ziyin,Yizhou Xu,Tomaso Poggio,Isaac Chuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了神经网络训练中台阶式损失下降与幂律损失曲线这两种看似矛盾行为的统一理论：通过对网络层置换对称性的分析，证明了在权重接近零的展开中，所有架构细节都收敛到一个通用的二次型结构，从而将各类架构（感知机、注意力层、混合专家、卷积）统一为同一模型；训练动力学在序参量上封闭，并可约化为Lotka-Volterra方程，解释了plateau与幂律这两种行为的起源及其转换条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降训练光滑代价函数的神经网络时，损失既可能呈现"长平台期后突然下降"的台阶式行为，也可能呈现光滑的幂律下降，二者在不同架构中均有出现。作者希望找到背后的统一机制——既然微观结构差异巨大的架构都表现出这几类行为，必然存在少数相关的集体变量在起支配作用。

Method: 作者从置换对称性出发：网络层是多个可互换单元的求和，重新标记这些单元不改变网络。结合光滑性以及单元梯度在原点处消失的条件，对称性强制了关于训练初期近零权重展开的普适前导形式，即二次型Tr[WW^T A(x)]，其中所有架构细节都压缩进单一的"结构矩阵"A(x)。据此可将动力学在序参量M=WW^T上闭合；当数据矩阵共享本征基时，进一步约化为Lotka-Volterra方程，其模式依次开启，初始权重越小，开启时间间隔越大。

Result: 平台现象是光滑流动的奇异极限：当大量模式不可分时，同一事件合并为训练时间上的幂律，其指数由理论预先给出。作者在多种训练方法和架构上均通过数值实验证实了这些预测。

Conclusion: 置换对称性将看似不同的架构统一为同一模型在不同结构矩阵A下的特例，为理解神经网络训练中离散台阶与连续幂律两种损失行为提供了统一的动力学框架，并首次给出预测幂律指数的理论工具。

Abstract: Neural networks trained by gradient descent on a smooth cost function can nevertheless learn in steps: the cost holds on long plateaus and then drops abruptly. Meanwhile, training losses instead follow smooth power laws. Variants of both behaviors occur in architectures with very different microscopic structures, which is the signature of a few relevant collective variables. We show that a symmetry fixes what those variables are: a network layer is a sum over interchangeable units, so relabeling the units leaves it unchanged; given smoothness and the condition that a unit's gradient vanish at the origin, symmetry then enforces a universal leading form for the expansion about the near-zero weights present at the start of training, the quadratic $\Tr[WW^{\top}A(x)]$, in which every architectural detail is confined to a single ``structure matrix" $A(x)$ that we compute for each architecture. Perceptrons, attention layers, mixtures of experts, and convolutions become one model at different $A$. Its training dynamics then close on the ``order parameter" $M=WW^{\top}$ and, whenever the data matrices share an eigenbasis, reduce to a Lotka--Volterra equation whose modes switch on one after another. The smaller the initial weights, the further apart the switch-on times, and the plateaus appear as a singular limit of a smooth flow; when many modes are unresolved the same events merge into a power law in training time whose exponent the theory predicts. We confirm both numerically across training methods and architectures.

</details>


### [240] [Where You Measure Decides What You Measure: Position Selection in Ablation-Based SAE Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13337)
*Valentin Noël*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出稀疏自编码器（SAE）的因果消融评估存在一个未被注意的方法论缺陷：评估"潜变量重要性"时默认在潜变量激活最强的token处测量效果，但该token的选择由字典（dictionary）决定而非实验者，导致不同字典的评估实际上在不同位置进行，从而使比较结果失真。作者通过训练六个仅拟合选择不同的自编码器证明，这种"字典分歧"大部分其实是位置差异，可通过一行代码修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器用于解释语言模型内部计算，验证潜变量重要性（causal importance）的标准做法是消融该潜变量并观察效果变化。但潜变量在多个token上激活，效果只能在某个token处测量。惯例在激活最强的token处测量，而这一选择由被评估的字典决定，不同字典会把测量点移动到不同token。作者发现这一细节导致跨字典比较严重失真，需要系统性研究并修正。

Method: 1) 使用Google发布的两个同模型SAE，按解码器相似度匹配潜变量，分析匹配对中测量位置的重合率；2) 从同一初始化训练六个仅拟合选择不同的SAE，确保潜变量语义一致，分离"位置效应"与"字典真实分歧"；3) 在16倍范围的数据集规模下研究位置分歧随规模的变化；4) 提出一行代码的协议修正（统一测量位置），并据此审计五篇已发表论文。

Result: 两个Google字典即使编码几乎相同的潜变量对，在大部分（很大比例）中对同一潜变量选择了不同的测量token。在六个仅拟合不同的SAE比较中，原本被解读为"字典分歧"的方差中有7.6%-11.9%来自测量位置的不同，统一测量位置后方差降至接近零。增加评估数据不能修复该问题：在16倍语料规模范围内，字典对测量位置的一致性反而下降，问题随规模增大。

Conclusion: 因果数字若不报告其测量位置，描述的不只是潜变量本身，还包括其被测量的token。作者提供统一的评估协议（一行代码）使跨论文因果数字可比较，并审计五篇已发表论文，说明该问题普遍存在且需被修正。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are meant to name the things a language model computes, and the usual way to check that a latent matters is to switch it off and see what changes. But a latent fires at many tokens, and the effect has to be measured at one of them. The convention is to measure where the latent fires hardest. That choice is almost never reported, and it is not made by the experimenter: it is made by the dictionary under evaluation. Change the dictionary and the measurement moves to a different token. We show this is not a detail. Take two sparse autoencoders released by Google for the same model and match their latents by decoder similarity: even among the pairs the two dictionaries encode almost identically, they pick different tokens for a large share of them. Two dictionaries compared under the usual protocol are therefore very often compared at different places. To separate the convention from the dictionaries we train six autoencoders from one initialisation, differing only in fitting choices, so that a latent means the same thing in each. Most of the variance such a comparison reads as "these dictionaries disagree about this latent" turns out to be the position instead: it falls from 7.6% and 11.9% of variance to near zero once every dictionary is measured at the same token. More evaluation data does not rescue it. Across a sixteenfold range of corpus sizes the dictionaries agree less about where to measure, not more, so the problem grows with scale. The correction is one line of evaluation code. We give the protocol an ablation-based causal number must report to be comparable across papers, and an audit of five published papers against it. In short: a causal number reported without its position describes the token it was taken at as much as the latent it was taken from.

</details>


### [241] [Simulation-to-real transfer learning for infrared spectroscopic chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13341)
*Yusen Tan,Yixuan Chen,Zheng Fang,Pan Liu,Yifan Li,Qinyu Guo,Zhedong Lin,Yuqiang Li,Xiangxiang Zeng,Tong Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了UltraIR，一个拥有超过1亿参数的红外光谱基础模型，通过约6000万模拟红外光谱的预训练（包括光谱重建、分子指纹相似性对齐和官能团预测）实现模拟到真实的迁移学习，在官能团预测、分子结构解析、理化性质预测、混合物鉴定等多个化学传感任务中超越传统机器学习和任务特定深度学习方法，并展现出强大的小样本和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外光谱广泛用于化学传感，但传统光谱解释依赖人工经验和参考光谱，难以规模化；现有机器学习方法仅针对单一任务或数据集设计，需要大量标注训练数据，跨分析目标和实验数据集的迁移能力差。

Method: 构建UltraIR基础模型（超1亿参数），在约6000万模拟红外光谱上采用三种预训练策略：光谱重建、分子指纹相似性对齐和官能团预测；随后针对下游任务使用任务特定标签或目标进行微调适配。

Result: 在官能团预测、分子结构解析、理化性质预测、混合物组分识别与定量、细菌分类、药用植物地理溯源与成分定量、微塑料分类和土壤性质预测等众多任务中，UltraIR均优于传统机器学习和任务特定深度学习方法；在有限标注实验光谱和跨FTIR光谱仪及实验室的零样本推断中表现优异。

Conclusion: UltraIR为红外光谱化学传感提供了一条适应性、数据高效的路径，能够应用于复杂真实世界样品，实现从分子到复杂样品的跨任务泛化。

Abstract: Infrared (IR) spectroscopy is widely used for chemical sensing, but extracting reliable chemical information from spectra remains challenging. Conventional interpretation is labor-intensive, relies on prior knowledge and reference spectra, and is difficult to scale, whereas most machine-learning methods are tailored to individual tasks or datasets, require large labeled training sets, and transfer poorly across analytical objectives and experimental datasets. Here we introduce UltraIR, a foundation model for IR spectroscopy with more than 100 million parameters that enables simulation-to-real transfer learning for chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples. UltraIR is pretrained on approximately 60 million simulated IR spectra using spectral reconstruction, molecular fingerprint similarity alignment, and functional-group prediction, then adapted to downstream objectives with task-specific labels or targets. Across functional-group prediction, molecular structure elucidation, physicochemical property prediction, mixture-component identification and quantification, bacterial classification, medicinal-herb geographic origin traceability and constituent quantification, microplastics classification, and soil property prediction, UltraIR outperforms conventional machine-learning and task-specific deep-learning baselines. It performs strongly with limited labeled experimental spectra and in zero-shot inference for the same analytical task across Fourier-transform infrared spectrometers and laboratories, providing a route to adaptable, data-efficient chemical sensing from complex real-world samples.

</details>


### [242] [When Local Variance Optimality Is Not Enough: RoPE-Aligned Q/K Rotations for Dynamic 4-Bit Quantisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13365)
*Shuhan Wang,Yilin Luo,Nan Xu,Chi Wang Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转后训练量化（rotation-based post-training quantization）中基于RoPE的成对正交变换与全头混合（full-head Hadamard）的对比。作者证明了在频率不同的前提下，没有其他单头正交映射能与RoPE交换（交换性完备性），并推导了共享成对旋转的最优角度。实验表明，成对变换在动态W4A4KV4设定下不优于全头混合，但其与Hadamard组合可满足PPL区间标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE将注意力头分解为二维频率对，此前已有工作建立了与RoPE可交换的逐对旋转。一个悬而未决的问题是：是否还有其他正交变换也能与RoPE交换（即交换性是否完备），以及这种尊重RoPE分解结构的变换能否在量化误差上优于全头混合变换。

Method: 1) 理论上证明：对于不同频率，不存在其他单头正交映射与RoPE交换（交换性反定理）；2) 在共享成对参数化下，推导使较大通道方差最小化的旋转角度（基于池化协方差、位置平均的替代目标），并验证实现达到了解析最小值；3) 在动态W4A4KV4设定下对四个检查点进行实验评估，比较成对变换、全头Hadamard以及两者的组合。

Result: 共享成对配置在动态W4A4KV4下未能提高准确率；在短程和长程上下文下均增加困惑度。成对旋转与Hadamard组合在默认估计器下满足±0.05-PPL区间标准。仅从K估计共享角度改善成对-only结果，但仍未达到全头混合水平。可控插值实验表明，随混合支持从双通道增加到全头，K范围、相对量化误差和困惑度退化均降低。

Conclusion: 结构化替代目标的最优性并不必然降低量化误差——当替代目标（位置平均的池化协方差二阶矩）与量化器的尺度设定统计量（逐token组范围）不一致、且混合支持（双通道）不足时，最优化替代目标可能反而有害。全头混合支持对减少量化误差至关重要。

Abstract: Rotation-based post-training quantisation commonly applies an orthogonal transform across an entire attention head to reduce outlier-induced error. RoPE instead partitions each head into two-dimensional frequency pairs, raising the question of whether a transform respecting this decomposition can improve on full-head mixing. Prior work has established the per-pair rotations that commute with RoPE. We state the converse result that, for distinct frequencies, no other single-head orthogonal map commutes with RoPE. For the head-shared parameterisation used in our experiments, we then derive the rotation angle that minimises the larger channel variance under a pooled-covariance, position-averaged surrogate and verify that the implementation attains its analytic minimum. The evaluated head-shared pairwise configuration does not improve accuracy in the tested dynamic W4A4KV4 setting. Across four checkpoints, replacing the full-head Hadamard with this configuration increases perplexity at both short and long context lengths. Composing the pairwise rotation with the Hadamard satisfies the selected $\pm0.05$-PPL interval criterion under the default estimator. Estimating the shared angle from K alone improves pairwise-only on every checkpoint but does not close its gap to full-head mixing. The analytic objective controls a position-averaged second moment of a pooled calibration covariance, whereas the dynamic quantiser sets its step from a tokenwise group range. The pairwise transform also has only two-channel mixing support. Along a controlled interpolation from two-channel to full-head mixing, K range, relative quantisation error, and perplexity degradation decrease as support increases. These results show that optimality for a structured surrogate need not reduce quantisation error when the surrogate and mixing support are misaligned with the quantiser's scale-setting statistic.

</details>


### [243] [Reduced Matrix Multiplication: Input-Adaptive Matrix-Product Reduction for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13426)
*Zixuan Lan,Yanhong Li,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的输入自适应推理方法RMM（Reduced Matrix Multiplication），通过在收缩维度上选择信息丰富的切片来减少Transformer矩阵乘法计算量，在不修改模型权重的前提下实现可预测的精度-效率权衡，并在1B到70B参数的语言模型及多模态模型中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型虽然性能强大，但由于其推理过程中频繁的高维矩阵乘法计算，导致推理成本高昂。现有加速方法往往需要重新训练或修改模型结构，缺乏一种训练免费、输入自适应的推理优化手段。

Method: RMM是一种无需训练、输入自适应的推理方法。其核心思想是沿着矩阵乘法的收缩维度选择信息性切片（informative slices），从而减少矩阵乘积的计算量。该方法不修改模型权重，仅通过一个简单的保留率（retention-ratio）控制参数来调节计算量，实现平滑且可预测的精度-效率权衡。

Result: 在1B到70B参数的语言模型上进行验证，发现缩减容限取决于模型家族、任务、组件和保留率，且通常随模型规模增大而改善。在中等缩减下，RMM在判别式任务、自回归生成和长上下文场景中均保持稳健。该原理同样适用于多模态视觉-语言推理。机制性消融实验揭示了Transformer内部的结构不对称性：注意力侧计算比MLP组件具有更高的可缩减性。在NVIDIA A100上使用自定义内核进行端到端基准测试表明，计算量的节省可以转化为实际的运行时加速，尤其在较长序列长度下更显著。

Conclusion: RMM为输入自适应推理期优化提供了可扩展的方向，能够在无需训练的情况下实现Transformer推理的加速，同时保持模型的鲁棒性与准确性，并具有跨模态的泛化能力。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models achieve strong performance but incur substantial inference cost due to repeated high-dimensional matrix multiplications. We propose Reduced Matrix Multiplication (RMM), a training-free, input-adaptive inference method that reduces Transformer matrix products by selecting informative slices along their contraction dimensions, without modifying model weights. Under a simple retention-ratio control, RMM provides a smooth and predictable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Across language models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, we find that reduction tolerance depends on the model family, task, component, and retention ratio, although it often improves with model scale. Under moderate reduction, RMM remains robust across the evaluated discriminative, autoregressive generation, and long-context settings. We further show that the same principle extends to multimodal vision-language inference. Mechanistic ablations reveal a structural asymmetry within Transformers: attention-side computations are substantially more reducible than MLP components. Finally, wall-clock benchmarks with custom kernels on an NVIDIA A100 show that these computational savings can translate into practical runtime gains, especially at longer sequence lengths. Together, these results position RMM as a scalable direction for input-adaptive inference-time optimization.

</details>


### [244] [Symmetry-Breaking De Novo Crystal Generation via Markovian Jump Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13457)
*Van Khoa Nguyen,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对称性破缺物理启发的扩散生成框架（SbCD），通过马尔可夫跳跃-扩散过程从最低对称性先验反向生成完整晶体结构规格，显式建模不同空间群之间的跃迁，在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上大幅优于对称性保持的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成模型只能生成到位点对称性级别，且依赖从经验分布中采样空间群，无法捕获全局对称性和结构依赖关系，限制了生成晶体的完整性和物理合理性。

Method: 受物理学中自发对称性破缺启发，采用马尔可夫跳跃-扩散过程建模对称性破缺动力学，从最低对称性先验开始反向生成完整结构规格，显式地整合空间群间跃迁到生成过程中。

Result: 在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上的从头生成实验中，SbCD相比对称性保持的对应模型取得了显著更优的性能。

Conclusion: SbCD提供了一种物理动机明确、原则性的晶体材料生成建模新视角，证明显式建模空间群跃迁是提升晶体生成质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Generating crystals has recently attracted significant interest due to their broad applications in materials science. However, existing generative models struggle to produce complete crystallographic specifications, limiting their ability to capture global symmetry and structural dependencies. In particular, current state-of-the-art approaches generate crystals only up to site symmetries and rely on sampling space groups from empirical distributions during generation. Inspired by \emph{spontaneous symmetry breaking} in physics, where crystals break symmetries under external conditions, we propose a novel diffusion-based framework that generates full structure specifications by reversing from the lowest-symmetry priors. Our method leverages a Markovian jump-diffusion process to model these symmetry-breaking dynamics, enabling it to traverse different space groups in a physically motivated manner. Our model, dubbed \emph{Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion} (SbCD), introduces a principled approach to explicitly incorporate inter-space-group transitions into the generative process. In de novo generation experiments on MP20 and MPTS-52, SbCD outperforms its symmetry-preserving counterpart by a substantial margin, offering a promising perspective for generative modeling of crystalline materials.

</details>


### [245] [Doubly Robust Estimation of Causal Effect on CVR with Targeted Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13461)
*Jiayi Dan,Bo Li,Lu Deng,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从半参数理论的角度重新审视点击后转化率(CVR)的因果效应估计问题，提出了一个针对链式结构结果的新的双重稳健因果效应估计器，并通过目标正则化框架提升数值稳定性和实际适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR是电商和广告等领域的关键指标，但直接应用现有因果推断方法到点击样本会引入样本选择偏差和方差增大问题。已有研究提出的"理想损失"方法虽能实现损失的无偏估计，但无法保证最终估计器的无偏性。因此需要开发一个具有坚实理论保证的新估计器。

Method: 从半参数理论出发，针对链式结构结果（如CVR）开发了一个新的双重稳健因果效应估计器，详细推导了其理论性质（包括更快的收敛速度和对灵活非参数估计器的鲁棒性）。基于理论发现，设计了基于目标正则化（targeted regularization）的框架来提升数值稳定性和实际可用性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的大量实验证明了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。此外，发现将损失去偏与标准因果估计器简单结合的效果不如所提方法，这强调了针对CVR目标开发具有坚实理论保证的新估计器的必要性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于半参数理论的双重稳健估计器能够有效解决CVR因果效应估计中的样本选择偏差问题，其理论性质保证了在实际应用中的稳健性，优于简单的损失去偏与标准因果估计器的组合方案。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (CVR) is a key metric in various scenarios including e-commerce and advertising, reflecting the efficiency and user experience in the second stage of the conversion process. Estimating the causal effect on CVR is therefore of great practical importance. However, directly applying existing causal inference methods to clicked samples introduces sample selection bias and increased variance due to the exclusion of non-click data. Recent studies on CVR prediction introduce "ideal loss", which optimizes model parameters using an unbiased estimate of the loss over the full sample. Nevertheless, there is no guarantee that unbiasedness of the loss implies unbiasedness of the final estimator.
  We revisit this challenge from the perspective of semiparametric theory. Specifically, we develop a new doubly robust causal effect estimator for chain-structured outcomes such as CVR, and derive its theoretical properties in detail. It achieves a faster convergence rate compared to nuisance parameters estimation and is therefore more robust when using flexible nonparametric estimators, including neural networks. Based on these theoretical findings, we further design a framework based on targeted regularization to improve numerical stability and practical applicability.
  Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our method. In addition, we find that naively combining loss debiasing with standard causal estimators underperforms our method, highlighting the necessity of developing the new estimator tailored to this CVR-style objective with solid theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [246] [Active-Trace Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13467)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究MYULA算法在非光滑复合目标采样上的收敛性，提出了以参考活跃迹B_ref而非全局曲率界d/λ来控制主导离散化误差的新分析框架。对于分段线性、LASSO、group和全变差等结构化惩罚，B_ref可与λ无关，从而获得~O(ε^{-2})的精度依赖，优于通用~O(ε^{-3})结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非光滑复合分布的采样在统计和机器学习中广泛存在，MYULA算法虽然已有收敛性保证，但现有分析依赖全局曲率界d/λ，导致精度依赖为~O(ε^{-3})。实际中许多结构化惩罚（如LASSO、TV）具有特殊的几何结构，全局的曲率上界过于保守，无法反映真实的局部行为，因此需要更精细的分析来揭示MYULA在结构化问题上的真实性能。

Method: 作者引入参考活跃迹B_λ（即从π_λ出发的一次MYULA热子步上a_λ的平均值）来替代全局曲率界d/λ，控制主导的MYULA离散化误差。利用Moreau包络g_λ的弱/逐点Hessian的迹结构，结合"曲率-管"估计（curvature-tube estimates），对分段线性、lasso型、group和TV惩罚证明了B_ref与λ无关的性质，从而得到改进的迭代复杂度。同时证明Moreau偏差界√m W2(π_λ,π)≤G²λ/4，通过选择λ≍ε/G²获得端到端保证。

Result: 得到迭代上界：N ≲ (1/m)[L_f + (τ_f+G²+B_ref)/ε²_alg + M_λ/ε_alg]（忽略对数因子），保证√m W2(μ_N, π_λ)≤ε_alg。通用估计B_ref≤d/λ给出~O(ε^{-3})精度依赖；对于结构化惩罚，曲率-管估计使B_ref与λ无关，达到~O(ε^{-2})的精度依赖，且使用经典的MYULA核，无需修改算法。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了MYULA的收敛行为由局部的参考活跃迹而非全局曲率界控制，填补了理论与实际表现的差距。对结构化非光滑惩罚，经典的MYULA算法即可达到~O(ε^{-2})的精度，与光滑情形相当，为大规模贝叶斯采样提供理论保障。

Abstract: We study the Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for the nonsmooth composite target \[ π(dx)\propto \exp\{-f(x)-g(x)\}\,dx, \qquad x\in\mathbb R^d, \] where \(f\) is \(m\)-strongly convex with \(L_f\)-Lipschitz gradient and \(g\) is convex and \(G\)-Lipschitz. Let \(g_λ\) be the Moreau envelope of \(g\), \(π_λ\) the corresponding smoothed target, and \(a_λ=\operatorname{tr}H_λ\), where \(H_λ\) is the a.e./weak Hessian of \(g_λ\). We show that the leading MYULA discretization error is controlled by the reference active trace \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\), the average of \(a_λ\) along the heat substep of one MYULA update started from \(π_λ\), rather than by the global curvature bound \(d/λ\). If \(M_λ\) is an a.e. upper bound for \(a_λ\), then, up to logarithmic factors, \[ N \lesssim \frac{1}{m} \left[ L_f + \frac{ τ_f+G^2+B_{\mathrm{ref}} }{ \varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}^2 } + \frac{M_λ}{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}} \right], \qquad τ_f:= \sup_x\operatorname{tr}\nabla^2 f(x), \] iterations suffice to ensure \(\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π_λ)\leq\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}\), where \(μ_N\) is the law of the \(N\)-th iterate and \(W_2\) is the quadratic Wasserstein distance. We also prove the Moreau-bias bound \[ \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,π) \leq \frac{G^2λ}{4}. \] Thus, choosing \(λ\asymp\varepsilon/G^2\) gives an end-to-end guarantee for \(π\). The universal estimate \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\leq d/λ\) yields \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})\) accuracy dependence. For the structured piecewise-linear, lasso-type, group, and total-variation penalties considered here, curvature--tube estimates make \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\) independent of \(λ\), yielding \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})\) for the same classical MYULA kernel.

</details>


### [247] [Synthetic Persona Pretraining: Alignment from Token Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13482)
*Julian Minder,Viktor Moskvoretskii,Raghav Singhal,Difan Jiao,Andy Arditi,Shaobo Cui,Yiderigun Borjigin,Kartik Bali,Stefan Krsteski,Harsh Raj,Huu Nguyen,Jannik Brinkmann,Ashton Anderson,Roland Aydin,Robert West*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出合成人格预训练（SPP）方法，从预训练的第一个token起就将目标助手人格嵌入语言模型。通过用价值一致的一阶反思注释预训练文档，在预训练中直接优化交叉熵损失安装人格，再用对话数据绑定助手身份。实验表明SPP在3B参数、500B token规模下提升了宪法遵循和越狱鲁棒性，降低了OOD道德困境中的失调率，且不损害能力。早期干预至关重要，干预越早效果越好且随预训练预算增加而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型的对齐（alignment）和助手身份通常只在预训练之后引入，这使得价值观成为一种"薄覆盖层"而非深层根基，容易导致后续的错位（misalignment）。作者旨在探索一种不同的范式：在预训练阶段就从token零开始安装期望的助手人格，使价值观真正根植于模型中。

Method: SPP分为三步：(1) 用源于规范性价值宪章的价值对齐一阶反思（first-person reflections）注释预训练文档；(2) 使用标准交叉熵损失在标准预训练文档及其反思上预训练，在众多人格中安装期望人格；(3) 在用户-助手对话数据上进行后训练，将期望人格绑定到助手身份上，称为"人格绑定"（persona binding）。

Result: 在3B参数、500B token的预训练规模下，SPP提升了宪法遵循和越狱鲁棒性，降低了OOD道德困境中的失调率，同时保持了模型能力。对比实验表明：仅在预训练结束时引入SPP（而非token零引入）会产生较弱的宪法遵循、不改变价值优先级、在困境中产生更多失调选择。该优势依赖于人格绑定，并随预训练预算增加而增强。

Conclusion: 早期塑造价值观对对齐至关重要。预训练期的人格干预是一种有效的对齐方法，为未来的安全对齐研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: As language-model-based AI is increasingly deployed in autonomous settings, aligning its goals and values with those of humans becomes critical. Today, alignment, and the assistant identity itself, are typically introduced only after pretraining, once behavioral priors are already established. This can make values a thin overlay, rather than deeply rooted, and facilitate subsequent misalignment. Pursuing a different paradigm, we introduce Synthetic Persona Pretraining (SPP), which installs the desired assistant persona from token zero in pretraining. First, we annotate pretraining documents with value-aligned first-person reflections derived from a normative value constitution. Second, we pretrain via the standard cross-entropy loss on standard pretraining documents as well as their reflections, which installs the desired persona among a multitude of other personas. Finally, we post-train on user-assistant dialogue data, which binds this desired persona to the assistant identity, a process we call persona binding. By pretraining models up to 3B parameters on 500B tokens, we show that SPP improves constitution following and jailbreak robustness, and reduces the misalignment rate in out-of-distribution moral dilemmas, while preserving capabilities. Early intervention matters: compared with alignment from token zero, introducing SPP only at the end of pretraining yields weaker constitution adherence, does not shift value priorities, and leads to less aligned choices in dilemmas. This advantage depends on persona binding and, importantly, increases with pretraining budget. Overall, our results show that shaping values early is critical for alignment and establish pretraining-time persona interventions as an effective approach to do so.

</details>


### [248] [Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique: A Spectral Framework for Equation Discovery, Approximation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13504)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏正交回归技术（SORT），一种从含噪和不规则采样数据中学习正交基展开的稀疏谱框架。SORT通过L1正则化回归直接从观测中估计展开系数，避免显式求积或解析内积计算，核心应用是数据驱动的常微分方程（ODE）发现。实验表明，SORT在基函数适配问题时匹配或优于基于库的稀疏回归基线，且在稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示失配下表现出更稳定的退化。该方法还将问题从通用项之间的脆弱选择转移到面向问题域的基设计上，并支持非线性逼近和高维积分估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的符号回归、基于语法的方法和SINDy类稀疏识别方法在处理动力学系统识别时，依赖有限的候选函数库，若库中缺少问题特定的非线性项则模型可能失败。作者希望提出一种替代路径：先恢复一个紧凑的谱表示，再以此为中间表示引导更简单的解析形式的搜索，从而缓解对函数库选择的脆弱依赖。

Method: SORT的核心方法是用L1正则化回归直接从含噪、不规则采样的观测数据中估计正交基的展开系数。与需要显式求积或解析计算内积的传统谱方法不同，SORT将系数估计建模为稀疏回归问题，避免了数值积分误差的累积。在ODE发现任务中，向量场被表示在选定的正交基中，并通过稀疏系数展开进行学习，从而获得一个紧凑的谱表示。

Result: 在动力学系统实验中，当基函数适配问题时，SORT匹配或优于基于库的稀疏回归基线（如SINDy风格方法）；在稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示失配条件下，SORT的退化更加稳定。实验还表明，主导低阶系数随模型阶数增加而持续存在，支持阶数一致的模型增长。此外，学习到的展开还可用于非线性逼近和高维积分估计。

Conclusion: SORT提供了一种可复用的中间表示，用于系统辨识、逼近和积分任务，同时将基设计明确作为科学建模问题的组成部分。它不是替代符号回归或SINDy，而是通过先恢复紧凑谱表示来补充这些方法，从而将问题从对通用函数库的脆弱选择转移到面向问题域的基设计上。

Abstract: We develop the Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique (SORT), a sparse spectral framework for learning orthonormal-basis expansions from noisy and irregularly sampled data. SORT estimates expansion coefficients directly from observations using L1-regularized regression, avoiding explicit quadrature or analytic inner-product evaluation. The central application is data-driven discovery of ordinary differential equations: vector fields are represented in chosen orthogonal bases and learned as sparse coefficient expansions. This provides a complementary route to symbolic regression, grammar-based discovery, and SINDy-style sparse identification by first recovering a compact spectral representation, which can later guide searches for simpler analytic forms. Across the dynamical-system experiments, SORT matches or improves upon library-based sparse-regression baselines when the basis is well adapted to the problem, and shows more stable degradation under sparse sampling, noisy derivative estimates, and representation mismatch. Specific examples illustrate why this representation is useful: if a finite library misses the problem-specific nonlinearity, the resulting model can fail. SORT is not immune to mismatch, but it shifts the problem away from brittle selection among generic terms to basis design adapted to the problem domain. The experiments also show that dominant low-order coefficients persist as model order increases, supporting order-consistent model growth. Beyond equation discovery, the same learned expansion supports nonlinear approximation and estimation of complex, high-dimensional integrals by coefficient readout. Overall, SORT provides a reusable intermediate representation for system identification, approximation, and integration, while making basis design an explicit part of the scientific modeling problem.

</details>


### [249] [Intern-S2-Preview: Scientific Agentic Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13505)
*Lei Bai,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Guanzhou Chen,Kai Chen,Guangran Cheng,Erfei Cui,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Shangheng Du,Yanhui Duan,Yue Fan,Youqing Fang,Quan Gan,Yuanyuan Gao,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yuzhe Gu,Qipeng Guo,Junjun He,Xin Hong,Ming Hu,Zhouqi Hua,Haian Huang,Junhao Huang,Zixian Huang,Minxi Jin,Lingkai Kong,Alexander Lam,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tianhao Liang,Dahua Lin,Junyao Lin,Tianyang Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Jiangning Liu,Jin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenran Liu,Yifei Liu,Yuhong Liu,Yuhong Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Ziyan Liu,Ziyu Liu,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Chengqi Lyu,Le Ma,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Haoyang Peng,Runyu Peng,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Kou Shi,Xiang Shi,Qisheng Su,Xuerui Su,Hao Sun,Xiao Sun,Yanan Sun,Yu Sun,Huanze Tang,Yinghao Tang,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Bingli Wang,Haomin Wang,Jiarui Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Rui Wang,Xiquan Wang,Yi Wang,Zhecan Wang,Ziyi Wang,Zun Wang,Rubin Wei,Lianyi Wu,Wen Wu,Yue Wu,Yuhan Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Zijian Wu,Shuhao Xing,Jun Xu,Xingle Xu,Xuenan Xu,Xiangchao Yan,Ziang Yan,Bowen Yang,Danni Yang,Lin Yang,Zhiqi Yang,Qian Yao,Haochen Ye,Peng Ye,Jinhui Yin,Jiashuo Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Fei Yuan,Yuhang Zang,Bo Zhang,Chao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Hongjie Zhang,Junming Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Zhuo Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Zhonghan Zhao,Zhihang Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Xin Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Yicheng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Intern-S2-Preview系列科学智能体基础模型，通过多模态预训练和统一的后期训练管线（包括监督微调、多任务强化学习、黑盒/白盒智能体RL和蒸馏）来支持科学多模态理解、推理、生成和长时程任务。397B参数的模型在多个基准上取得领先结果，时间序列模块提升了科学信号理解和预测能力，而独立的记忆增强路径MemDec在无需修改冻结主干的条件下提升了生物学指令任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现越来越需要AI系统具备在异构模态科学证据上进行推理、与科学工具和环境交互、并在长时间任务范围内持续进展的能力。现有模型在这类科学智能体场景中仍面临挑战。

Method: 训练流程包括两个阶段：(1) 科学多模态预训练，使用渲染的科技文档、交错的图文数据和多样化的科学语料；(2) 统一的后期训练管线，由监督微调、可扩展多任务强化学习、黑盒和白盒智能体RL以及on-policy蒸馏组成。关键技术支持包括部分回放与off-policy校正、自适应长度正则化、在线投机解码、鲁棒多任务优化和智能体任务的trace感知经验组装。架构上，397B模型扩展了时间序列建模以支持长序列理解和数值预测；Memory Decoder作为独立的记忆增强路径，可在不修改冻结的397B主干的情况下实现快速科学专业化。

Result: 在科学、多模态、智能体及通用基准上的评估显示，Intern-S2-Preview-397B在多种设置下取得了具有竞争力或领先的结果。时间序列模块在SciTS上提升了科学信号理解和预测能力；独立的Intern-MemDec-4B扩展将Biology-Instructions平均得分从56.92提升至60.32，且无需修改冻结的397B主干。

Conclusion: Intern-S2-Preview系列展示了通过精心设计的预训练和后期训练管线构建科学智能体基础模型的可行性，同时在架构层面通过时间序列建模和记忆增强路径拓展了模型在科学领域的能力边界，为科学AI研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires AI systems that can reason over scientific evidence of heterogeneous modalities, interact with scientific tools and environments, and sustain progress across long task horizons. We present Intern-S2-Preview, a series of scientific agentic foundation models designed to support multimodal scientific understanding, reasoning, generation, and long-horizon tasks. The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. Evaluations across scientific, multimodal, agentic, and general-purpose benchmarks show that Intern-S2-Preview-397B achieves competitive or leading results in multiple settings. The time series modules improve scientific signal understanding and forecasting on SciTS, while the separate Intern-MemDec-4B extension improves the Biology-Instructions average score from 56.92 to 60.32 without modifying the frozen 397B backbone.

</details>


### [250] [Intervention-Aware Clinical World Model for Post-Op Outcome Forecasting in Cardiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13518)
*Yunsung Chung,Yingshuo Liu,Abboud F. Hassan,Han Feng,Mary M. Maleckar,Nassir Marrouche,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种干预感知的临床世界模型，通过结构化潜在状态和时序事件更新来建模术后不规则恢复轨迹，用于房颤消融后的复发风险预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床预测模型将术后结局视为从基线测量到未来终点的单步映射，未能捕捉术后恢复过程中异步记录的事件（如临床观察、用药变化、重复干预等）对风险评估的时序影响。

Method: 模型首先将基线影像编码为3D空间潜在状态，随后利用手术过程信息、静态协变量、经过时间及围事件生理嵌入来逐步更新该状态；随访影像仅通过潜在预测目标提供训练阶段监督。

Result: 在DECAAF-II数据集上进行重复内部交叉验证，复发预测AUROC为0.756、AUPRC为0.777；瘢痕范围MAE为2.971个百分点，且推理时无需随访MRI强度。该学习状态支持不同时间跨度的复发风险查询及对空白期记录的回溯编辑。

Conclusion: 术后90天恢复期内不规则记录能为长期复发风险提供具临床意义的证据，所提出的世界模型方法在房颤消融复发预测上表现良好，并具备灵活的推理能力。

Abstract: Many clinical prediction models treat post-intervention outcomes as a one-step mapping from baseline measurements to a future endpoint. However, recovery after a procedure often unfolds as an irregular trajectory: clinical observations, medication changes, repeat interventions, and physiological measurements are recorded asynchronously and can change risk assessment over time. We propose an intervention-aware clinical world model that represents each patient with a structured latent state and evolves it through time-ordered post-intervention events. The model first encodes baseline imaging into a 3D spatial latent state. It then updates this state using procedural context, static covariates, elapsed time, and peri-event physiological embeddings. Follow-up imaging provides training-only supervision through a latent forecasting objective. We apply the framework to atrial fibrillation ablation. During the 90-day recovery window, irregular post-procedure records provide clinically meaningful evidence for long-term recurrence risk. In repeated internal cross-validation on DECAAF-II, our model achieves AUROC 0.756 and AUPRC 0.777 for recurrence prediction. It also achieves a scar-extent MAE of 2.971 percentage points without requiring follow-up MRI intensities at inference. The learned state supports recurrence-risk queries at different horizons and retrospective input editing of blanking-period records.

</details>


### [251] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对离散采样的掩码扩散(masking diffusion)问题，引入了名为"去掩码增长复杂度"(Unmasking Growth Complexity, UGC)的路径解析式数据几何度量。UGC的局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差，从而统一了伯努利子集和固定基数两种去掩码方案的分析。基于log-reveal-odds坐标，作者设计了优化的单块和多块调度策略，并证明了UGC增量可从样本中通过耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量估计得到，最终生成了在常数因子内达到oracle性能的"认证最优"采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型(discrete diffusion)在生成建模中应用广泛，但其离散采样(discrete sampling)的调度策略(schedule)设计缺乏统一的理论框架。现有方法主要依赖启发式或经验性的调度选择，无法量化数据几何结构对离散化误差的影响，也难以保证采样质量。作者希望通过建立一种路径解析式的复杂度度量，实现调度的理论优化并保证采样器的性能上界。

Method: 1) 引入UGC度量：定义去掩码过程中的路径解析式复杂度，其局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差。2) 统一分析：同时覆盖Bernoulli-subset与fixed-cardinality两种去掩码方案。3) 坐标变换：在log-reveal-odds坐标系下推导单块与多块的最优调度。4) 估计方法：通过耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量估计UGC增量，实现数据驱动的最优调度。5) 理论分析：建立certified-optimal采样器，实现目标KL误差的高概率保证及常数因子迭代复杂度。6) 极限分析：在细分区极取下，UGC密度的平方根积分决定欧拉离散化误差的尖锐首阶最优值。

Result: 1) 建立了UGC与KL离散化误差之间的直接控制关系，统一了两种去掩码方案的分析。2) 推导出最优单块和多块调度策略，量化了根据数据几何自适应分配计算资源的收益。3) 证明了UGC增量可从样本中估计，生成certified-optimal采样器，其迭代复杂度在常数因子内逼近oracle过程。4) 展示UGC与经典多元依赖度量和既有离散扩散复杂度分析的联系。5) 在细分区极限下得到欧拉离散化误差的尖锐首阶最优表达式。6) 实验/理论示例显示相对于粗糙调度可获得维度相关的显著改进，其中包括使用常数个自适应放置块实现约Ω̃(√d)的改进。

Conclusion: UGC为离散扩散采样中的调度设计提供了统一、可估计且理论最优的框架。该工作不仅深化了对离散扩散过程中数据几何与采样复杂度关系的理解，还给出了可实际部署的认证最优采样算法，并在理论上明确了调度适应性在维度上的收益边界。细分区极限分析进一步将UGC与扩散过程的连续极限行为联系起来，为后续研究提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [252] [Vero: Can AI Agents Build Formally Verified Software Repositories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13522)
*Zhe Ye,Hantao Lou,Yuechun Sun,Peiyang Song,Zhengxu Yan,Timothe Kasriel,Qingyang Zhang,Kaiyu Yang,Soonho Kong,Jingxuan He,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Vero是首个在仓库级别评估联合实现与证明合成的基准，包含43个源自真实项目的多模块Lean 4实例。最强智能体仅能完全解决27/43个实例，当前智能体在仓库规模的形式化验证软件合成上仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在编程中越来越常用，但生成的代码缺乏正确性保证。现有的验证代码生成基准要么只关注单个函数，要么只评估在给定实现基础上的证明生成，无法回答智能体能否在真实多模块代码库中做出连贯的实现与证明选择这一开放问题。

Method: Vero从Python、Dafny、Verus和Coq的真实仓库中提取43个多模块实例，每个实例包含多模块Lean 4仓库、预定的API接口、人工整理的形式化规范和参考实现。支持纯证明与代码加证明两种评估模式，并设计了审计机制允许智能体正式证明规范不可满足或参考代码错误，以发现和修正标注阶段的潜在错误。

Result: 在配备Lean工具链的最新前沿编码智能体配置上，最强的智能体仅完全解决了43个实例中的27个，且在最具挑战性的仓库上未能关闭任何规范。这表明当前智能体在仓库级验证软件合成上仍有明显差距。

Conclusion: Vero为衡量仓库规模验证软件合成的进展提供了具体测试平台，揭示了当前AI智能体在端到端形式化验证代码生成方面的显著不足，并公开了基准、标签流程和评估工具以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for programming, but do not provide any guarantee on the correctness of generated code. Verified code generation, in which an agent produces both an implementation and a machine-checked proof of its specification, offers a stronger path toward trustworthy AI-generated software. Existing benchmarks in this direction either focus on individual functions or only evaluate proof generation with provided implementations. It is still an open question whether agents can make coherent implementation and proof choices across real multi-module codebases. To bridge this gap, we introduce Vero, the first benchmark to evaluate joint implementation and proof synthesis at the repository level. Vero contains 43 multi-module instances sourced from real-world repositories spanning Python, Dafny, Verus, and Coq, and covering diverse domains from cryptographic protocols to distributed systems. Each instance consists of a multi-module Lean 4 repository with predetermined API interfaces, manually curated formal specifications, and reference implementations, supporting both proof-only and code-and-proof evaluation modes. To improve benchmark reliability, Vero also includes an audit mechanism where agents are allowed to formally prove unsatisfiability of provided specification or incorrectness of reference code, which surfaces and corrects latent code and specification errors during curation. We evaluate frontier coding-agent configurations with Lean toolchain access. The strongest agent fully solves only 27 of 43 instances and closes no specifications on the hardest repositories. Vero provides a concrete testbed for measuring progress toward repository-scale verified software synthesis, where current agents still fall short. We release the benchmark, curation pipeline, and evaluation harness at https://github.com/sunblaze-ucb/vero.

</details>


### [253] [DARTree: Speculative Diffusion Decoding with Autoregressive Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13524)
*Tianyi Li,Yaxin Luo,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARTree是一种无需训练的投机解码方法，将预训练的AR修正头从链推广到树结构，通过批量扩展和评分构建固定宽度候选树，再用最佳优先剪枝选择验证树，在七个基准上达到最高平均接受长度和加速比，最高每轮接受12.97个token，实现9.73倍无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式草稿模型能并行预测整个token块以降低提案延迟，但其逐位置分布是边际分布，而非基于草稿路径上已选token的条件分布。现有的递归修正只在单一草稿链上引入因果信息，而基于扩散的树结构增加了候选覆盖却没有沿各分支传递修正。因此需要一种方法同时兼顾树状候选覆盖和沿分支的因果信息修正。

Method: DARTree包含两个关键步骤：(1) 构建固定宽度候选树——在每一深度通过单次批处理并行扩展和评分所有节点；(2) 仅应用最佳优先剪枝来选择验证树，从而将AR修正头的推理与顺序堆操作解耦。该方法无需训练，直接扩展预训练的自回归修正头从链结构到树结构。

Result: 在七个数学、代码和聊天基准上，DARTree在所有四种模型-温度配置中均取得最高平均接受长度和加速比。每轮验证最多接受12.97个token，比DFlash多98.6%，比Domino多27.9%，相对于本地测量的自回归解码实现最高9.73倍无损加速。

Conclusion: DARTree通过将AR修正从单链扩展到树结构，有效结合了扩散式草稿的并行性与递归修正的因果信息，在不增加训练成本的前提下实现了更优的投机解码性能，证明了批处理候选树构建与最佳优先剪枝解耦的可行性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates autoregressive language models by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce proposal latency by predicting an entire token block in parallel, but their position-wise distributions are marginal rather than conditioned on tokens selected along each draft path. Existing recurrent correction incorporates causal information along a single draft chain, whereas diffusion-based tree construction broadens candidate coverage without carrying this correction along individual branches. We introduce DARTree, a training-free speculative decoding method that extends a pretrained AR correction head from chains to trees. DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [254] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多标签分类和二元分割中逐实例Jaccard分数（IoU）的校准性质。证明了Jaccard分数、平移损失和普通损失矩阵的非奇异性及仿射维度，建立了CCdim的上下界（2^{s-1} ≤ CCdim ≤ 2^s-1），证明了精确校准需要指数级预测坐标，同时给出了两个多项式维度的近似保证和显式遗憾转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard分数（IoU）是多标签分类和二元分割中的标准评估指标，但其损失矩阵结构、校准性质和所需预测维度等基础理论问题尚未被充分研究。理解精确校准所需的最小预测维度以及在可接受遗憾容忍度下的近似可能性，对设计高效、可扩展的代理损失方法至关重要。

Method: 论文结合有限MinHash Gram表示和布尔Möbius反演来证明损失矩阵的非奇异性和仿射维度。下界通过构建因子加权分布和贝叶斯最优报告实现。此外，提出了两种近似方案：(1) F_1-to-Jaccard转移，利用已有的(s^2+1)维F_1代理；(2) MinHash平方损失代理，包括直接构造和符号变体两种形式。

Result: (1) 证明Jac(∅,∅)=1约定下，Jaccard分数、平移损失和普通损失矩阵均非奇异，损失列的仿射维度为2^s-1；(2) 证明2^{s-1} ≤ CCdim ≤ 2^s-1，任何精确校准的凸代理需要指数级预测坐标；(3) F_1-to-Jaccard转移实现渐近Jaccard遗憾至多3-2√2；(4) MinHash平方损失在任意条件标签分布下达到遗憾下限α，直接和符号变体分别需要O((s^2+s log(1/ρ))/α^2)和O((s+log(1/ρ))/α^2)维度。

Conclusion: 精确Jaccard校准本质上需要指数级维度，这构成了固有的理论瓶颈；然而，对于任意固定的加性遗憾容忍度，多项式预测维度即可实现近似校准。这一"指数vs多项式"的二元性与遗憾容忍度建立了清晰的维度-精度权衡边界。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [255] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"防御性提升器"(Defensive Booster)的在线概率预测算法，它在自适应对抗序列上同时获得两个不兼容的性能保证：一是与在线梯度提升相同的Brier得分竞争性保证，二是在平滑弱学习条件成立时与在线分类提升相同速率的分类错误保证。该算法通过操作化提升的"对偶视角"，仅访问一个弱类别学习器，且运行时间比现有方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线提升技术分别提供两类不相容的保证：在线梯度提升在Brier得分上与H的跨度生成的最佳预测器竞争，但当跨度中不含精确预测器时无任何保证；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下将分类错误驱动到零，但该条件不满足时几乎无保证。作者希望设计一种算法，能够同时获得这两类保证，而不需要预先知道哪种条件会成立。

Method: 作者提出了Defensive Booster算法，该算法操作化了提升的"对偶视角"：当算法的随机化分类错误持续较高时，其错误权重会形成一种平滑的重新加权，使得每个弱假设在该权重上的边(edge)都很低，从而提供事后的硬核证书(hard-core certificate)证明弱学习条件不成立。同时，该算法仅访问一个弱类别学习器（而非像先前方法那样维护大规模弱学习器集成），并且还开发了一个强自适应变体，能在每个时间区间上同时满足两种保证。

Result: Defensive Booster在每条自适应序列上，以与在线梯度提升相同的速率实现与H跨度诱导的最佳预测竞争的Brier得分；同时，每当实现的实际转录满足平滑弱学习条件时，其Brier得分和随机化分类错误满足与在线分类提升相同的速率保证。实验表明其在合成和真实数据流上表现出强的预测性能（有时显著优于所有先前基线），且运行时间比现有方法快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作通过一个高效的算法解决了在线预测中两类重要保证的兼容问题，消除了先前在线提升方法需要维持大规模弱学习器集成的开销，为在线概率预测和提升方法提供了统一的理论框架和实用的高效实现。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.GN'></div>

# q-bio.GN [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [Static analysis-guided agentic AI translation enables Rust as a full stack bioinformatics language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13029)
*Johan Henriksson*

Main category: q-bio.GN

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用agentic AI结合静态分析，将生物信息学领域的遗留代码（如Perl、Fortran编写的旧代码）翻译成现代语言Rust，并在其软件Bascet上验证了效果：代码体积减少约80倍、构建时间缩短约10倍、关键步骤性能提升3倍以上，且去除了Unix依赖，使Bascet成为能在原生Windows上运行的唯一单细胞流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物信息学领域存在大量遗留代码，这些代码可能缺乏维护者、使用过时语言（如Perl、Fortran），带来技术债务、环境负面影响、未能利用现代硬件，且可能存在安全隐患不适合临床使用。亟需一种系统化的方法将这些遗留代码迁移到现代语言。

Method: 使用agentic AI（智能体驱动的人工智能）结合静态分析技术，将遗留代码翻译为Rust语言。作者提供了提示词（prompts）和配套支持软件以辅助系统化翻译，并在NGS（下一代测序）和成像相关的常用软件上进行了评估。

Result: 在软件Bascet上，代码体积减少约80倍，构建时间缩短约10倍，关键步骤性能提升超过3倍。同时移除了Unix依赖，使得Bascet成为唯一能在原生Windows环境下运行（无需容器）的单细胞分析流程。

Conclusion: 大规模重构生物信息学软件在有限预算下成为可能，从而能够开发更复杂的工具。这标志着agentic AI在软件现代化迁移中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The field of bioinformatics struggles with legacy code - old code that is commonly used but may no longer have a maintainer, or may be written in an now-unfamiliar language (e.g. Perl, Fortran). This incurs maintenance cost (technical debt), but dynamically typed languages also negatively impacts the environment and fail to make use of modern hardware. Legacy code may also have security or safety problems that make it unsuited for use in clinical settings. Here we show that agentic AI, combined with static analysis, can be used to translate legacy code to the modern language Rust. We provide prompts and supporting software to aid systematic translation, and evaluate it on common software for NGS and imaging. We showcase the result on our software Bascet: Size was reduced by ~80x, build time decreased by ~10x, and performance of key steps improved >3x. Unix dependencies were also removed, making Bascet the only single-cell pipeline able to run on native Windows, without a container. Large-scale refactoring of bioinformatics software is thus now possible at a limited budget, enabling more complex tools to be developed.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [DCBA: Detection of Collaborative Black-Hole Attacks in Connected Dominated Set using Baiting Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12347)
*Lata B T,Venugopal K R*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级技术（LW-CBH），通过增强AODV路由协议，结合基于定时器的诱饵过程和反向追踪机制，利用MAC层的控制状态消息（R-SEQ和C-SEQ）来检测并隔离MANET中的协作型黑洞攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MANET网络因缺乏中心化管理而容易遭受各类攻击，其中黑洞攻击（特别是协作型黑洞攻击）会严重降低网络性能。现有AODV路由协议在动态拓扑变化中无法有效检测恶意节点，因此需要一种轻量级且高效的检测与隔离机制。

Method: 提出LW-CBH方案，核心包括：(1)基于定时器的诱饵（baiting）过程吸引恶意节点暴露；(2)反向追踪（reverse tracing）机制定位恶意节点；(3)利用MAC层的控制状态消息（Reply Sequence R-SEQ和Code Sequence C-SEQ）通过连接支配集中的节点来检测和隔离恶意节点。该方案对现有AODV路由协议进行增强。

Result: 使用NS-2.35离散事件模拟器进行仿真，评估指标包括吞吐量、分组投递率、平均端到端时延和归一化路由开销，结果表明所提方案能够有效检测和隔离协作型黑洞攻击节点。

Conclusion: LW-CBH通过诱饵与反向追踪结合的轻量级机制，在增强AODV协议的基础上能够有效识别并隔离协作型黑洞攻击，提升MANET在网络攻击环境下的整体性能。

Abstract: Mobile Ad-hoc Network (MANET) is temporary and dynamic network topology, wherein nodes are mobile in nature and distributed randomly in a network area. In MANET, nodes cooperate with each other to operate and forward data through multihop communication between source and destination. MANET is exposed to different types of attacks due to absence of central administration. However, some nodes decline to cooperate, misbehaves and appears to be malicious affecting network functionality and connectivity. Providing security and identifying malicious node has become one of the challenging research topics in MANET. Black-hole attack is considered to be most popular attack that degrades the overall network performance. Black-hole node falsely advertises the shortest path to destination intentionally to disrupt the network communication resulting in packet drop. In collaborative black-hole attacks, multiple black-hole nodes cooperate and launch attacks in order to degrade network reliability. In this article we propose a Lightweight technique to detect and isolate Collaborative Black-Hole attacks (LW-CBH) by enhancing existing AODV routing protocol. In this scheme a timer based baiting process and reverse tracing setup is used to detect malicious node through control status message in MAC layer which are Reply Sequence (R-SEQ) and Code Sequence (C-SEQ) message of connected dominated set of nodes. However existing AODV routing protocol fails to detect malicious node during dynamic topology changing in MANET. Simulation of proposed technique is performed using discrete event simulator tool NS-2.35. The simulation results are evaluated for throughput, packet delivery ratio, average end-to-end delay and normalized routing overhead.

</details>


### [258] [The energetic cost of mitigating AI attacks in cellular networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12431)
*Adrián Losada,Hao Qiang Luo-Chen,David Segura,Carlos S. Alvarez-Merino,Milan Groshev,Emil J. Khatib,Raquel Barco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了蜂窝网络中集成AI/ML所带来的安全漏洞及其防御策略，重点刻画了防御技术带来的计算负载与能源消耗，并阐述了ML精度、鲁棒性和能效三者之间的权衡挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝网络各层面广泛集成AI/ML算法（如O-RAN中的RIC），虽然带来了优化的资源分配等功能，但也因依赖数据而引入新的安全漏洞。现有的防御缓解策略虽有效，却产生计算负载和能源成本，这一成本在当前能源意识背景下常常被忽视。因此有必要对防御技术的能耗进行表征，并揭示ML精度、鲁棒性与能效之间的三方权衡难题。

Method: 本文对一种防御技术（对抗机器学习缓解策略）的能耗消耗进行了特征化刻画/定量分析，并系统地论述了ML精度、鲁棒性和能量效率三者构成的挑战性权衡框架。

Result: 该工作表征了防御技术的能源消耗特征，并明确了ML精度、鲁棒性和能效三者之间存在的挑战性权衡关系（即提升鲁棒性会增加能耗，进而影响能效）。

Conclusion: 作者指出在当下能源意识日益重要的背景下，防御策略带来的能源成本不可忽视，需要在ML精度、鲁棒性和能源效率之间寻求平衡，为后续在该方向上的研究指明了挑战与方向。

Abstract: The integration of Artificial Intelligence (AI), generally as Machine Learning (ML) algorithms, in all levels and aspects of cellular networks demonstrates the success of data-driven algorithms; for example, the Radio Intelligence Controller (RIC) of the O-RAN paradigm bestows the network with optimised radio resource allocation, load balancing or energy efficiency functions, among others. Nevertheless, this dependency on data opens new security vulnerabilities, as attackers can alter data properties and steer ML models to underperform or degrade. Conversely, the developed mitigation strategies are effective, but they generate a computational load which, in consequence, results in an energy cost generally overlooked, even in the current energy-awareness context. In this work, consumption of a defence technique is characterised, and the challenges raised by the triad of ML accuracy, robustness and energy efficiency are outlined.

</details>


### [259] [Non-Degenerate Risk Certification for Automated Security Decisions: A Decision-Contract Theory with ATT\&CK-Aligned Triage as a Worked Instance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12444)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出自动化决策的无条件风险界存在结构性空洞——不行动的选择器即可使风险界归零。作者提出决策契约理论（错误守恒定律、无标签容量认证、非退化可行动性认证），并在ATT&CK对齐的LLM入侵检测告警分流场景中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化决策的风险认证存在空洞性缺陷：一个从不行动的选择器即可将所有风险界驱动为零，说明无条件风险界不构成有意义的自动化保证。作者希望从结构上揭示这类空洞的来源，并建立能排除全弃权退化解的理论框架。

Method: 提出"决策契约"（decision contract）概念，包含系统作用的输入集及输出计为正确的语义关系。在此框架下建立：(1)错误守恒定律——误差仅在有害自动化、人工延迟与语义掩蔽之间再分配；(2)无标签单例容量认证——以无标签方式认证结构性无能力，并提供风险可行细化以区分可恢复的阈值错位与风险约束下的无能力；(3)非退化可行动性认证——通过构造排除全弃权解。

Result: 在3个IDS数据集、6个LLM和4个错误率阈值下，90.3%配置的实证误归属风险达到或低于目标，83.4%均值正确自动化。容量诊断解释了所有低效用配置，细化区分了真正的错位与风险约束下的无能力；训练稳定性复跑未发现确认的结构性无能力实例；真实细粒度攻击子类型标签确认了粗粒化-转移恒等式（在真实多对一映射下），掩蔽质量小但非零。

Conclusion: 自动化决策的风险认证必须显式定义决策契约，否则任何风险界都可能因弃权选择器而空洞化。决策契约理论提供了错误守恒与容量诊断工具，可有效识别并区分自动化能力缺陷的类型，为LLM入侵检测告警分流提供可靠的风险认证框架。

Abstract: An unconditional risk bound on automated decisions can be satisfied without automating anything, since a selector that never acts drives the bound to zero. We show this is structural: any risk certificate is defined over a decision contract, the inputs a system acts on plus the semantic relation under which an output counts correct, and weakening either hides base-classifier error. We develop a decision-contract theory: an error-conservation law showing error is only reassigned among harmful automation, human deferral, and semantic masking; a label-free singleton capacity certifying structural incapacity, with a risk-feasible refinement separating recoverable threshold misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity; and a non-degenerate actionability certificate excluding all-abstain solutions by construction. We instantiate this on ATT\&CK-aligned alert triage for LLM-based intrusion detection, the setting that exposed the vacuity failure. Across 3 IDS datasets, 6 LLMs, and 4 error-rate thresholds, empirical false-attribution risk stays at or below target in 90.3% of configurations, with 83.4% mean correct automation. The capacity diagnostic explains every low-utility configuration; its refinement separates genuine misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity, confirmed by an exhibited alternative threshold; a training-stability re-run finds no confirmed structural-incapacity instance; and real fine-grained attack-subtype labels confirm the coarsening-transfer identity under a genuine many-to-one map, with small but non-zero masking mass.

</details>


### [260] [SoK: From Generation to Consumption of Privacy Documents in Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12511)
*Shidong Pan,Clark LaChance,Zhen Tao,Sepideh Ghanavati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文献综述（SoK）从软件工程视角提供了一个统一的、以生命周期为导向的隐私文档研究全景视图，系统梳理了2010至2025年间发表的290篇论文，围绕五个研究问题（定义与范围、生成、分析与提取、一致性与合规性检查、可用性评估与改进）进行组织，识别出15项关键研究趋势和21个开放机会，并提出了四个更广泛的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私文档（如隐私政策）是数字服务披露数据实践和获取用户同意的重要机制。随着该研究领域快速发展，涵盖传统隐私政策、短通知（如隐私标签）和界面级透明机制等，研究者难以获得一个连贯的、覆盖隐私文档全生命周期的整体视图。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个统一的生命周期视角。

Method: 采用SoK（Systematization of Knowledge）方法论，从软件工程角度，系统性地回顾和分析了2010年至2025年间发表的290篇论文。论文以五个研究问题为组织框架，涵盖隐私文档的定义与范围、生成、分析与提取、一致性与合规性检查、可用性评估与改进等全生命周期环节。

Result: 识别出15项关键研究趋势和21个开放机会，并进一步绘制了四个更广泛的研究方向：AI中心平台的涌现挑战、多样化和最新的数据基础需求、基于LLM的统一策略-代码分析、以及面向终端用户和开发者的双重可用性。

Conclusion: 该SoK旨在为未来关于隐私政策和隐私文档的研究提供一个共享的基础框架，帮助研究社区更清晰、更系统地理解该领域的现状、趋势与空白。

Abstract: Privacy documents (e.g., privacy policies) are a central mechanism through which digital services disclose data practices and seek user consent. Over the past decades, research on privacy documents has expanded significantly, encompassing not only traditional privacy policies but also short notices (e.g., privacy labels) and interface-level transparency mechanisms. As this research area continues to grow, it has become increasingly difficult to obtain a coherent view of how privacy documents are created, analyzed, evaluated, and maintained across their lifecycle. This SoK provides a unified, lifecycle-oriented view of privacy documents from a software engineering perspective. We systematically review and analyze 290 papers published between 2010 and 2025, organizing them around five research questions that examine how privacy documents are (1) defined and scoped, (2) generated, (3) analyzed and extracted, (4) checked for inconsistencies and noncompliance, and (5) evaluated and improved for usability. Building on our findings, we identify 15 key research trends and 21 open opportunities. We further chart four broader research directions that highlight (i) emerging challenges in AI-centric platforms, (ii) the need for diverse and up-to-date data foundations, (iii) LLM-based unified policy-code analysis, and (iv) dual usability for end-users and developers. We hope this SoK provides a shared foundation for future research on privacy policies and privacy documents.

</details>


### [261] [Tracing Provenance and Detecting Tampering with Complementary LLM Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12713)
*Xiaoyan Feng,Yanjun Zhang,He Zhang,Leo Yu Zhang,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种联合水印方法，在同一令牌中共同嵌入鲁棒信号（保真溯源）和脆弱信号（篡改检测），通过独立密钥和不同播种窗口实现双重功能，支持Intact、Tampered、No-Watermark三种检测状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印的鲁棒性在保持溯源的同时，也允许攻击者篡改关键内容仍保留归属，即piggyback spoofing漏洞。需要一种既能证明来源又能提供篡改证据的水印方案。

Method: 在规范化文本上使用独立密钥和不同播种窗口，将鲁棒信号和脆弱信号共同嵌入每个生成的token；信号共享同一机制但参数独立；采用多轮无偏锦标赛重加权保留期望生成分布，通过周期性轮次分配模式控制两种信号间的权衡；检测时两信号分数构成二维空间，支持三态决策。

Result: 在两种大语言模型和两个提示数据集上，该方法实现了最高的篡改检测率，同时保持有竞争力的归因鲁棒性和困惑度。消融研究表明三态可靠检测需要清晰的完整度定义、两信号共同嵌入以及互补的编辑敏感度。

Conclusion: 所提出的双信号联合水印方案在溯源归属与篡改检测之间取得了有效平衡，能够可靠区分完整内容、被篡改内容和无水印内容，弥补了现有鲁棒水印在篡改证据方面的不足。

Abstract: Watermarking LLM-generated text is an important task for tracing its provenance. Existing LLM watermarks preserve provenance under editing, but this same robustness allows an adversary to alter critical content while retaining attribution, a vulnerability known as piggyback spoofing. We introduce an innovative watermark that jointly provides provenance and tamper evidence. It co-embeds a robust signal and a fragile signal into each generated token. The signals share the same mechanism but use independent keys and different seeding windows over normalized text, making one resilient to edits and the other sensitive to reader-visible changes. Multiple rounds of unbiased tournament reweighting preserve the expected generation distribution, while a periodic round-allocation pattern controls the trade-off between the two signals. At detection, their scores form a two-dimensional space supporting three decisions: Intact, Tampered, and No-Watermark. Across two large language models and two prompt datasets, our method demonstrates the highest tamper-detection rate among the evaluated methods while maintaining competitive attribution robustness and perplexity. Ablation studies show that reliable three-state detection requires a well-defined notion of intactness, co-embedding of the two signals, and complementary sensitivity to edits.

</details>


### [262] [PIPES: Securing Agent Perception with Provenance and Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12789)
*Sanjay Kariyappa,Severin Klingler,G. Edward Suh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了工具使用智能体面临的状态腐败攻击问题，并提出PIPES框架（基于来源追踪与语义先验的筛选机制），在六个基准测试上将攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%，同时保持甚至提升良性场景的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具响应的各组成部分来源不同、可信度不同，但响应中缺少关于"谁产生了每个组件"以及"该组件应该在什么信息范围内传达"的标识。这种信息缺口使攻击者能够通过注入受控内容制造环境层面的虚假主张，从而破坏智能体对环境的感知，使后续行动在现有防护机制看来是合理的。作者旨在填补这一安全缺口。

Method: PIPES包含两个核心机制：(1) 静态字段契约——当模式提供稳定期望时，用于筛查结构化字段；(2) 对开放内容的条件筛查——基于执行前的对话轨迹和可信来源元数据动态决定是否筛查。PIPES根据语义先验和来源层级识别违规单元，部署时可选择移除、警告、阻断或升级处理。文中实现了原子移除操作，并针对自适应的PAIR风格攻击进行评估。

Result: 在Gemma 4 31B IT作为目标智能体的VitaBench三个子集和AgentDyn三个子集上，PIPES将平均攻击成功率从84.7%降低至2.3%；良性效用从90.6%提升至92.5%（带PIPES），表明防御在安全性大幅提升的同时并未牺牲、甚至略微提升了正常任务表现。

Conclusion: PIPES通过引入来源追踪（provenance）和语义先验的筛选机制，有效防御了工具使用智能体的状态腐败攻击，且不损害良性效用，为构建更安全的工具调用型AI代理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Tool-using agents consume external data from sources with different levels of trust, yet tool responses rarely identify who produced each component or what it should convey. We show that this gap enables state-corruption attacks, in which attacker-controlled content makes environmental claims beyond the informational authority of its response component and corrupts the agent's perceived environment, making the resulting action appear justified to existing guardrails. We introduce PIPES (Provenance-Informed, Prior-Enforced Screening), which screens response units using semantic priors and source provenance. PIPES uses static field contracts when schemas provide stable expectations, and conditions screening of open-ended content on the pre-response trajectory and trusted provenance metadata. It marks units that violate their semantic prior or the provenance hierarchy; deployments may remove, warn, block, or escalate detected violations. We instantiate atomic removal and evaluate PIPES against adaptive PAIR-style attacks. Across the three VitaBench and three AgentDyn splits with Gemma 4 31B IT as the target agent, PIPES reduces average attack success from 84.7% to 2.3%, while preserving average benign utility (92.5% with PIPES versus 90.6% without defense).

</details>


### [263] [RealmEye: Virtual Machine Introspection for Arm CCA Realm VMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12822)
*Ruofei Qu,Wei Feng,Hongzhan Ma,Menghan Jia,Muyan Shen,Yu Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RealmEye，这是首个针对Arm CCA Realm VM的虚拟机自省（VMI）系统。RealmEye将全部自省逻辑部署在Realm Management Monitor（RMM）的R-EL2层，通过硬件强制的隔离实现对外部VM的内存、寄存器和页级访问的监控，无需在Realm内部运行任何agent，支持LibVMI和DRAKVUF等现有工具的即插即用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密虚拟机（CVM）的硬件隔离在保护敏感工作负载免受恶意云平台攻击的同时，也使得租户无法查看VM内部运行状态，导致内核rootkit（如进程隐藏、内核数据篡改、模型权重窃取）可以在隔离掩护下肆意活动。现有VMI系统依赖可信Hypervisor，而CVM将Hypervisor排除在TCB之外；目前最先进的CVM-VMI方案00SEVen依赖AMD SEV-SNP特有的VMPL0机制，该机制在Arm CCA上不存在，导致Realm VM完全没有自省解决方案。

Method: RealmEye将完整的自省逻辑嵌入Arm CCA的RMM（Realm Management Monitor）的R-EL2权限层，实现监控器与被监控VM之间的硬件强制隔离：1) 不依赖任何in-VM agent，Realm保持未修改状态；2) 能够读取Realm内存和寄存器状态；3) 可挂起VM以获取一致的快照；4) 可陷阱捕获页级访问；5) 采用周期性自驱动触发模式，扫描时序完全在RMM内部，防止Hypervisor与Realm内rootkit串通；6) 通过硬件认证通道将结果返回给远程所有者；7) 提供CCA驱动后端，使LibVMI和DRAKVUF等现有工具无需修改即可与RealmEye互操作。

Result: 在Arm FVP（Fixed Virtual Platform）上，RealmEye成功检测到Diamorphine rootkit的进程隐藏和系统调用表hook行为。其位于RMM内部的开销可根据原语调用次数进行线性预测，表明性能开销具有高度可预测性和可分析性。

Conclusion: RealmEye填补了Arm CCA平台上CVM缺乏自省能力的空白，通过将自省逻辑移入RMM层，在保持硬件隔离的同时实现了对Realm VM的完整监控能力，为Arm CCA上的机密计算环境提供了首个实用的恶意行为检测方案。

Abstract: Confidential VMs (CVMs) have become the dominant substrate for sensitive cloud workloads, from financial services to privacy-preserving AI inference. The hardware isolation that protects these CVMs from a malicious cloud also blinds their owners to what runs inside them: kernel rootkits planted via network or supply-chain attacks can hide processes, tamper with kernel data, and exfiltrate model weights under the cover of the same isolation that defends the VM. Tenants therefore need to inspect a running CVM from outside, yet classical VM introspection (VMI) presupposes a trusted Hypervisor, which CVMs exclude from the TCB. The state-of-the-art CVM-VMI system, 00SEVen, restores introspection on AMD SEV-SNP via an in-VM agent at a privileged tier (VMPL0), a mechanism that does not exist on Arm CCA, leaving Realm VMs without any introspection solution.
  We present RealmEye, the first VMI system for Arm CCA Realm VMs. RealmEye places the entire introspection logic inside the Realm Management Monitor (RMM) at R-EL2, achieving hardware-enforced separation between the monitor and the monitored VM: no agent runs inside the Realm, and the Realm remains unmodified. RealmEye reads Realm memory and registers, suspends the VM for consistent snapshots, and traps page-level accesses, without relying on any in-VM interface. A periodic, self-driven trigger mode keeps scan timing internal to the RMM, preventing the Hypervisor from colluding with in-Realm rootkits. Results are returned to the remote owner over a hardware-attested channel, and a CCA driver backend lets existing tools such as LibVMI and DRAKVUF interoperate with RealmEye unchanged. On the Arm FVP, RealmEye detects process hiding and syscall-table hooking by Diamorphine, and its in-RMM cost is linearly predictable from primitive invocation counts.

</details>


### [264] [Beyond Source: An Empirical Study of Python Bytecode Security Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12853)
*Baihong Chen,Tian Xie,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Python字节码（.pyc文件）作为安全工件进行了实证研究，发现字节码在PyPI生态中被广泛暴露，现有分析工具在鲁棒性上存在显著缺陷，且恶意构造的字节码可导致解释器崩溃、内存损坏等问题，且这些行为无法从源码级复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python包安全分析主要聚焦于源码层面，但Python运行时可直接执行字节码（.pyc文件、编译模块、marshalled code对象），形成"检查-执行"之间的间隙。攻击者可能利用此间隙绕过基于源码的审查与检测，因此需要系统地研究字节码作为安全工件的特性。

Method: 本研究对1,034,843个PyPI制品进行大规模收集与扫描，测量字节码暴露程度；使用版本感知的工具链（多个反编译器）评估CPython 3.8-3.14字节码的可分析性；通过对抗性字节码和模糊测试（fuzzing）评估CPython运行时及反编译工具的鲁棒性，并对字节码发现进行源码级复现测试。

Result: 共识别出7,388个含字节码的制品，包含228,578个.pyc文件和28,193个制品内无源码的.pyc文件。现代CPython字节码中204,904个在测文件里204,901个至少能由一个反编译器产出源码（但仅测量产出而非功能等价性）。工具鲁棒性差：PyPI字节码触发托管代码异常与超时，对抗性突变字节码导致反编译器原生进程崩溃，共总结17种鲁棒性签名。模糊测试产生1,009个堆栈去重运行时发现，以指针解引用症状为主，261组具有潜在内存损坏特征，至少91.7%的执行超出文档标注的非安全摄入边界，且均无法从普通Python源码复现。

Conclusion: 字节码是生态中可见的制品、实际可分析的目标，同时也是与安全相关的解释器输入，其行为无需与源码级行为一致。这揭示了现有Python包安全审查存在"检查-执行"间隙的严重风险，提示安全工具链需要将字节码纳入审计范围。

Abstract: Python package security is largely source-centric, yet Python runtimes can execute bytecode directly through .pyc files, compiled-only modules, and marshalled code objects, creating an inspection-execution gap. We present an empirical study of Python bytecode as a security artifact. We measure bytecode exposure in PyPI distributions, evaluate practical analyzability using version-aware tooling, assess CPython runtime robustness under adversarial bytecode, and test source-level reproduction of bytecode findings. Across 1,034,843 collected PyPI artifacts, we identify 7,388 bytecode-containing artifacts, including 228,578 .pyc files and 28,193 artifact-local source-less .pyc files. For modern CPython 3.8-3.14 bytecode, at least one selected decompiler emits source for 204,901 of 204,904 in-scope files, a result measuring emission rather than verified functional equivalence. Tools are non-robust: observed PyPI bytecode triggers managed-code exceptions and timeouts, while adversarial mutated bytecode also drives decompilers into native process failures; together these outcomes yield 17 distinct robustness signatures. Fuzzing produces 1,009 stack-deduplicated runtime findings dominated by pointer-dereference symptoms; 261 groups exhibit potential memory-corruption characteristics, and at least 91.7% of groups reach execution beyond the documented-unsafe ingestion boundary. None reproduce from ordinary Python source. Bytecode is thus a visible ecosystem artifact, a practical analysis target, and a security-relevant interpreter input whose behavior need not match source-level behavior.

</details>
