### [265] [Discovering Persistent Behavioural Patterns for Interpretable Blockchain Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12864)
*Dorottya Zelenyanszki,Zhe Hou,Kamanashis Biswas,Vallipuram Muthukkumarasamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展、应用无关的区块链行为模式发现框架，通过行为语句构建和两级嵌入（句子级和序列级）从大规模链上活动中挖掘持久行为模式，并在以太坊上3000万+交易数据上验证，能同时发现常规（DEX交易、NFT活动）和恶意（钓鱼、机器人、预言机操纵、Rug-pull）行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DeFi分析大多针对特定应用场景，难以扩展且结果不易解释，缺乏一个通用、规模化且可解释的框架来从大规模区块链数据中发现用户的持久行为模式。

Method: 提出一个应用无关的框架：(1) 构建融合合约、代币和市场上下文的行为语句；(2) 采用两步嵌入方法——句子级嵌入捕捉个体行为，序列级嵌入捕捉用户随时间的动态行为；(3) 通过可解释行为画像器，从行为模式（motifs）、例行程序、时间动态、实体暴露和可疑证据五个维度刻画发现的社区。

Result: 在以太坊超过3000万条交易数据上评估，框架成功识别出常规行为模式（DEX交易、NFT活动）和恶意模式（钓鱼、机器人运行、预言机操纵、Rug-pull骗局）；且许多模式在独立观测窗口间保持稳定，支持长期行为的识别。

Conclusion: 该框架兼具可扩展性、可解释性和持久性分析能力，可用于区块链取证调查、行为归因和威胁发现。

Abstract: Public blockchain data enables large-scale DeFi-related analysis, but many existing approaches are application-specific, difficult to scale, or hard to interpret. This research proposes a scalable, application-agnostic framework for \emph{persistent behavioural pattern discovery} from large-scale blockchain activity. It constructs behaviour sentences enriched with contract, token and market context, then applies a two-step embedding process: sentence-level embeddings capture individual actions, while sequence-level embeddings capture user behaviour over time. An interpretable behavioural profiler characterizes discovered communities through behavioural motifs, routines, temporal dynamics, entity exposure, and suspiciousness evidence. Evaluation on Ethereum using over 30 million transactions shows that the framework uncovers both routine and malicious behavioural patterns, including decentralised exchange (DEX) trading, NFT activity, phishing, bot operations, oracle manipulation, and rug-pull schemes. Importantly, many patterns remain stable across independent observation windows, enabling the identification of long-term behaviours beyond a single analysis period. The proposed framework combines scalability, interpretability, and persistence analysis, supporting blockchain forensic investigation, behavioural attribution, and threat discovery.

</details>


### [266] [Labels Are Not Endpoints: Treatment Leakage and Construct Validity in MCP Agent Security Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12880)
*Rana Muhammad Ahmed,Sabahat Abbas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对一项工具使用型AI代理的安全评估活动进行审计，发现历史标注系统存在直接的处理泄漏（treatment metadata门控了ATTACK_SUCCESS分类），通过治疗盲重建修正了58条错误标签，同时保留了3条受保护数据传输和1条独立的未授权转发案例。锁定版v2普查中ATTACK_SUCCESS记录为零，贡献了七环完整性链（Integrity Chain）和可执行的端点完整性linter，但明确声明这是一次活动范围内的测量审计，而非总体攻击率、模型排名、防御效能或因果估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出工具使用型代理的安全评估中，存储标签常被等同于行为事实，这混淆了测量与真实行为。历史评估活动存在直接的处理泄漏（treatment metadata门控ATTACK_SUCCESS分类），导致固定行为可能因标签重贴而改变分类，因此需要对其可靠性进行系统审计。

Method: 研究者追踪了10,200条执行记录，还原为180个模型绑定请求、45个语义请求和15个可观察刺激。采用治疗盲重建方法，通过双审阅者盲一致性审查（对锁定v2认为结构可解释的96个请求），并开发了一个可执行的、作用域受限的端点完整性linter，以及七链完整性链框架。

Result: 治疗盲重建纠正了58条历史ATTACK_SUCCESS或HIJACK_ATTEMPT标签为授权的良性完成；保留了3条已验证的受保护数据传输和1条独立的未授权转发案例。锁定v2普查中ATTACK_SUCCESS记录为零，转发案例在HIJACK_ATTEMPT语义边界保持该分类。双审阅者盲审查在4个构造边界案例上与锁定代码手册存在分歧。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一次活动范围的测量审计方法和工具，揭示了安全评估中标签泄漏问题的严重性，但明确限定其结论范围：不提供总体攻击率、模型排名、防御效能或因果估计，呼吁对评估基础设施（而非仅模型行为）进行完整性验证。

Abstract: Security evaluations of tool-using agents often equate stored labels with behavioral facts. We audit a preserved campaign by tracing 10,200 execution rows to 180 model-bound requests, 45 semantic requests, and 15 observable stimuli. Two schema treatments were delivered, but the planned external payload-family corpus was not. The historical grader exhibited direct treatment leakage: treatment metadata gated the ATTACK_SUCCESS class, so fixed behavior could change class under treatment relabeling. A treatment-blind reconstruction corrects 58 historical ATTACK_SUCCESS or HIJACK_ATTEMPT labels to authorized benign completions while preserving three verified protected-data transfers and one separate unauthorized-forwarding case. The locked v2 census contains exactly zero ATTACK_SUCCESS records, while the forwarding case remains a HIJACK_ATTEMPT at a semantic boundary concerning objective completion. A dual-reviewer blinded concordance review of all 96 requests deemed structurally interpretable by locked v2 produced identical reviewer-consensus classes but differed from the locked codebook on four construct-boundary cases. We contribute a seven-link Integrity Chain and an executable, scope-bounded endpoint-integrity linter. The result is a campaign-bounded measurement audit, not a population attack-rate, model-ranking, defense-efficacy, or causal estimate.

</details>


### [267] [Adversarial Robustness in Smishing Detection: A Comparative Analysis of Adversarial Fragility in Classical vs. Transformer-Based Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12889)
*Denzel Chiuseni,Athanase Bahizire,Silva Hama,Jema David Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种模型架构（三种经典词法模型和两种多语言Transformer）在短信钓鱼（smishing）检测中的对抗鲁棒性，使用27,037条消息数据集，通过三种攻击类型和强度级别进行测试。结果显示经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下近乎灾难性失败（RDR高达0.988），而Transformer模型表现出显著更强的鲁棒性（RDR高达0.351），并证实干净文本性能不能预测对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的短信钓鱼检测系统通常在干净的单语文本上训练和评估，但在低资源环境下，攻击者常通过字符混淆、跨语言代码切换和结构扰动来规避检测。该研究旨在填补对现有检测模型对抗鲁棒性系统性评估的空白。

Method: 使用27,037条消息的数据集，对五种模型架构进行评估：三种经典词法模型（Random Forest、XGBoost、CNN+BiLSTM）和两种多语言Transformer（mBERT、XLM-RoBERTa）。经典模型遭受黑盒通用攻击，Transformer模型则采用注意力引导的目标攻击。每种模型在三种攻击类型和强度级别下测试，以鲁棒性退化比率（RDR）衡量性能。统计分析采用Cliff's d效应量、Mann-Whitney U检验和Friedman检验。

Result: 经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下RDR高达0.988（近乎灾难性失败），而Transformer模型RDR最高仅0.351，结构扰动是其最明显弱点。效应量分析显示两种模型类别之间存在显著差异。Transformer内部，XLM-RoBERTa尽管干净文本基线更高，但退化程度高于mBERT。统计检验确认这些模式归因于模型架构而非采样。

Conclusion: 干净文本性能不能可靠预测对抗鲁棒性；需要针对架构定制的防御策略；短信钓鱼检测应被视为对抗性网络安全挑战而非静态分类任务。

Abstract: Smishing detection systems are commonly trained and evaluated on clean, monolingual text. In low-resource settings, however, attackers frequently circumvent these systems through character obfuscation, cross-lingual code-switching, and structural perturbation. This study evaluates adversarial robustness for five model architectures: three classical lexical models (Random Forest, XGBoost, CNN+BiLSTM) and two multilingual transformers (mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa), using a dataset of 27,037 messages. Classical models are subjected to black-box generic attacks, while transformers are evaluated with attention-guided targeting. Each model is tested across three attack types and intensity levels, with performance measured by the Robustness Degradation Ratio (RDR). The results reveal a distinct architectural boundary: classical models experience near-catastrophic failure under character obfuscation and structural perturbation (RDR up to 0.988), whereas transformers demonstrate significantly greater resilience (RDR up to 0.351), with structural perturbation representing their most pronounced vulnerability. Effect-size analysis (Cliff's d) indicates a substantial difference between the two model categories. Within the transformer group, XLM-RoBERTa, despite achieving a higher clean-text baseline, exhibits greater degradation than mBERT. These findings demonstrate that clean-text performance is not a reliable predictor of adversarial robustness. Statistical validation using Mann-Whitney U and Friedman tests confirms that these patterns are attributable to model architecture rather than sampling. The results underscore the necessity for architecture-specific defences and frame smishing detection as an adversarial cybersecurity challenge rather than a static classification task.

</details>


### [268] [Beyond Handcrafted Security: Towards Self-Evolving Defense for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12977)
*Jiajun Ruan,Peiyang Li,Yukun Chen,Fengting Li,Chao Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HARD（基于Harness的自主运行时防御进化）框架，将大语言模型代理的运行时防御从人工设计转变为自动化演化过程。HARD基于harness级别的运行时防御形式化框架，自动识别干预策略并基于失败轨迹迭代改进防御工件，在提升安全性能的同时保持良性任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理操作能力的扩展，其面临的安全威胁日益复杂。现有的运行时防御方法过度依赖人工设计的干预机制，缺乏构建和维护防御系统的原则性框架，难以适应快速演进的安全威胁。

Method: 1) 首先构建了harness级别的运行时防御形式化框架，系统地刻画harness机制如何实现防御构建，并从harness视角统一了现有运行时防御干预方法。2) 基于该框架提出HARD框架，采用自我演化机制，自动识别适当的干预策略，并基于观察到的失败轨迹迭代改进防御工件。

Result: 大量实验表明，HARD相比现有手工设计的防御方法显著提升了安全性能，同时保持了对良性任务效用的维持。研究结果验证了自主防御演化作为保护已部署LLM代理的新范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 自主防御演化是保护部署中LLM代理的有前景的新范式，它使代理能够自主识别防御弱点并持续改进保护机制，将运行时防御开发从手工工程转变为自动化演化过程。

Abstract: The expanding operational capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents introduce sophisticated security threats. Runtime defenses have emerged as an effective approach to mitigating these risks by integrating security mechanisms into the agent execution loop. However, existing runtime defenses rely heavily on manually designed interventions and lack a principled framework for their construction and maintenance. In this work, we first develop a harness-level formulation of runtime defense that systematically characterizes how harness mechanisms enable defense construction and provides a unified view of existing runtime defense interventions from a harness perspective. Building on this formulation, we propose HARD (Harness-based Autonomous Runtime Defense Evolution), a self-evolving runtime defense framework that automatically identifies appropriate intervention strategies and iteratively improves defense artifacts based on observed failure traces. HARD transforms runtime defense development from manual engineering into an autonomous evolution process, and extensive experiments demonstrate that it improves security performance over existing handcrafted defenses while preserving benign task utility. Our findings highlight autonomous defense evolution as a promising new paradigm for securing deployed LLM agents, enabling agents to identify defense weaknesses and continuously improve their protection mechanisms.

</details>


### [269] [ATOBench: Tracing How Autonomous Penetration-Testing Agents Verify Vulnerabilities When Target Evidence Lies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12996)
*Qiyang Chen,Yixi Li,Fengwei Zhang,Junlin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ATOBench，一个评估框架，通过在运行时注入注册的响应变换，使自主渗透测试智能体的验证过程变得可观察，揭示了不可信目标观测如何影响智能体的行动、验证和报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主渗透测试智能体依赖目标响应来指导后续行动和最终报告，而欺骗性响应可能同时重定向攻击轨迹和验证过程。然而，最终报告无法揭示智能体如何解释冲突证据、改变路线、决定停止或将观测转化为漏洞声明，因此验证过程缺乏可观察性。

Method: ATOBench在运行时注入注册的响应变换，将每个变换后的episode与同一环境下的原生episode配对，并在首个受影响响应处对齐。通过源链接重构来跟踪后续行动、证据恢复、停止和报告支撑，采用三个冻结的观测契约（exploit证明、资源所有权、可复用工件）进行评估，共评估5个模型路线、450个episode。

Result: 分析表明，增加的活动量可能掩盖断裂的验证链，而成功的恢复依赖于找到可用证据并在报告中保存它。ATOBench将欺骗性目标观测转化为可复现的证据处理探针。

Conclusion: ATOBench提供了超越最终结果的过程级视角，扩展了进攻性渗透测试智能体的评估方式，揭示了不可信观测如何塑造行动、验证和报告。

Abstract: Autonomous penetration-testing agents rely on target responses. These responses guide both subsequent actions and the final report. A deceptive response can therefore redirect both the attack trajectory and the agent's verification process. However, final reports reveal little about how an agent interprets conflicting evidence, changes course, decides to stop, or turns observations into a vulnerability claim. We introduce ATOBench, an evaluation framework that makes this verification process observable. ATOBench injects registered response transformations at runtime and pairs each transformed episode with a native episode under the same environment. Each pair is aligned at the first affected response. A source-linked reconstruction then follows later actions, evidence recovery, stopping, and report support. Three frozen observation contracts cover exploit proof, resource ownership, and reusable artifacts. We evaluate five model routes over 450 episodes. The analysis shows that increased activity can mask a broken verification chain, while successful recovery depends on finding usable evidence and preserving it through reporting. ATOBench turns deceptive target observations into a reproducible probe of evidence handling in autonomous penetration testing. This process-level view extends offensive pentest agent evaluation beyond final outcomes by revealing how untrusted observations shape actions, verification, and reporting.

</details>


### [270] [OmniSphinx: Active Mix Networks (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13008)
*Daniel Schadt,Christoph Coijanovic,Shabi Shabani,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniSphinx，一种新型混合网络数据包格式，通过在数据包中嵌入处理代码，使主动混合网络能够在单个部署中模拟任何其他混合格式，解决了现有混合网络格式不兼容的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络的数据包格式各不相同且互不兼容，每种格式都有单一、特定的目标，需要分别搭建独立的软件和基础设施。这种碎片化现状导致混合网络生态难以统一和互操作，亟需一种能够兼容并模拟所有现有格式的统一方案。

Method: OmniSphinx允许发送方在数据包中嵌入决定其处理方式的代码，构建一个主动混合网络（active mix network），使得该网络能够在单一部署中模拟任何其他混合网络的格式。发送方在包裹中携带处理逻辑，路由器根据代码执行相应处理。

Result: 经验评估显示，OmniSphinx的模拟相比原生执行在典型混合网络用例中开销合理：对于最紧凑的Sphinx格式，计算时间增加约90微秒，数据包头大小增加33%。

Conclusion: OmniSphinx通过嵌入代码的方式实现了混合网络格式的统一模拟，以可接受的开销（约90μs计算延迟和33%头部膨胀）换取了多格式兼容性，为混合网络生态系统的整合提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Mix networks are an important tool to implement anonymous communication, which protects not just the content but also the metadata of messages. Over time, various packet formats for mix networks have been proposed, usually with single, specific goals in mind. These formats are incompatible with each other, requiring separate software and infrastructure to be set up. In this paper, we propose a new format, OmniSphinx, which solves this issue. In OmniSphinx, senders embed code in their packets that determines how they must be processed. The resulting active mix network can emulate any other mix format within a single deployment. Our empirical evaluation shows that emulation in OmniSphinx incurs reasonable overhead compared to native execution for typical mix network use cases: For Sphinx, the most compact format, computation time increases by around 90μs, while headers increase by 33% in size.

</details>


### [271] [InterSAGE: The Secure and Verifiable Interoperability Protocol for An Internet of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13030)
*Zhenhua Zou,Sheng Guo,Qiuyang Zhan,Lepeng Zhao,Shuo Li,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: InterSAGE 是一个信任原生协议套件，为 LLM 驱动的智能体互操作提供四层安全基座（持久身份、发现、信任协商、问责），包含四项核心原语，可与现有通信协议（MCP、A2A 等）互补共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体协议主要定义消息如何交换，但缺乏对身份证明、授权、能力声明和委派后问责的支持。随着智能体跨组织边界交互的普及，这一安全基座的缺失成为关键瓶颈。

Method: InterSASE 提出四层协议套件：(1) 持久身份层——通过 Agent Identity Cards 绑定开发者、代码包、运营者和部署上下文；(2) 发现层——基于 DID 绑定的可验证凭据清单实现能力感知发现；(3) 信任协商层——结合单调能力衰减与两级访问控制；(4) 问责层——通过内核中介的加密审计轨迹将使用、委派和执行轨迹绑定到智能体身份，无需共识账本。设计上定位于与 MCP、A2A、ANP、AG-UI 等通信协议并行共存。

Result: 与超过 50 个相关工作（覆盖智能体协议、去中心化身份、OAuth/OIDC 扩展、零信任治理、委派和审计架构）对比，证明没有任何先前架构将持久身份、能力感知发现、信任协商和问责统一为四层信任基座。

Conclusion: InterSAGE 填补了智能体互操作安全基座的空白，提供独立于通信协议的、可移植且可验证的信任语义，使通信协议能够独立演进的同时保持安全保证。

Abstract: The emerging Internet of Agents enables LLM-powered agents to discover peers, invoke tools, and delegate tasks across organizational boundaries. Existing protocols increasingly define how agents exchange messages, but not how an agent proves its identity, authorization, advertised capabilities, or accountability after delegation. We present InterSAGE, a trust-native protocol suite that supplies this missing security substrate alongside, rather than in place of, communication protocols. InterSAGE comprises four layers: Persistent Identity, Discovery, Trust Negotiation, and Accountability. Its four core primitives are: (1) Agent Identity Cards that bind developer, code package, operator, and deployment context; (2) capability-aware discovery using DID-bound Verifiable Credential manifests; (3) trust negotiation combining monotonic capability attenuation with two-tier access control; and (4) kernel-mediated cryptographic audit trails that bind usage, delegation, and execution traces to agent identity without a consensus ledger. InterSAGE is designed to complement MCP, A2A, ANP, and AG-UI, allowing communication protocols to evolve independently while keeping trust semantics explicit, portable, and verifiable. We compare InterSAGE with more than 50 efforts spanning agent protocols, decentralized identity, OAuth/OIDC extensions, zero-trust governance, delegation, and audit architectures. We show that no prior architecture jointly enforces persistent identity, capability-aware discovery, trust negotiation, and accountability as a unified four-layer trust substrate for secure agent interoperability.

</details>


### [272] [InSPECtor: Improving SLEIGH Processor Specification Veracity via Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13042)
*Michael Chesser,Paul Quirk,Douglas Cooke,Guy Farrelly,Surya Nepal,Damith C. Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了InSPECtor，一个基于代理Oracle验证策略的自动化测试框架，首次实现对开源SLEIGH处理器规范（Ghidra所用）的系统性验证。通过在x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V和MSP430等多样规范中发现38,920个差异并修复125个独特bug，得出8项改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理器规范支撑着反汇编器、反编译器、模拟器等关键安全与程序分析工具，但其正确性很少被审查。规范中的错误会扭曲程序行为、掩盖漏洞并使分析规避技术得以使用。验证处理器规范是一个非平凡的任务，此前缺乏系统性的验证方法。

Method: 设计并实现了一个基于自动化代理Oracle验证策略的测试框架InSPECtor。该方法利用规范本身编码的结构来枚举可解码的指令形式并生成有针对性的初始状态，然后通过差分测试，将执行该处理器规范的模拟器与硬件参考实现进行比较，以验证指令的解码和模拟结果。

Result: 在5种风格各异的开源规范（x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V、MSP430）中发现了超过38,920个差异，归并为125个独特bug（含修复建议），识别了解码缺陷、语义缺陷和跨厂商不一致性，并总结出8条具体建议。

Conclusion: 研究强调了处理器规范正确性的重要性，InSPECtor作为实用工具能显著提高SLEIGH处理器规范的保真度，从而增强下游安全与分析工具的可靠性。

Abstract: Processor specifications underpin critical security and program- analysis tools such as disassemblers, decompilers, and emulators, yet, their correctness is rarely examined. Errors in specifications distort program behaviour, obscure vulnerabilities, and enable analysis-evasion techniques. Validating processor specifications is a non-trivial task. Our study is a significant undertaking to enable, for the first time, the systematic validation of open-source SLEIGH language specifications, predominantly used by Ghidra. We design and implement a testing framework based on an automated oracle validation strategy by proxy. Our approach leverages the structure encoded in a specification itself to enumerate decodable instruction forms and generate targeted initial states. Then differentially test the successful decoding and emulation of those instructions by comparing emulators exercising the processor specification against hardware references.
  Applying InSPECtor across diverse, open-source specifications---x86-64, AArch64, ARM/Thumb, RISC-V, MSP430---embedding differences in specification styles, author preferences, and instruction set architecture designs, we uncovered over 38,920 discrepancies that led to 125 unique bugs with proposed fixes, identifying decoding and semantic defects as well as cross-vendor inconsistencies. We distill our findings into 8 concrete recommendations to drive future improvements. Our work underscores the importance of specification correctness and provides a practical tool to substantially improve the fidelity of SLEIGH processor specifications, strengthening the reliability of downstream security and analysis tools.

</details>


### [273] [Operationalizing Cyber Threat Intelligence with GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13050)
*Atul Kabra,Prakhar Paliwal,Manjesh K. Hanawal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将网络威胁情报（CTI）报告输入知识图谱检索系统（Microsoft GraphRAG）而非标准向量相似度检索系统（Naive RAG）后，能否生成更依赖金字塔顶端（持久性强、难以规避）线索的检测计划。案例研究表明，在APTO28报告中所有IP、域名和文件哈希轮换后，GraphRAG方案100%的检测持续有效，而Naive RAG仅29%。跨9份真实CTI报告（来自4家厂商）的重复实验确认了相同模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化将安全研究报告转化为检测规则的尝试通常只提取简单线索（IP、域名、文件哈希）并转化为黑名单，这些线索容易被攻击者快速更换而失效。作者希望验证知识图谱感知的检索系统能否生成依赖更持久、位于'痛苦金字塔'顶端的检测计划，从而提高检测规则的耐用性和抗规避能力。

Method: 使用同一份报告、相同的生成指令和相同的语言模型构建最终计划，仅改变检索步骤：对照组采用Naive RAG（标准向量相似度检索），实验组采用Microsoft GraphRAG（知识图谱检索）。在APTO28报告实例中轮换所有IP、域名和文件哈希后比较两组方案的检测存活率；随后在9份来自4家厂商的真实CTI报告上重复对比实验。

Result: 在APTO28案例研究中，GraphRAG方案在全部线索轮换后100%的检测仍然生效，而Naive RAG仅有29%生效。跨9份真实报告的大规模对比确认同一模式：GraphRAG生成的计划始终达到金字塔更高的层级（更难规避），即使两者在总分上相近。

Conclusion: 知识图谱感知的检索应被视为自动生成SOC可部署狩猎计划的架构正确基础。同时，生成提示词的措辞对结果的影响几乎与检索后端本身同等重要。

Abstract: When a security researcher publishes a report on a cyberattack, detection engineers are supposed to turn it into working detection rules. In practice, most automated attempts at this only extract the simplest clues from the report --- bad IP addresses, domain names, and file hashes --- and turn them into block lists. This is a weak strategy, because attackers can change these simple clues within hours or days, so the resulting detections stop working almost as soon as they are deployed. Security teams describe this idea with the Pyramid of Pain. This project asks whether feeding a report into a knowledge-graph retrieval system, Microsoft GraphRAG, rather than a standard vector-similarity retrieval system (Naive RAG), produces detection plans that rely more on these durable, top-of-pyramid clues. Both systems are given the same report, the same generation instructions, and the same language model to write the final plan; only the retrieval step differs. In a detailed case study of one APT28 report, the GraphRAG plan kept firing at 100\% of its detections after every IP address, domain, and file hash in the report was rotated, while the Naive RAG plan kept firing at only 29\%. Repeating the comparison across nine real CTI reports from four vendors confirms the same pattern: GraphRAG plans consistently reach higher, harder-to-evade levels of the pyramid, even when the two systems end up close on total score. The results support treating knowledge-graph-aware retrieval as the architecturally correct foundation for automatically generating SOC-deployable hunting plans, while showing that the wording of the generation prompt matters almost as much as the retrieval back-end itself.

</details>


### [274] [A Commitment-Based Hybrid Post-Quantum Cryptographic Model for Multi-File Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13138)
*Lemdi Frank Prikutse,Regina Esi Turkson,Alimatu-Saadia Yussiff,Abdul-Lateef Yussiff,Maame G. Asante-Mensah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于承诺（commitment）的混合后量子认证模型，通过SHA3-256批量承诺将所有密文绑定为单一摘要并仅签名一次，将每批后量子签名调用次数从n次降为1次，在n=1000时签署阶段时间分别减少629、606和725倍（针对100KB、1MB、10MB文件）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云存储客户端需要面对未来量子攻击者仍安全的认证方案，因此催生了经典密码原语与标准化后量子替代方案相结合的混合构造。然而，当这种构造朴素地扩展到多文件上传时，每个文件都需要一次基于格的签名，其开销主导认证时间，在实际批量规模下变得不可承受。

Method: 提出基于承诺的混合后量子模型，包含AES-256-GCM批量加密、X25519与ML-KEM-768混合密钥封装机制、以及Ed25519与ML-DSA-65混合双签名；所有密文先经SHA3-256批量承诺绑定为单一固定大小摘要，再对该摘要仅进行一次签名；剩余的加密与哈希操作受快速对称吞吐量约束。

Result: 在商品化客户端平台上，20次重复取平均，该模型使签署阶段时间在批量增长时保持近似恒定，而逐文件基线呈线性扩展。在n=1000时，相对逐文件双签名基线（共享其他所有原语），100KB、1MB、10MB文件的签署阶段时间分别减少629、606和725倍。

Conclusion: 承诺式批量聚合签名策略有效消除了批量上传中后量子签名次数随文件数线性增长的瓶颈，使认证效率不随批量规模退化，为大规模云存储的后量子安全认证提供了可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Cloud storage clients increasingly require authentication that remains secure against future quantum-capable adversaries, motivating hybrid constructions that combine classical primitives with standardized post-quantum alternatives. Extended naively to multi-file upload, such constructions incur a per-file lattice signing cost that dominates authentication time and becomes prohibitive at realistic batch sizes. This paper presents a commitment-based hybrid post-quantum model that addresses this bottleneck. It comprises AES-256-GCM bulk encryption, a hybrid X25519 with ML-KEM-768 key encapsulation mechanism, and a hybrid Ed25519 with ML-DSA-65 dual signature, computed over a SHA3-256 batch commitment. The commitment binds all ciphertexts in a batch to a single fixed-size digest that is signed once, reducing the number of post-quantum signature invocations per batch from n to one, independent of batch size; the remaining encryption and hashing is bounded by fast symmetric throughput. On a commodity client platform, averaged over 20 repetitions, this holds signing-phase time near-constant as the batch grows while the per-file baseline scales linearly. At n = 1000, the model reduces signing-phase time by factors of 629, 606, and 725 for 100 KB, 1 MB, and 10 MB files respectively, against a per-file dual-signing baseline sharing every other primitive.

</details>


### [275] [Smart Contract Invariants Protect Against Cybercriminals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13191)
*Sofia Bobadilla,Humaira Afrin,Angela Novelli,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出程序不变量是抵御区块链智能合约攻击的最有力方案，并通过实验工具链（INVARIANTEVAL基准测试和PONDEREPLAY回放框架）验证了不变量可以阻止全部28个真实以太坊漏洞利用攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链是计算领域中最具对抗性的环境，数十亿美元被盗，但目前没有任何概念或技术能真正有效解决智能合约安全问题。作者认为经典的程序不变量概念可能是解决该问题的最有力方法。

Method: 设计了一个原创的实验协议：1) 研究不变量如何抵御过去的真实世界攻击；2) 检验最先进的自动化工具能否找到这些不变量。构建了INVARIANTEVAL基准（包含28个真实以太坊漏洞利用，每个配有人工编写且能阻止攻击的不变量），并通过PONDEREPLAY回放框架重新执行交易以证明智能合约不变量的正确性和可靠性。

Result: 智能合约不变量阻止了INVARIANTEVAL中所有的网络犯罪攻击，并通过回放108,637笔历史交易得到完全验证。大规模实验清楚证明智能合约不变量能够有效防范网络犯罪分子。

Conclusion: 智能合约不变量是一种有效的安全防护手段，能够阻止真实世界中的所有已记录攻击，为区块链安全领域提供了有力的解决方案。

Abstract: Blockchains are among the most adversarial environments in computing. Billions are stolen by cybercriminals who exploit vulnerabilities. This is an open problem and no concept or technique has proven to really make a difference. In this paper, we claim that the classical notion of program invariant is perhaps the most powerful solution to the problem. We devise anoriginal experimental protocol to 1) study how invariants would have protected against past real-world attacks and 2) whether state-of-the-art automated tools can find them. The experimental toolchain is sophisticated. It is based on INVARIANTEVAL, a benchmark of 28 real Ethereum exploits, each paired with a human-authored invariant that blocks the attack. We validate every invariant with PONDEREPLAY, a replay framework that re-executes transactions in order to prove the correctness and soundness of smart contract invariants. We demonstrate that smart contract invariants block all the cybercriminal attacks in INVARIANTEVAL, fully validated by replaying 108,637 historical transactions. Our large-scale experiments clearly demonstrate that smart contract invariants protect against cybercriminals.

</details>


### [276] [Slow and Steady: Preventing MEV with Verifiable Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13271)
*Zeta Avarikioti,Dimitris Karakostas,Karl Kreder,Shreekara Shastry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对分布式账本中最大可提取价值（MEV）机会的防御机制，通过强制引入可验证延迟来阻止区块创建者利用MEV机会。作者在拜占庭容错模型和博弈论理性参与者模型中均给出了正面结果，同时提出了该防御路线的局限性理论边界，并基于历史MEV数据证明了该机制在实际部署中能够有效抵御大多数现有MEV威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV（最大可提取价值）是分布式账本中的一种系统性风险，允许区块创建者通过重新排序、插入或删除交易来提取额外价值，损害用户权益并威胁网络去中心化。现有防御手段（如公平排序协议、混淆交易）往往以牺牲性能或可组合性为代价。作者希望设计一种既能有效抑制MEV机会又不破坏系统活性（liveness）的防御机制。

Method: 核心思路是在交易生成阶段强制施加可验证延迟（Verifiable Delay, VDD），使得区块创建者无法在MEV机会出现时迅速反应——即使检测到机会，也会因时间延迟的约束而违反活性要求。作者分别在拜占庭容错模型与博弈论理性模型下分析该机制的安全性，并通过理论推导给出防御能力的上下界。此外，利用历史MEV数据对真实部署场景下的防护效果进行了实证评估。

Result: 在拜占庭设置和理性参与者博弈模型中均获得正面结果，证明该机制在多种攻击模型下有效；同时给出了该防御路线的负面理论边界，表明其存在固有局限性；基于历史MEV数据的实证分析显示该机制可实际抵御大多数现有MEV威胁。

Conclusion: 可验证延迟是一种实用且有效的MEV防御手段，能够在保持系统活性的前提下显著削弱区块创建者利用MEV机会的能力。尽管存在理论上的局限性边界，但结合真实数据验证，该机制具备现实可部署性。

Abstract: Our work presents a defense mechanism against Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) opportunities in distributed ledgers. The mechanism relies on the idea of enforcing a verifiable delay when generating transactions, such that a block creator cannot react to the appearance of a MEV opportunity without breaking liveness. We present positive results both in the Byzantine setting and in a game theoretic model of rational participants. We additionally present negative bounds that outline the limitations of this line of defense. Finally, we explore real-world implementation details of verifiable delays and show that, based on historical MEV data, our mechanism could realistically help prevent most existing MEV threats.

</details>


### [277] [VR-Themis: A Scalable Framework for Virtual Reality Application Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13290)
*Gengyang Xu,Hanyang Guo,Hong-Ning Dai,Weizhi Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VR-Themis，一个基于Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour (HOB)指标的两阶段VR应用克隆检测框架，通过粗粒度聚类和细粒度相似度分析，在4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用且无误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用重打包（应用克隆）威胁用户安全隐私并侵犯开发者版权。现有检测方法主要针对Android等传统移动平台，未能捕捉虚拟现实（VR）应用的独有特征，无法有效检测VR市场中的克隆应用。

Method: 提出两阶段克隆检测框架VR-Themis，基于Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour (HOB)模型。第一阶段为粗粒度阶段，利用可检索的统计特征对应用进行聚类，确保框架可扩展到大规模VR应用数据集；第二阶段为细粒度阶段，对第一阶段筛选出的可疑应用进行深入分析，通过自定义的HOB指标计算相似度。

Result: 在收集的4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用，且无误报（零假阳性），验证了该方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: VR-Themis结合两阶段检测策略和VR特有的HOB特征，能有效且可扩展地检测VR应用克隆问题，弥补了现有方法在VR领域的不足。

Abstract: Repackaging of mobile applications (aka app cloning) not only threatens the security and privacy of mobile users but also infringes upon the copyright of the original app developers. However, existing detection methods that primarily focus on mobile platforms (such as Android) fail to capture the essential features of virtual reality (VR). Consequently, they are inadequate for effectively detecting cloned VR apps, which have often been targeted by illegal users in the VR market. Considering the unique features of VR apps, this paper proposes a two-stage app clone detection framework, namely VR-Themis, based on \emph{Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour} (HOB). Firstly, VR-Themis exploits the coarse-grained stage to cluster apps based on their retrievable statistical features, making this tool scalable to large-scale VR app datasets. Then, in the fine-grained stage, VR-Themis performs in-depth analysis of the suspicious apps (identified in the first stage) by calculating similarity using our defined \emph{HOB metrics}. Our extensive experiments indicate that VR-Themis successfully detects 307 suspected clone apps from the collected 4,277 VR apps without false positives, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [278] [TeleGapper: On the (un)reliability of Privacy Policies in Telegram Mini apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13390)
*Luca Ferrari,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TeleGapper，一个针对Telegram Mini Apps的黑盒动态分析框架，通过捕获运行时网络流量、识别第三方通信并比对隐私披露，评估了278个Mini App的隐私态势。研究发现59.4%的应用联系了未披露的第三方，78.8%仅依赖Telegram默认隐私政策，且没有任何应用提供同意或选择退出机制，揭示了该生态系统中严重的透明度与合规性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps作为嵌入在Telegram客户端中的Web应用，用户规模庞大且被广泛采用，但其隐私属性尚未被深入探索。与WeChat等采用严格受控专有执行框架的生态不同，Telegram的Mini Apps运行在WebView中，结合了平台上下文、标准Web能力以及不受限制的出站网络连接，使得敏感信息可以通过普通Web请求传输给分析、广告、追踪等第三方，且可视性有限，因此亟需系统性评估其隐私态势。

Method: 本文提出TeleGapper，一个黑盒动态分析框架，通过三个步骤评估Mini Apps的隐私态势：(1)捕获运行时网络流量；(2)识别第三方通信；(3)将观察到的数据流与披露的隐私信息进行比对。研究从tApps Center（社区驱动的Telegram第三方应用目录）收集了278个可用的Mini Apps作为评估样本。

Result: 研究发现：59.4%的Mini Apps联系了至少一个未披露的第三方；78.8%完全依赖Telegram的默认隐私政策；没有任何一个应用提供同意或选择退出（opt-out）机制。这些结果揭示了该生态系统中存在实质性的透明度和合规性差距。

Conclusion: Telegram Mini Apps生态虽然在用户中广泛使用，但其隐私保护机制存在显著缺陷：大多数应用未主动披露第三方数据共享行为，普遍依赖平台默认隐私政策，且缺乏用户选择权。这一发现暴露了Telegram平台在Mini App隐私监管方面的重大透明度与合规性缺口，需要平台方和开发者共同改进。

Abstract: Telegram Mini Apps are Web applications embedded within the Telegram client, forming an ecosystem of third-party services within one of the world's most widely used messaging platforms. Despite their growing adoption and access to Telegram-provided context, their privacy properties remain largely unexplored. Unlike ecosystems such as WeChat, which rely on tightly controlled, proprietary execution frameworks, Telegram adopts a different model: Mini Apps run inside a WebView, combining platform-provided context with standard Web capabilities and unrestricted outbound networking. This enables applications to transmit sensitive information to analytics, advertising, tracking, or other third parties through ordinary Web requests, often with limited visibility.
  Privacy disclosures are therefore critical for transparency. Telegram allows Mini Apps either to define an application-specific privacy policy or to rely on a platform-provided default policy. While the latter reduces the developer's disclosure burden, it may lead to generic statements that do not accurately capture actual data practices of individual Mini Apps.
  In this paper, we present TeleGapper, a black-box dynamic analysis framework to assess the privacy posture of Mini Apps by capturing runtime network traffic, identifying third-party communications, and comparing observed data flows against disclosed privacy information. We evaluate 278 working Mini Apps collected from tApps Center, a community-driven catalogue for discovering third-party applications in Telegram. We find that 59.4% contact at least one undisclosed third party, 78.8% rely exclusively on Telegram's default privacy policy, and none provides a consent or opt-out mechanism. These findings expose a substantial transparency and compliance gap in a widely used yet understudied ecosystem.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [279] [RoboSynChallenge: Mastering Real-World Dexterity via Generalizing Synthesized Manipulation Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12416)
*Runyi Zhao,Ruixin Wu,Chengkun Li,Hongrui Zhang,Ang Li,Ruixing Jin,Yueci Deng,Yingying Guo,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Tianfan Xue,Yanjun Gao,Yudong Luo,Pascal Poupart,Simo Wu,Kui Jia,Wei-shi Zheng,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSynChallenge是一个统-的机器人操作泛化性基准，通过大规模合成数据生成与标准化真实世界评估相结合，推动跨任务、环境和难度等级的通用操作策略研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能领域虽然模型架构和学习算法进展迅速，但真实世界数据的稀缺性和狭隘多样性严重限制了机器人操作策略的泛化能力，需要一个统一基准来系统评估和推动泛化性研究。

Method: 该基准将大规模合成数据生成（synthesized state-action trials）与标准化真实世界机器人评估相结合；竞赛分为训练与评估两阶段，训练阶段利用合成数据，最终评估仅在未见过的真实操作环境中进行；提供Transformer、Diffusion、VLA（视觉-语言-动作）和World-Action-Model等基线实现以确保可复现性和可比性。

Result: 通过耦合可扩展的仿真训练与严格的真实世界验证，RoboSynChallenge旨在推动数据高效、广泛适用且可适应的操作系统的开发，为通用机器人智能铺路。

Conclusion: RoboSynChallenge通过"仿真训练+真实验证"的双阶段范式，为解决真实数据短缺与策略泛化难题提供了系统化解决方案，是通向通用机器人操作智能的重要一步。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable robotic manipulation remains a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Despite rapid advances in model architectures and learning algorithms, progress is often limited by the scarcity and narrow diversity of real-world data. The RoboSynChallenge competition introduces a unified benchmark to evaluate and advance the generalizability of manipulation policies across a spectrum of tasks, environments, and difficulty levels. To alleviate the shortage of realistic data, the challenge integrates large-scale synthetic data generation with standardized real-world robotic evaluation. Participants are encouraged to leverage synthesized state-action trials to improve general-purpose policy learning, while final assessments are conducted exclusively on unseen real-world manipulation environments. Baseline implementations, including Transformer-, Diffusion-, Vision-Language-Action, and World-Action-Model-based policies, are provided to ensure reproducibility and comparability. By coupling scalable simulation-based training with rigorous real-world validation, RoboSynChallenge aims to foster the development of broadly capable, data-efficient, and adaptable manipulation systems, thereby paving the way toward truly general robotic intelligence.

</details>


### [280] [Attune: A Self-Annotation Tool for Understanding Robot Operator Attention Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12650)
*Puqi Zhou,Sungsoo Ray Hong,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Attune，一个部署前的启发式工具，利用操作员眼球注视行为作为机器人行为设计线索，自动识别、标注并总结多机器人监督场景中的注意力特征，帮助设计人员校准机器人行为以捕捉、维持或释放操作员注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂真实环境中部署机器人集群需要操作员同时监督多台机器人，管理操作员注意力是多机器人监督界面设计的基本挑战。目前设计人员缺乏关于如何改变机器人行为以捕捉、维持或释放操作员注意力的实证指导，需要一种工具来帮助理解并校准机器人行为以适应不同操作员的注意力特征。

Method: 将操作员眼球注视视为机器人行为设计的线索，开发了部署前启发式工具Attune。Attune自动识别有意义的注视转移时刻、提供AI辅助用于标注注视转移原因，并输出操作员注视模式的摘要供操作员审查。通过用户研究让参与者标注吸引其注意力的视觉触发器来评估Attune。

Result: 研究发现操作员注视模式存在差异，并揭示了Attune如何帮助刻画操作员的注意力特征。

Conclusion: Attune能够有效帮助设计人员理解并表征操作员在多机器人监督任务中的注意力模式，为机器人行为设计提供实证指导依据。

Abstract: Deploying robot fleets in complex, real-world environments requires human operators to supervise multiple robots simultaneously. Managing operator attention is a fundamental challenge of designing multi-robot supervision interfaces, encompassing both feed layout and feed content (i.e., robot behavior design). Thus far, designers lack empirical guidance on the latter-how to change a robot's behavior to capture, sustain, or relinquish operator attention during multi-robot supervision. In our vision of the future, designers should be able to use this guidance to calibrate robot behavior to different operator attention profiles. Treating operator eye gaze as a robot behavior design clue, we created a pre-deployment elicitation tool called Attune. Attune automatically identifies when meaningful gaze shifts occur, provides AI assistance for annotating why shifts occurred, and outputs a summary of operator gaze patterns for operator review. We evaluated Attune through a user study in which participants annotated the visual triggers that drew their attention. Our findings unveil variation in observed gaze patterns and reveal how Attune helps characterize operator attention.

</details>


### [281] [FUSE: Active Functional Affordance Grounding through Adaptive Semantic-Geometric Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12683)
*Zhou Chen,Sathyanarayanan N. Aakur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出主动功能可供性定位（Active Functional Affordance Grounding）新任务，并开发FUSE框架，通过自适应语义-几何证据采集，结合显式不确定性驱动探索和学习的摊销规划器，高效选择信息丰富的视角来定位满足功能查询的物体。在Habitat基准上，FUSE实现了最高的非oracle定位性能，同时计算开销降低1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体常需根据物体的功能而非身份来识别和交互，但现有的可供性定位方法仅从固定视角工作，缺乏在功能线索被遮挡或不完整时决定下一步看哪里的机制，因此需要主动探索策略。

Method: FUSE框架结合两种互补策略：显式不确定性驱动的探索用于发现判别性功能证据，以及学习的摊销规划器用于高效选择信息丰富的视角。两者协同以平衡探索的充分性与计算效率。同时构建基于Habitat的基准来评估主动功能定位性能。

Result: 实验表明FUSE在非oracle设置下取得了最高的定位性能，相比完全显式探索方案计算开销降低1.33倍，且在不同可供性知识来源下均保持有效。

Conclusion: 主动视角选择对于鲁棒的功能可供性定位至关重要，FUSE通过融合显式与摊销探索策略在性能与效率之间取得了良好平衡，验证了其跨知识来源的通用性。

Abstract: Embodied agents must often identify and interact with objects based on their function rather than their identity, requiring them to actively acquire observations that reveal discriminative functional evidence. Existing affordance grounding methods operate from fixed viewpoints and lack mechanisms for deciding where to look when functional cues are occluded or incomplete. We introduce Active Functional Affordance Grounding, a new task in which an agent sequentially explores a scene to identify and spatially ground an object satisfying a functional query. To address this problem, we propose FUSE, an adaptive semantic-geometric evidence acquisition framework that combines explicit uncertainty-driven exploration with a learned amortized planner to efficiently select informative viewpoints. We further introduce a Habitat-based benchmark for evaluating active functional grounding. Experiments show that FUSE achieves the highest observed non-oracle grounding performance while reducing computation by 1.33x relative to fully explicit exploration, and remains effective across multiple affordance knowledge sources.

</details>


### [282] [SAP-Nav: Spatial Semantic Representation Meets Active Perception for Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12707)
*Xuetong Pei,Jian Liu,Vidura Munasinghe,Bo Miao,U-Xuan Tan,Wenrui Ding,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAP-Nav，一个全在线、零样本的主动感知框架，用于解决层级开放词汇目标导航问题。它通过增量构建可查询的空间语义表示和主动视点验证机制，在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上取得最优性能，并在真实机器人实验中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级开放词汇目标导航（OVON）要求智能体在未知环境中根据自由形式的指令（可能涉及场景、房间、区域、实例级别的线索）找到目标。尽管已有工作LangMap首次形式化了该设定，但在部分观测条件下可靠解决仍具挑战性：空间定位需要持续的全局环境证据，而目标验证需要清晰且具有判别力的候选视图。

Method: SAP-Nav包含两个核心组件：(1) 增量构建可查询的空间语义表示（Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation），从主动获取的房间视图中提取信息，使得智能体能够在任意已探索位置进行空间语义查询；(2) 主动视点验证（Active Viewpoint Verification），评估当前观测是否提供足够证据，若证据不足则将智能体重新定位到更具信息量的视点，再依据类别和属性约束对候选目标进行验证。该方法无需任务特定训练或预计算的场景地图。

Result: 在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上的实验表明，SAP-Nav取得了整体最优性能，其中在区域级导航上相较于基于训练的方法取得了12.2%的成功率提升。真实机器人实验进一步验证了该方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: SAP-Nav通过主动感知策略同时解决了层级OVON中对空间证据持久性和目标验证判别性的双重需求，是一个统一支持层级和标准类别级OVON的零样本、全在线框架，展示了良好的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Hierarchical open-vocabulary object navigation (OVON) requires agents to follow free-form instructions that may specify targets through scene-, room-, region-, and instance-level cues in unseen environments. Although recent work LangMap has formalized this setting, reliably solving it under partial observations remains challenging: spatial grounding requires persistent environment-level evidence, whereas target verification requires clear and discriminative candidate views. We present SAP-Nav, a fully online, zero-shot framework that addresses both requirements through active perception. SAP-Nav incrementally constructs a Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation from actively acquired room views, enabling spatial semantic queries from any explored location. It further employs Active Viewpoint Verification to assess whether the current observation provides sufficient evidence and, when necessary, reposition the agent to a more informative viewpoint before verifying candidates against category and attribute constraints. Although designed for hierarchical OVON, SAP-Nav supports both hierarchical and standard category-level OVON without task-specific training or precomputed scene maps. Experiments on LangMap and HM3D-OVON show that SAP-Nav achieves the overall best performance, including a 12.2% improvement in SR over training-based methods on region-level navigation. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate its practical feasibility. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [283] [Genetic Fuzzy System-Based Multi-Robot Coordination for Planetary Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12755)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于遗传模糊系统的多机器人系统（MRS）分散式协作搬运方法，通过地形坡度可达性分析将环境转换为二维可通行地图，并利用遗传算法优化模糊推理系统以生成搬运速度，从而在非结构化环境中最小化总路径长度并避开障碍物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统（MRS）在非结构化环境中执行协作物体搬运任务时，面临路径总长度最小化和避障的双重挑战，需要一种能够在复杂地形中有效决策的分散式控制方法。

Method: 该方法包括：1）基于高程图进行地形坡度可达性分析，降维并将不可通行区域视为障碍物，生成二维可通行地图；2）设计模糊推理系统（FISs）生成MRS搬运物体至目标位置的速度；3）使用遗传算法在多种场景（局部极小值、目标邻近障碍物、杂乱环境）下对FIS进行训练优化；4）将训练好的FIS模型应用于可通行地图测试环境并进行多场景验证。

Result: 训练后的模糊推理系统模型在测试环境（转换后的可通行地图）中通过多个场景进行了验证，表明该方法能够有效实现协作搬运和避障。

Conclusion: 遗传模糊系统方法能够在非结构化环境中实现多机器人系统的分散式协作搬运任务，有效最小化总路径长度并避开障碍物。

Abstract: This paper proposes a decentralized approach for a multi-robot system (MRS) using a genetic fuzzy system to perform a collaborative object transportation task that minimizes the total path length of the MRS in unstructured environment while avoiding obstacles. For an environment given by an elevation map, terrain traversability analysis with respect to the slope is performed to reduce the dimension and identify non-traversable areas that can be considered as obstacles, and the given map is converted into a traversability map in two dimensional space. In the training process, proposed fuzzy inference systems (FISs) to generate the MRS's velocity for transporting an object to a target position are optimized by a genetic algorithm with several scenarios, such as a local minima, a target that is close to an obstacle, and a cluttered environment. The trained FIS models are applied to the testing environment, which is the converted traversability map, and validated using multiple scenarios.

</details>


### [284] [AirForesight: Current-to-Future Spatial Map Imagination with Cross-Space Planning Consistency for UAV-VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12835)
*Yutong Liu,Xiaojie Li,Mingzhu Xu,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AirForesight框架，通过"当前-未来"空间地图想象机制解决无人机视觉-语言导航中的场景感知和未来空间推理问题，在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法大多将视觉-语言输入直接映射到动作，缺乏显式的场景锚定（scene grounding）和面向未来的空间推理能力，导致在复杂室外环境中导航性能受限。

Method: AirForesight首先从多视角观测中学习结构化的当前地图表示，该表示通过当前地图重建和未来轨迹预测进行联合监督；在结构化因果注意力下，当前空间知识被传播到未来地图推理中，聚合当前与未来表示以预测下一个3D路径点；引入跨空间规划一致性损失，促进地图空间轨迹与专家动作方向的对齐。

Result: 在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S数据集上的实验以及大量消融研究表明，该框架取得强性能，验证了其有效性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模当前与未来空间地图并利用因果注意力传播空间知识，AirForesight为UAV-VLN提供了更具场景感知和未来预测能力的导航方案。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to follow language instructions, infer spatial structure from sparse multi-view observations, and execute feasible 3D motion in complex outdoor environments. Despite recent progress with large language models, most existing methods still map vision-language inputs directly to actions, providing limited explicit scene grounding and future-aware spatial reasoning. We propose AirForesight, a current-to-future spatial map imagination framework for UAV-VLN. AirForesight first learns a structured current-map representation from multi-view observations. This representation is jointly supervised by current-map reconstruction and future-trajectory prediction, encouraging it to encode both present scene structure and future motion intent. Under structured causal attention, the current spatial knowledge is propagated to future-map reasoning, and the resulting current and future representations are aggregated to predict the next 3D waypoint. To make spatial imagination more relevant to navigation, we introduce a cross-space planning consistency loss that encourages directional agreement between the predicted map-space trajectory and the expert action direction derived from the ground-truth waypoint displacement. Experiments on OpenUAV and AerialVLN-S, together with extensive ablations, demonstrate strong performance and support the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [285] [ASPIRE-VINS: Adaptive Spline-based Visual-inertial Navigation System With Robust 3D Measurement Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12840)
*Kwangyik Jung,Eungchang Mason Lee,Taekjun Oh,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ASPIRE-VINS，一种自适应连续时间视觉惯性导航系统，结合自适应节点放置（AKP）、多分辨率样条（MRS）和3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR），在多样运动与传感条件下取得具有竞争性或更低的轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉惯性导航系统多采用基于关键帧的离散时间方法，虽然精度和效率良好，但在残差需在任意时间戳评估或需要依赖运动的时变分辨率时灵活性不足。连续时间样条虽能解决这一问题，但均匀分布的节点在快速动态时表示不足，在静态区间则过参数化，需要运动自适应的节点分配策略。

Method: ASPIRE-VINS包含三个核心组件：1）自适应节点放置（AKP），根据局部运动变化分配节点位置；2）多分辨率样条（MRS），在切空间中添加有界局部细化；3）3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR），通过对齐变换后的特征与标定观测射线实现3D测量空间中的方位一致性约束。

Result: 实验表明，ASPIRE-VINS在多种运动和传感条件下相比基线方法取得具有竞争力或更低的轨迹误差，验证了运动自适应连续时间轨迹建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 运动自适应的连续时间轨迹建模方法在视觉惯性导航中有效，AKP、MRS和3D-MSR三者协同能够在保持连续时间轨迹表征灵活性的同时，避免均匀节点分布带来的精度损失和参数冗余。

Abstract: Visual-inertial navigation systems estimate six-degree-of-freedom motion by fusing visual and inertial data. Modern discrete-time methods with IMU preintegration provide strong accuracy and efficiency, but keyframe-based representations can be less flexible when residuals must be evaluated at arbitrary timestamps or when motion-dependent temporal resolution is needed. Continuous-time splines address this issue by representing the trajectory as a smooth temporal function, but uniformly spaced knots can under-represent rapid dynamics or over-parameterize static intervals. This letter proposes ASPIRE-VINS, a continuous-time VINS framework that combines adaptive knot placement (AKP), multi-resolution splines (MRS), and 3D measurement-space residuals (3D-MSR). AKP allocates knots according to local motion variation, MRS adds bounded local refinement in tangent space, and 3D-MSR provides bearing consistency by aligning transformed features with calibrated observation rays in 3D measurement space. Experiments show that ASPIRE-VINS achieves competitive or lower trajectory errors than the compared baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of motion-adaptive continuous-time trajectory modeling under diverse motion and sensing conditions.

</details>


### [286] [BrainWAM: Action-Space Coordination of Semantic Priors and Predictive Dynamics for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12854)
*Bing Zhan,Shuyao Shang,Jiahao Gu,Shuo Lu,Yuan Xu,Zhao Wang,Yida Wang,Xueyang Zhang,Kun Zhan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrainWAM提出了一种结构化动作空间协调框架，将语义推理（VLA）与预测世界建模（WAM）整合到统一的自动驾驶规划器，通过专用动作通路和异步整流流推断策略，在NAVSIM v1和v2上达到最先进性能（89.5 PDMS / 89.6 EPDMS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要在语义约束和预测动力学下进行规划，但现有端到端方法（VLA和WAM）各自只强调一方面。简单的token级联合注意力组合会导致注意力分配不匹配，语义捷径主导共享注意力空间并抑制预测动力学，因此需要一种既能利用语义先验又能利用预测动力学的统一规划框架。

Method: 1) 受神经科学中功能特化系统协调产生复杂行为的启发，提出BrainWAM结构化动作空间协调框架，将语义推理和预测世界建模转化为两个专用的面向动作的通路，并在紧凑动作表示级别对齐两者；2) 引入异步整流流推断策略，将视频和动作去噪解耦，缩短推理延迟同时保留规划相关的预测上下文。

Result: BrainWAM在NAVSIM v1上达到89.5 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到89.6 EPDMS，均取得最先进性能，一致优于仅用VLA或仅用WAM的方法。

Conclusion: BrainWAM验证了结构化动作空间协调框架的有效性，表明通过功能特化通路与异步去噪推断，可以同时发挥语义推理和预测动力学的优势，为自动驾驶系统提供了实际且具有前景的方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires planning under both semantic constraints and predictive dynamics. Existing end-to-end driving approaches, however, typically emphasize only one side of this requirement: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exploit VLM priors for semantic reasoning, while World Action Models (WAMs) provide future-aware prediction through generative world modeling. This naturally motivates a unified planner that can leverage both semantic priors and predictive dynamics. However, we find that a naive combination through joint token-level attention suffers from an attention-allocation mismatch, where semantic shortcuts dominate the shared attention space and suppress predictive dynamics. Inspired by neuroscience evidence that complex behavior arises from coordination among functionally specialized systems, we propose BrainWAM, a structured action-space coordination framework that converts semantic reasoning and predictive world modeling into two specialized action-oriented pathways, and aligns them at the level of compact action representations. We further introduce an asynchronous rectified-flow inference strategy with decoupled video and action denoising, which shortens inference latency while preserving planning-relevant predictive context. BrainWAM reaches state-of-the-art performance on both NAVSIM v1 (89.5 PDMS) and NAVSIM v2 (89.6 EPDMS), consistently outperforming VLA-only or WAM-only methods, highlighting BrainWAM as a practical and promising direction for autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [287] [HumanoidVLN: A Physics-Grounded Simulator and Benchmark for Vision-Language Navigation Across Diverse Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12860)
*Quan-Dung Pham,Anh Dao,The-Anh Nguyen,Minh Nguyen-Dinh,Phuong Nam Dang,Tri Pham,Hung Tran,Bach Dao,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HumanoidVLN，一个基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建的物理仿真VLN平台与基准，支持多种人形机器人构型，提供碰撞感知的参考episode和细粒度指令数据，并通过仿真与真实实验验证了跨构型VLN性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准未考虑人形机器人的双足运动物理约束、跨平台形态差异以及运动引起的视觉观测畸变，需要新的物理仿真平台与基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建仿真平台，采用分层控制栈（强化学习步态策略+PD或MPC路径跟踪器）支持四种人形机器人；环境来自艺术家设计场景和3DGS重建；指令通过多智能体生成-评审-改写流水线配合人工验证生成；兼容NaVILA等主流VLN模型。

Result: JanusVLN在四种模型和四种构型中取得最高平均成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38；20集sim-to-real实验（DualVLN+Unitree G1）中导航误差与仿真强相关（r=0.935），平均绝对误差0.68m，nDTW相似度0.782±0.188。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了VLN模型、控制器与人形机器人构型在物理执行下的相互影响，为面向人形机器人的VLN研究提供了平台、基准与数据资源。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) for humanoid robots poses challenges existing benchmarks fail to address: bipedal locomotion imposes physical constraints absent from wheeled agents, humanoid morphologies vary across platforms, and egocentric observations are distorted by locomotion-induced camera dynamics. We present HumanoidVLN, a physics-grounded simulator and benchmark for VLN across diverse humanoid embodiments. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim, our platform supports an extensible set of humanoid configurations, demonstrated on four robots (Unitree G1, Unitree H1, Internal-A, Internal-B) spanning 10-12 lower-body DoF and heights from 1.17m to 1.80m, via a hierarchical control stack combining a reinforcement learning locomotion policy with interchangeable PD or MPC path trackers. New robots and VLN models integrate with minimal effort; we demonstrate compatibility with NaVILA, DualVLN, StreamVLN, and JanusVLN. Environments are drawn from artist-designed scenes and 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions, filtered for navigable areas exceeding 100 square meters. Instructions are generated by a dual generator-reviewer plus paraphraser multi-agent pipeline with human-in-the-loop verification, yielding 933 collision-aware reference episodes, each paired with one fine-grained instruction and three coarse-grained stylistic variants (formal, natural, casual). Across four models and four embodiments, JanusVLN achieves the highest mean success rate of 43.55% and nDTW of 48.38. In a 20-episode sim-to-real pilot with DualVLN and the Unitree G1, navigation errors correlate strongly (r=0.935), with a mean absolute difference of 0.68m and mean trajectory similarity of 0.782 (+/-0.188) nDTW. These results highlight the interaction between VLN models, controllers, and humanoid embodiments under physical execution. Code, benchmark, and data will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-vln.github.io/.

</details>


### [288] [AMR-Pose: An Active LED Marker-Based Relative Pose Estimation Framework With Probabilistic Switching PnP for Cooperative AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12866)
*Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AMR-Pose，一个基于主动LED标记的自主水下机器人(AUV)相对位姿估计框架。该系统由红心蓝边的LED标记模块和概率切换PnP估计器(PSwPnP)组成，结合SE(3)上的Lie群位姿传播、概率标记关联和可见性自适应测量融合，在水下复杂条件下实现鲁棒的六自由度相对位姿估计。水箱实验和闭环主从实验验证了其准确性和实时可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV之间的可靠相对位姿估计对协同海洋探测、采样和多机器人协调至关重要，但水下环境中的光学退化（浊度、光照变化、反射、特征遮挡）使得基于视觉的相对定位极具挑战，现有方法在这些条件下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 1) 设计紧凑标记模块：1个红色中心LED加3个蓝色外围LED，安装在领航AUV上，提供水下复杂条件下的独特视觉特征；2) 提出概率切换PnP估计器(PSwPnP)：结合SE(3)上的Lie群位姿传播进行时间一致性约束，采用概率标记关联处理部分可见性，并通过可见性自适应测量融合动态调整估计过程，维持部分观测和可见性切换时的几何一致性与时间稳定性。

Result: 水箱实验中以动作捕捉系统为真值验证，AMR-Pose在挑战性水下条件下实现了准确、平滑且鲁棒的相对位姿估计；闭环主从实验进一步证明了其在合作水下机器人实时相对位姿反馈中的可行性。

Conclusion: AMR-Pose通过主动LED标记与概率切换PnP估计器的联合设计，有效解决了水下环境中的视觉相对定位难题，为实现AUV协同作业中的可靠相对位姿反馈提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reliable relative pose estimation between autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for cooperative ocean exploration, sampling, and multi-robot coordination. However, achieving robust vision-based relative localization in underwater environments remains challenging due to severe optical degradation, including turbidity, illumination variations, reflections, and intermittent feature occlusions. This paper presents AMR-Pose, an active LED marker-based relative pose estimation framework for cooperative AUVs. A compact marker module consisting of one red central LED and three blue peripheral LEDs is developed and integrated onto the leader AUV to provide distinctive visual features under complex underwater conditions. Building upon the detected marker observations, a probabilistic switching Perspective-n-Point estimator (PSwPnP) is developed by combining Lie-group pose propagation on $SE(3)$, probabilistic marker association, and visibility-adaptive measurement fusion for robust six-degree-of-freedom relative pose estimation. The proposed framework dynamically adapts the estimation process according to marker visibility, maintaining geometric consistency and temporal stability during partial observations and visibility transitions. Extensive water-tank experiments with motion-capture ground truth validate that AMR-Pose achieves accurate, smooth, and robust relative pose estimation under challenging underwater conditions. Closed-loop leader-follower experiments further demonstrate its feasibility for real-time relative pose feedback in cooperative underwater robotics.

</details>


### [289] [Temporal GRPO: Beyond Trajectory-Level Credit in Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13026)
*Yao Zhou,Hang Gao,Fengge Wu,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal GRPO方法，通过构建可检测的任务阶段、将轨迹与阶段特定动作区间对齐、仅比较进入同一阶段的轨迹，解决VLA后训练中GRPO的轨迹级信用混淆问题，在RoboTwin 2.0和LIBERO-Long上取得一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于GRPO的VLA后训练中，单个rollout级别的优势值被应用于轨迹中的每个动作。一个完成多个有效阶段但后期失败的rollout会错误地惩罚其早期进步阶段的动作，造成轨迹级信用混淆（trajectory-level credit aliasing），限制了稀疏任务成功反馈下的学习效率。

Method: Temporal GRPO通过三个关键步骤解决信用混淆：1）构建可检测的任务阶段；2）将每个rollout与阶段特定的动作区间对齐；3）仅比较进入同一阶段的rollouts，计算阶段级优势值并在单次策略更新中应用于对应动作区间。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上，Temporal GRPO显著提升了任务成功率和样本效率，在不同任务长度上表现一致；在LIBERO-Long上的受控更新实验表明，该方法能保留共享前提阶段，并将改进集中在rollout结果分化出现的首个阶段。

Conclusion: Temporal GRPO通过细粒度的阶段级信用分配有效缓解了轨迹级信用混淆问题，为VLA后训练提供了更精确和高效的强化学习范式，尤其在长视野任务中展现出明显优势。

Abstract: Outcome-driven reinforcement learning offers a scalable way to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) policies from sparse task-success feedback. In common GRPO-based VLA post-training, one rollout-level advantage is applied to every action in the trajectory. A rollout that completes several valid stages but fails later can therefore penalize the actions that produced its earlier progress. We call this trajectory-level credit aliasing. Temporal GRPO addresses this problem by constructing detectable task stages, aligning each rollout with stage-specific action intervals, and comparing only rollouts that have entered the same stage. The resulting stage advantages are applied to their corresponding intervals in a single policy update. On RoboTwin 2.0, Temporal GRPO improves task success and sample efficiency, with consistent gains across task horizons. Controlled updates on LIBERO-Long preserve shared prerequisite stages and concentrate improvement at the first stage where rollout outcomes diverge.

</details>


### [290] [H2R-Bench: Benchmarking Human-to-Robot Manipulation Video Generation in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13049)
*Dingyi Rong,Yue Shi,Chaofan Ma,Jiezhang Cao,Zongrui Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai,Ning Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出H2R-Bench，一个用于评估跨具身（从人到机器人）操作视频生成能力的基准测试。该基准将人类第一人称演示视频转化为指定机器人具身下的操作视频，从五个维度评估生成质量。实验表明，当前最先进的视频世界模型在跨具身操作迁移方面仍存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习依赖大规模操作数据，但真实机器人演示采集成本高昂且难以扩展。人类第一人称操作视频资源丰富，但跨具身迁移存在人手与机器人末端执行器之间的巨大差异。视频世界模型可能提供一条从人类观测合成机器人视角操作视频的路径，但其跨具身迁移能力尚未被系统评估。

Method: 构建H2R-Bench基准，包含人类演示视频、目标具身约束以及源锚定注释（涵盖任务目标、动作事件、功能接触和物体响应）。采用五个评估维度：目标状态完成度、动作事件完成度、功能接触迁移、具身正确性和视频整体质量。对六个操作族和两种机器人具身下的十一个先进视频生成模型进行系统评估。

Result: 发现当前视频世界模型在人类到机器人操作迁移中表现有限：即使领先模型也常在具身一致性、功能交互和任务执行方面失败。

Conclusion: H2R-Bench提供了一个系统性的诊断框架，用于评估视频世界模型能否弥合人类与机器人之间的具身鸿沟，并将人类操作观测转化为可用于机器人训练的具身资源。

Abstract: Large-scale manipulation data is essential for robot learning, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Meanwhile, abundant egocentric human manipulation videos provide rich behavioral experiences, but transferring them across embodiments remains challenging due to differences between human hands and robotic end-effectors. Recent advances in video world models offer a promising pathway to synthesize robot-centric manipulation videos from human observations, while their cross-embodiment transfer capability remains largely unexplored. Therefore, we introduce H2R-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-embodiment human-to-robot manipulation video generation, where models transform egocentric human demonstrations into robot manipulation videos under specified embodiments. Each benchmark instance contains a human demonstration video, target embodiment constraints, and source-grounded annotations covering task goals, action events, functional contacts, and object responses. H2R-Bench evaluates generated videos through five dimensions, including goal-state completion, action-event completion, functional contact transfer, embodiment correctness, and general video quality. We benchmark eleven state-of-the-art video generation models across six manipulation families and two robot embodiments. Our evaluation reveals that current video world models remain limited in human-to-robot manipulation transfer: even leading models often fail in embodiment consistency, functional interaction, and task execution. H2R-Bench provides a systematic diagnostic framework for evaluating whether video world models can bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap and convert human manipulation observations into robot-centric training resources.

</details>


### [291] [Semantic Radiance Fields as Simulators for Spatial Reasoning in Real-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13095)
*Nico Heider,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Katarzyna Wasielewska-Michniewska,Przemysław Hołda,Martin Schieck,Marcin Paprzycki,Maria Ganzha,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为具身智能体空间推理的模拟器，将预训练视觉模型的2D语义分割提升到3D辐射场中，统一编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份，从真实场景的RGB图像重建，支持新视图合成、语义和自由空间查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体空间推理的训练与评估环境存在两难：合成模拟器具有真实语义标注但缺乏视觉真实感，基于真实世界重建的模拟器外观真实但缺少语义标注，无法同时满足几何保真与语义可查询的需求。

Method: 提出语义辐射场（SRF）作为空间推理智能体的模拟器，从带位姿的RGB图像中重建真实场景，将2D语义分割结果提升至3D辐射场，联合编码几何、外观和语义身份，支持新视图合成、语义查询和自由空间查询。

Result: 以一个果园苹果采摘任务为例，展示了SRF驱动的模拟器：辐射场为物理引擎提供相机渲染、语义真值和占用查询，支持高效生成多样化的真实世界环境来训练和评估空间推理模型。

Conclusion: SRF统一了视觉真实性与语义可查询性，为具身智能体提供了既能保持真实场景几何外观、又能提供ground truth语义标注的模拟方案。

Abstract: Training and evaluating spatial reasoning in embodied agents requires diverse environments that are both geometrically faithful and semantically queryable. Synthetic simulators offer ground truth semantics but sacrifice realism; simulators based on reconstructions of real-world environments have realistic appearance but lack ground truth semantics by default. We propose using Semantic Radiance Fields (SRF) as simulators for spatial reasoning agents. SRFs are a representation that unifies these requirements by lifting 2D semantic segmentations from pretrained vision models into a 3D radiance field that jointly encodes geometry, appearance, and per-class semantic identity. The resulting fields are reconstructed from posed RGB captures of real scenes and support novel-view synthesis, semantic and free-space queries within a single grounded representation. This enables the efficient generation of diverse real-world environments to train and evaluate spatial reasoning models. As an example application, we outline an SRF-driven simulator for an orchard apple-reaching task, in which the radiance field supplies camera rendering, semantic ground truth, and occupancy queries to a physics engine.

</details>


### [292] [S2-HWM: Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model for Long-Horizon Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13103)
*Shuzhe Zhang,Xin Zhu,Yinling Qian,Qiong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: S2-HWM是一种稀疏事件结构化的分层世界模型，通过从原始潜轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据来协调事件级管理器和原始步骤工作者，解决长视野手术机器人操作中的稀疏奖励和可变持续时间问题。在SurRoL PegTransfer任务上，S2-HWM达到98.7%的成功率，比平面基线GAS DreamerV3高出22.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野手术机器人操作面临两个关键挑战：任务奖励稀疏，且有意义的交互变化发生在不规则的间隔。现有世界模型代理通常以原始步骤分辨率进行想象，隐含地忽略了可变持续时间的任务进展。虽然手动指定的阶段可以提供中间结构，但其特定任务边界难以与状态依赖的交互转换对齐。

Method: S2-HWM从原始潜轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据，用于调度事件级管理器（manager）的目标更新，并条件化原始步骤工作者（worker）的动作。学习的事件证据还构成可变持续时间段，用于事件转换模型（ETM），该模型预测下一边界随机状态、段持续时间和累积段奖励。通过链式事件级预测提供超出原始想象范围的可变持续时间延展，同时工作者保留原始步骤的actor-critic学习。

Result: 在基于SurRoL的PegTransfer任务上，S2-HWM实现98.7%的成功率，比平面GAS DreamerV3基线高出22.7个百分点。

Conclusion: S2-HWM通过自动学习稀疏事件边界而非依赖手动定义的任务阶段，有效处理了长视野手术操作中的稀疏奖励和可变持续时间交互问题，显著优于平面世界模型基线。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical robot manipulation is challenging because task rewards are sparse, while meaningful interaction changes occur at irregular intervals. Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit. Manually specified stages can provide intermediate structure, but their task specific boundaries are difficult to align with state-dependent interaction transitions. We propose S2-HWM, a Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model that learns sparse event evidence from primitive latent trajectories to coordinate an event-level manager and a primitive-step worker. The event evidence schedules manager goal updates, and each selected latent goal conditions the worker's primitive actions until the next update. The learned event evidence also forms variable-duration segments for an Event Transition Model (ETM), which predicts the next?boundary stochastic state, segment duration, and accumulated segment reward. Chaining these event-level predictions provides a variable-duration continuation beyond the primitive imagination horizon for manager learning, while the worker retains primitive-step actor-critic learning. On a SurRoL-based PegTransfer task, S2-HWM achieves a success rate of 98.7%, outperforming the flat GAS DreamerV3 baseline by 22.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [293] [FAM-DQ: A Dual-Quadrotor-Based Fully Actuated Aerial Manipulator for High-Torque Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13220)
*Xuwei Yang,Ruoyu Ren,Ziqian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAM-DQ是一种基于双四旋翼的完全驱动空中机械臂，通过将两个四旋翼推进模块安装在中央框架两端并使用被动关节与齿轮驱动舵机，实现了6自由度解耦末端控制、全向操作能力和高扭矩输出，最大扭矩1.019 N·m，扭矩质量比达2.28 N·m/kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中物理交互需要空中操纵平台产生大力和扭矩同时保持精确的末端执行器控制。然而，传统的欠驱动空中机械臂存在强位置-姿态耦合问题，而完全驱动平台设计常面临结构复杂、有效载荷有限和扭矩输出不足等挑战。

Method: 提出FAM-DQ方案：将两个四旋翼推进模块通过被动关节安装在中央框架两端，并利用齿轮驱动的舵机来调节指向方向，从而实现解耦的6自由度末端执行器控制，具备全向操纵能力和增强的扭矩输出。

Result: 通过轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量和螺丝驱动等实验验证了设计有效性。FAM-DQ在总质量0.447 kg条件下实现最大扭矩1.019 N·m，扭矩质量比达到2.28 N·m/kg。

Conclusion: FAM-DQ通过双四旋翼驱动的创新构型，在保持结构相对简单的同时实现了高扭矩输出和解耦的6自由度控制，为空中物理交互任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction requires aerial manipulation platforms to generate large interaction forces and torques while maintaining precise end-effector control. However, conventional underactuated aerial manipulators suffer from strong position-attitude coupling, whereas fully actuated platform designs often face structural complexity, limited payload capacity, and insufficient torque output. This paper presents FAM-DQ, a dual-quadrotor based fully actuated aerial manipulator designed for high-torque physical interaction tasks. By mounting two quadrotor propulsion modules at the ends of a central frame through passive joints, while using a gear-driven servo to regulate the pointing direction, FAM-DQ achieves decoupled $6$-DoF end-effector control with omnidirectional manipulation capability and enhanced torque output. Experiments including trajectory tracking, attitude tracking, static torque measurement, and screw driving validate the proposed design. FAM-DQ achieves a maximum torque of $1.019~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m}$ with a total mass of $0.447~\mathrm{kg}$, corresponding to a torque-to-mass ratio of $2.28~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m/kg}$.

</details>


### [294] [Manufacturing Complex Airtight Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: Process Evaluation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13233)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对多种软体气动执行器的制造工艺进行了系统评估，包括热缩成型、硅胶浇注、粉末基和液态基增材制造以及FDM等路线。研究发现FDM因主导缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步消除而最具适应性，并建立了以制造约束与执行器架构兼容性为导向的设计-制造准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造复杂软体气动执行器需要同时满足几何保真度、柔顺性、结构完整性和气密性，但现有制造路线各具局限，缺乏系统的制造导向评估来区分工艺固有限制与可修正制造缺陷。

Method: 通过工艺筛选、基线制造、失效分析和工艺改进四步框架，对热缩成型、硅胶浇注、粉末基/液态基增材制造及FDM等制造路线进行系统性比较评估，并分析气密性对壁厚和挤出路径架构的依赖关系。

Result: 热缩成型受几何贴合度限制，浇注受模具可达性和粘接界面制约，粉末基方法因密闭通道内残留粉末受限，数字光处理受材料属性和后处理要求限制；FDM因其主导缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步消除而被认定为最具适应性路线。气密性不仅依赖标称壁厚，还与挤出路径架构相关。

Conclusion: 建立了实用的设计-制造（Design for Manufacturing）方法，以执行器架构与制造约束之间的兼容性为导向进行工艺选择，为软体机器人领域开发复杂、柔顺且气密的软体气动执行器提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Manufacturing complex soft pneumatic actuators remains challenging because geometric fidelity, compliance, structural integrity, and airtightness must be achieved simultaneously. This study presents a manufacturing-focused evaluation of several fabrication routes for complex pneumatic structures, including heat-shrink forming, silicone casting, powder- and liquid-based additive manufacturing, and fused deposition modeling (FDM). The processes were assessed through process screening, baseline fabrication, failure analysis, and process improvement to distinguish inherent process limitations from correctable manufacturing defects. Heat-shrink forming was limited by geometric conformity, casting by mold accessibility and bonded interfaces, powder-based methods by residual material trapped within enclosed passages, and digital light processing by the material properties and post-processing requirements of the investigated system. FDM provided the most adaptable route because its dominant defects could be progressively reduced through process optimization. The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [295] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级模块化框架，在末端执行器速度指令层面实现主动碰撞规避，通过基于TTC的碰撞预测和Rodrigues旋转公式偏转相对速度矢量，不改变速度大小，避免了传统APF方法的死锁问题，在仿真和物理机器人实验中均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程操作中，人类操作员在面临突然出现的障碍物时（尤其在网络延迟或注意力受限条件下）可能无法及时有效地规避，存在安全隐患。现有方法缺乏实时、轻量级的碰撞规避方案。

Method: 在末端执行器速度指令层面操作，首先预处理点云数据，然后基于时间到碰撞（TTC）预测潜在碰撞（集成超调保护），随后利用Rodrigues旋转公式偏转相对速度矢量方向而保持其大小不变，从而缓解传统人工势场（APF）方法的死锁问题。预测模块补偿了复杂遥操作管线中由点云处理带来的延迟，轻量设计支持高频控制。

Result: 在多种场景下的仿真表明，所提方法的末端执行器碰撞规避率高于基线方法；物理机器人系统实验进一步验证了其碰撞规避的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法在速度指令层面实现了轻量、模块化的主动碰撞规避，兼顾了高频率控制需求与延迟补偿，有效提升了遥操作中应对突发障碍物的安全性。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [296] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NestDex是一个嵌套策略学习框架，通过学习的手部技能辅助灵巧操作演示数据收集，操作者仅需控制手臂并通过单自由度离合调节手部技能，内层手部策略根据本体感受历史自适应运动，视觉-语言选择器为每个任务阶段激活合适的技能，生成的演示训练独立的外层视觉运动策略，在部署时无需内层策略即可同时控制手臂和手。实验表明该方法提高了演示的可靠性和效率，支持有效的自主策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作学习受限于演示数据收集的困难——与平行夹爪操作不同，灵巧任务要求操作者在整个任务过程中协调手臂运动与精确的、接触丰富的手指行为，直接指定完整的手指轨迹负担极重，亟需降低演示收集负担的方法。

Method: NestDex采用嵌套策略框架：内层手部策略根据最新本体感受历史自适应调整手指运动，视觉-语言选择器为任务各阶段激活合适的技能；操作者只需控制手臂并通过单自由度离合（clutch）调节活动的手部技能，无需直接指定完整手指轨迹。手部动作变分自编码器提供紧凑的手部动作目标，同时保留关节空间的手臂指令。生成的演示用于训练独立的外层视觉运动策略，该策略在部署时无需内层策略即可同时控制手臂和手。

Result: 在真实世界的灵巧操作实验中，NestDex提高了演示收集的可靠性和效率，实证评估支持有效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: NestDex通过将灵巧操作演示收集的负担从全自由度手指控制转移到内层技能选择，显著降低了灵巧操作学习的数据收集难度，证明了嵌套策略框架在灵巧操作领域的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [297] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIRE-VLA提出了一种失败感知的自进化框架，通过从冻结的回合开始副本模型进行自我蒸馏，将GRPO（群体相对策略优化）无法解决的失败样本转化为特权监督信号，从而提升自动驾驶视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在强化学习中通过比较同批次采样轨迹的奖励差异来优化策略，但当所有采样轨迹都较差时，相对信号只能区分失败之间的优劣，无法提供失败区域之外的引导信号。这种"未解决的失败"缺乏有效的监督，限制了策略的进一步改进。

Method: FIRE-VLA包含以下关键设计：(1) 触发机制：低奖励、低多样性的组触发自我蒸馏；(2) 教师模型：使用冻结的回合开始策略副本，与学生的参数规模相同，但只有教师能观察到隐藏的未来轨迹；(3) 监督方式：监督遵循学生生成的前缀，且仅限制在答案token上；(4) 混合优化：GRPO对所有组保持激活，自我蒸馏作为补充；(5) 动态路由：更新后的策略作为下一轮的教师，使失败分布随策略演化而变化，无需更大的外部教师模型。

Result: 在相同的Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT检查点基础上，在6,019个样本（来自150个保留的nuScenes场景）上验证：(1) 保持可比的单样本规划性能；(2) G=4的均值L2误差从1.848降至1.500米；(3) 评估中持续失败的占比从13.03%降至11.20%。误差降低主要来源于减少罕见严重错误轨迹，而非普通轨迹的统一改善。

Conclusion: FIRE-VLA通过将失败信号转化为自蒸馏监督，有效地解决了GRPO在全体样本较差时缺乏引导信号的问题。该方法不依赖更大的外部教师，失败分布能随策略更新动态演化，实现了有效的自进化强化学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group. When all sampled trajectories are poor, this relative signal can rank failures without identifying behavior outside the failed region. We introduce FIRE-VLA, a failure-informed self-evolution framework that converts such unresolved failures into privileged supervision for the next policy. Low-reward, low-diversity groups trigger self-distillation from a frozen round-start copy of the same model. Teacher and student have the same parameter scale, but only the teacher observes the hidden future trajectory. Supervision follows the student's generated prefix and is restricted to answer tokens, while GRPO remains active for every group. The updated policy supplies the teacher for the next round, allowing the routed failure distribution to change with the policy without requiring a larger external teacher. Starting from the same Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT checkpoint, the comparison matches student rollout and policy-update counts. On 6,019 examples from 150 held-out nuScenes scenes, FIRE-VLA retains comparable single-sample planning, reduces G=4 mean L2 from 1.848 to 1.500 m, and lowers evaluation-persistent failure prevalence from 13.03% to 11.20%. The reduction in mean error arises mainly from rare severe rollouts rather than uniform improvement across ordinary trajectories.

</details>


### [298] [Capstan-driven Continuum Surgical Robot: Design, Modeling, and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13396)
*Gang Zhang,Yufu Qiu,Junyan Yan,Wenhui Zeng,Wenlong Lu,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"驱动-感知协同设计"（actuation-perception co-design）理念的集成式设计-建模-传感框架，通过在电机安装支架中引入柔性元件来实现缆绳张力实时测量，并利用基于多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架实现实时形状与力感知，最终在单段和双段缆绳驱动连续体手术机器人上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆绳驱动连续体手术机器人中，形状与力感知一直是关键瓶颈，主要困难在于在紧凑的绞盘组件内难以获取缆绳张力信息，这限制了机器人的感知能力与临床应用。

Method: 提出驱动-感知协同设计框架：(1) 在电机安装支架中引入柔性元件，使其在缆绳反作用力下产生微变形，在不占用紧凑绞盘空间的前提下实现实时缆绳张力测量；(2) 针对空间缆绳布线策略引入的非传统关节构型，提出基于多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架，捕捉短梁段的剪切效应及多缆协同交互，同时满足实时性要求；(3) 引入近端多轴力/力矩传感器作为额外测量锚点，实现稳定的形状与力感知。

Result: 在单段和双段连续体机器人上进行了实验验证，成功实现了实时末端位姿估计，以及接触力与接触位置的感知，证明了所提出框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架在不牺牲紧凑绞盘架构的前提下实现了缆绳张力反馈，使集成式感知在缆绳驱动连续体手术机器人中成为可行方案，为该类机器人的智能化与临床应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Shape and force sensing have long been critical bottlenecks in the development of compact capstan-driven continuum surgical robots, primarily due to the difficulty of obtaining cable tension information within the confined capstan assembly. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents an integrated design-modeling-sensing approach based on the concept of actuation-perception co-design. A compliant element is introduced into the motor mounting bracket of the drive system, enabling micro-deformation under the cable reaction force and thereby allowing real-time cable tension measurement without occupying the compact capstan space. To address the modeling complexity arising from unconventional joint configurations introduced by the spatial cable routing strategy, a parallel computation framework based on a multibody short-thick-beam model is proposed, which captures shear effects in short beam segments and synergistic multi-cable interactions while achieving real-time performance. Building on this framework, stable shape and force sensing is achieved by incorporating a proximal multi-axis force/torque sensor as an additional measurement anchor. Following this design-modeling-sensing framework, capstan-driven continuum surgical robots with single- and dual-segment configurations are developed. Experimental results validate the proposed framework in both single- and dual-segment continuum robots, demonstrating real-time tip pose estimation together with contact force and location perception. By enabling cable tension feedback without compromising the compact capstan architecture, the proposed framework makes integrated perception feasible for capstan-driven continuum surgical robots.

</details>


### [299] [Deliberate Practice: Learning Robot Skills under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13415)
*Shivam Vats,Sudarshan Harithas,Mete Tuluhan Akbulut,Arvind Raghunathan,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"刻意练习"(Deliberate Practice, DP)的主动技能学习算法，在有限的练习预算下，通过计算预算最优的技能练习分配方案，使机器人能够在长时间跨度的操作任务中最大化累积奖励并提升长期规划能力。该算法的核心贡献是将预算最优分配问题转化为一个双线性规划，可利用现成的求解器精确求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限的练习预算下自主学习机器人技能具有挑战性，因为需要在大量组合性的技能规划方案中做出最优选择。传统方法无法保证在给定预算内得到最优的技能练习分配策略。

Method: 提出Deliberate Practice (DP)算法，它同时估计掌握技能所需的时间以及技能所开启的任务规划带来的累积奖励，并证明能计算预算最优的分配方案。关键创新是将该组合优化问题转化为一个双线性规划(bilinear program)，从而可以使用现成的优化求解器精确求解。

Result: 通过模拟实验和真实世界的长时间跨度操作任务实验，证明了所提方法能使机器人最优地利用有限的练习时间获取有用的策略，并改进长期规划性能。

Conclusion: Deliberate Practice算法为受限预算下的机器人主动技能学习提供了可证明最优且可精确求解的解决方案，在长时间跨度的操作任务中显著提升了技能学习效率和长期规划能力。

Abstract: We consider the problem of autonomously learning robot skills under a limited practice budget for sequential tasks. We propose an active skill learning algorithm, \emph{Deliberate Practice (DP)}, that computes a provably \emph{budget-optimal} allocation---practicing skills that maximize expected cumulative reward while being learnable within the budget.
