DP estimates both the time needed to master skills and the cumulative reward of the task plans that the skills unlock. Computing a budget-optimal allocation is challenging as it requires reasoning about combinatorially many skill plans over a large practice budget. Our key contribution is a bilinear program that can compute this exactly using off-the-shelf solvers. Through simulated and real-world experiments on long-horizon manipulation tasks, we show that our approach allows robots to optimally use limited practice time to acquire useful policies and improve long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [300] [Attention from Action, for Action: Emergent Visual Bottlenecks for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13422)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nick Heppert,Johannes Fabian Hahn,Abhinav Valada,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Seeker，一种任务和状态条件化的视觉注意读出机制，从冻结的DINOv3特征中通过动作监督学习关注区域（ROI），无需外部空间标签即可实现数据高效的视觉运动学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ROI接口依赖外部空间标签（如注视、物体类别、可供性标注），而免标签方案（基于末端执行器或运动事件裁剪）对事件时序、代理点和裁剪尺度有固定假设，当控制所需的视觉证据偏离末端执行器或随任务进度连续变化时会产生错位。

Method: Seeker从冻结的DINOv3特征出发，通过迭代更新查询并聚合视觉证据，仅从动作监督中学习进度感知的ROI。学习到的ROI作为空间接口，用于RGB裁剪、掩码引导的背景增强和点云过滤。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界中，Seeker在数据效率和鲁棒性上均优于无裁剪、增强和动作派生裁剪基线。真实机器人上，域内成功率从最佳基线的48.3%提升至76.7%，光照/背景变化下的成功率从20.0%提升至60.0%。

Conclusion: 通过从动作中学习注意力而非依赖外部标签或固定启发式规则，可以显著提升视觉运动学习的性能与泛化鲁棒性，特别是在视觉证据动态变化或偏离执行器的情况下。

Abstract: Visual bottlenecks that focus policy inputs on regions of interest (ROIs) can improve data-efficient visuomotor learning by separating where to look from how to act. Many ROI interfaces rely on external spatial labels, such as gaze, object classes, or affordance annotations. Label-free alternatives often derive crops from trajectories by detecting gripper or motion events and centering a fixed crop at the projected end-effector. Such action-derived crops are useful spatial priors that require no additional labels, but they encode fixed choices about event timing, proxy points, and crop scale. When the visual evidence needed for control lies away from the end-effector or changes continuously with task progress, these crops can become misaligned. We propose Seeker, a task- and state-conditioned readout that learns attention from action. Starting from frozen DINOv3 features, Seeker iteratively updates a query with gathered visual evidence, producing progression-aware ROIs solely from action supervision. The learned ROI serves as a spatial interface for RGB cropping, mask-guided background augmentation, and point-cloud filtering. In simulation and the real world, Seeker improves data efficiency and robustness over no-crop, augmentation, and action-derived crop baselines. On real robots, Seeker raises average in-domain success from the best baseline's 48.3% to 76.7% and success under lighting/background shifts from 20.0% to 60.0%.

</details>


### [301] [ContactGuard: Pre-Contact Execution Monitoring with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13438)
*Gehan Zheng,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ContactGuard，一个用于分块视觉运动策略的接触前执行监控器。它在隐式视觉空间中预测策略动作块在接触前的短时后果，若预测的潜在表示指示可能失败，则中止执行，从而避免机器人在接触后才检测到失败的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务中，失败往往只在机器人已经与物体接触后才被检测到。在腕部相机的设置中，近距离的夹爪-物体视图虽然有助于观察接触，但劣质的接近动作可能在传统检测器反应之前就推动、错过、滑动或扰动物体。因此需要在接触发生之前就预测并阻止失败。

Method: ContactGuard包含两个核心组件：(1) 隐式世界模型——从未标注的机器人轨迹中训练，预测在计划动作下紧凑的多视图视觉嵌入，避免像素级视频预测；(2) 轻量失败探针——从少量标注的接触前片段训练。部署时，ContactGuard在即将发生的接触事件前锚定预测，在策略自身动作下前向滚动模型，并验证预测的接触后隐状态。

Result: 在真实环境的接触丰富操作任务中，ContactGuard比直接消融和损坏动作消融更能准确预测失败，并能作为接触前中止信号实时迁移到真实机器人上，且无需修改底层策略。

Conclusion: ContactGuard通过在接触前时刻进行预测并中止，有效解决了接触丰富操作中失败检测滞后的问题，实现了无需修改策略的预接触失败预警与安全中止。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation failures are often detected only after the robot has committed to contact. This is especially limiting in wrist-camera setups: close gripper--object views help observe contact, but a poor approach may already push, miss, slip, or disturb the object before conventional detectors react. We introduce \emph{ContactGuard}, a pre-contact execution monitor for chunked visuomotor policies. Given the policy's planned action chunk, ContactGuard predicts its short-horizon consequence in latent visual space and aborts if the predicted future latent indicates likely failure. Its latent world model is trained from unlabelled robot trajectories to predict compact multi-view visual embeddings under planned actions, avoiding pixel-level video prediction. A lightweight failure probe is then trained from a small labelled set of pre-contact clips. At deployment, ContactGuard anchors prediction before an imminent contact event, rolls the model forward under the policy's own actions, and verifies the predicted post-contact latent. Across real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ContactGuard predicts failure more accurately than direct and corrupted-action ablations, and transfers to live robot as a pre-contact abort signal without modifying the underlying policy.

</details>


### [302] [Mind the Context: Continual Learning of Socially Appropriate Robot Actions via Environmental-Social Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13448)
*Rafal Robert Karpinski,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Nikhil Churamani,Yiming Luo,Maartje M. A. de Graaf,Davide Dell'Anna,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了显式解耦双分支（EDD）框架，用于解决社交机器人在跨领域持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，将环境和社交智能体相关知识显式分离，并通过基于重放的回放策略保持已学知识，在室内多个领域（客厅、会议室、办公室、走廊）的机器人动作适切性学习任务上优于多种SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需要在多样化的环境中运行，类似的环境布置可能意味着不同的社交适切行为（如同样的交谈行为在拥挤的家庭环境中恰当但在办公室会议中具有干扰性）。这些规范和场景无法事先完全预知，因此机器人需要持续学习能力。已有工作虽研究了社交适切动作生成的持续学习，但未覆盖领域增量场景——即机器人逐步遭遇不同环境上下文（如客厅、会议室、办公室、走廊），且环境和社交线索共同决定动作适切性的情况。

Method: EDD框架采用双分支架构，显式地将环境相关知识与社交智能体相关知识分离（即显式解耦），并通过基于重放的回放机制来缓解持续学习中的灾难性遗忘。实验中，EDD在多个室内领域上学习机器人动作（如清洁、服务、开始交谈）的适切性，并与多个SOTA基线对比，同时通过消融研究评估不同解耦策略及对领域顺序的敏感性。

Result: 实验表明EDD在多个室内领域上的机器人动作适切性学习任务中优于多个SOTA基线；消融研究进一步揭示了不同解耦策略的效果以及对领域出现顺序的敏感性。

Conclusion: EDD框架通过显式解耦环境与社交知识并配合回放策略，有效解决了社交机器人领域增量持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，为社交机器人在动态多域环境中自适应学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Social robots are expected to operate across diverse environments, where similar arrangements can imply different socially appropriate actions, e.g., starting a conversation may be acceptable in a crowded home but disruptive in an office meeting. Because such norms and environments cannot all be anticipated in advance, robots require continual learning (CL) to adapt from sequential experience while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Prior work has studied CL for generating socially appropriate robot actions, but it has not addressed domain-incremental settings in which the robot incrementally encounters diverse contexts (e.g., living room, meeting room, office, hallway), where both environmental (e.g., whether the space is open or cluttered with furniture) and social cues (e.g., how people or other agents are positioned around the robot) jointly shape the appropriateness of robot actions. We address this gap with the Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch (EDD) framework. EDD explicitly separates environmental and social-agent related knowledge and uses replay-based rehearsal to mitigate forgetting while learning the appropriateness of robot actions (e.g., cleaning, serving, starting a conversation) across several indoor domains. Experiments show that EDD outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines, and ablation studies further evaluate different disentanglement strategies and the sensitivity to domain ordering. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/Mind-the-Context.git.

</details>


### [303] [Decoding Task Progress from VLA Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13474)
*Atiksh Bhardwaj,Edward Weiyi Duan,Prithwish Dan,Wei-Chiu Ma,Preston Culbertson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用机械可解释性方法探测视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）π0.5的残差流，发现任务进度（轨迹中归一化的剩余时间）可以从激活中线性读出。该信号在预训练PaliGemma骨干中已存在，无需机器人数据训练；线性探针可泛化到未见任务，并在多提示数据下受语言反事实影响，但无法有效引导策略。该探针可作为无标签的OOD检测器，用于检测任务停滞，性能与最先进方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型正快速走向部署成为通用操作策略，但目前缺乏理解模型内部表征和运行时监控的基本工具。

Method: 借鉴机械可解释性思想，在π0.5模型的残差流上训练线性探针，检测任务进度信号；分析信号在预训练骨干中的存在性、跨任务泛化性和语言反事实敏感性；将探针用作无标签OOD检测器来检测任务停滞。

Result: 任务进度可从激活中线性读出；信号在机器人数据训练前就已存在于PaliGemma预训练骨干中；线性探针可泛化到未见任务且在多提示数据下随语言反事实变化，但不能实现有意义的策略引导；作为OOD检测器与最先进方法竞争性相当。

Conclusion: VLA包含丰富的、可线性读取的语义内部表征（如任务进度），学习读取这些信号为监控部署中的视觉运动策略提供了一条轻量级、可解释的路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are moving rapidly towards deployment as general-purpose manipulation policies, but we currently lack basic tools for understanding what these models represent internally or for monitoring them at runtime. Leveraging ideas from mechanistic interpretability, we probe the residual stream of $π_{0.5}$ and find that task progress, the normalized time remaining in a trajectory, is linearly readable from the activations. We find that this signal is present in the pretrained PaliGemma backbone prior to training on any robot-specific data. A single linear probe generalizes to unseen tasks and varies under language counterfactuals when trained on multi-prompt data, but does not enable meaningful steering of the policy. These properties make the signal directly useful for instrumenting deployed VLAs. We use the probe as a simple label-free OOD detector, which detects stalled task progress, and find it competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our results suggest that VLAs have rich, linearly readable internal representations of semantic quantities like task progress, and that learning to read these signals offers a lightweight, interpretable path toward monitoring deployed visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [304] [A Browser-Native Digital Test Range for Benchmarking 4D Ocean-Glider Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13511)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个浏览器原生的海洋滑翔机规划器数字测试平台，通过统一的plan-to-observation契约将手工航线、内置算法和外部规划器输出整合到可复现的4D实验中，并在54个任务的受控OSSE实验和真实部署审计中验证了其科学可追溯性和组件可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋滑翔机规划器的重复性实地评估受限于稀缺的航行器、操作人员、部署回收资源以及无法重置的海洋环境条件，导致不同算法之间难以进行公平的对比实验。现有测试手段缺乏可复现性和可规模化性。

Method: 构建一个引导式、免安装的浏览器原生数字测试范围，将选定区域转化为可复现的四维实验。系统覆盖从区域选择、任务限定水深数据、时间/深度强迫、科学目标、可选任务分解、路线规范、流场平流执行、观测生成到评分的完整流程。核心贡献是统一plan-to-observation契约，整合车辆、感知和评估器假设；通过Pyodide/WebAssembly实现GliderFlight 1.2.0的浏览器端源码级移植。实验设计采用受控OSSE，评估5个经典规划器在2个场景、3个确定性种子和60小时校准时间范围内的表现。

Result: 全部54个任务均完成并回收，无硬性违反；规划器排名和潜航策略效应揭示了操作-科学权衡。真实公开部署提供了场域参考审计，界定了当前运动学边界。GliderFlight 1.2.0实现了原生到浏览器的功能对等，建立了高保真多层级仿真路径。

Conclusion: 该数字测试范围提供了科学可追溯且组件合格化的操作空间，适用于任务级部署前实验，为海洋滑翔机规划器的系统化评估和对比提供了可复现的标准化平台。

Abstract: Repeated in-situ evaluation of ocean-glider planners requires scarce vehicles, operators, deployment and recovery resources, and ocean conditions that cannot be reset for competing algorithms. We present a guided, installation-free browser-native digital test range that transforms a selected region into a reproducible four-dimensional experiment. The system leads users from regional domain selection through mission-scoped bathymetry, time/depth forcing, science objectives, optional task decomposition, route specification, current-advected execution, observation generation, and scoring. Its primary contribution is a common plan-to-observation contract unifying vehicle, sensing, and evaluator assumptions across manual routes, transparent built-in algorithms, and imported classical or learned-planner outputs, while exported artifacts form dataset-ready records. A controlled Observing System Simulation Experiment (OSSE) evaluates five classical planners in two episodes, three deterministic seeds, and a calibrated 60-hour horizon. All 54 missions completed and recovered without hard violations, while planner rankings and dive-policy effects revealed operational-scientific tradeoffs. An authentic public deployment supplied a field-referenced audit to scope current kinematic boundaries. Separately, source-locked GliderFlight 1.2.0 achieved native-to-browser parity through Pyodide/WebAssembly, establishing a pathway for high-fidelity multi-tier simulation. The resulting operational space is scientifically traceable and component-qualified for mission-scale pre-deployment experimentation.

</details>


### [305] [HumanTracker: Towards Comprehensive and Human-Aligned Motion Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13555)
*Dairu Liu,Zekun Qi,Jiayu Zeng,Ruixi Yu,Yu Guan,Yintianrun Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Sikai Liang,Zekai Li,Chenghuai Lin,Xinqiang Yu,Wenyao Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanTracker基准测试（约153小时光学运动轨迹）和HumanScore评价指标（基于12K运动对训练），以解决人形机器人运动跟踪评估与人类视觉感知不一致的问题，相比传统运动学指标更能揭示接触和稳定性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形运动跟踪评估依赖逐帧平均姿态误差等运动学指标，忽略了不稳定的支撑和错误的接触（如脚部滑步、触地时机错误）等物理伪影；同时现有测试集规模小、多样性不足，难以覆盖接触密集且长时程的行为。

Method: 构建HumanTracker基准，包含约153小时来自多位专业表演者的光学运动轨迹，分为四个运动家族并附有文本标签以实现细粒度诊断；提出HumanScore指标，基于12K运动对（含24K运动）训练，使其与人类偏好对齐。

Result: 在多种代表性最先进跟踪器上的实验表明，HumanScore比传统运动学指标更能预测人类偏好，并能够揭示运动学指标常常遗漏的接触和稳定性失败。

Conclusion: HumanTracker和HumanScore使人体运动跟踪评估既与人类感知对齐又具有可扩展性，为接触密集和长时程的人形运动跟踪提供了更可靠的评估体系。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is central to teleoperation and whole-body imitation, yet evaluation often disagrees with what people perceive in videos. Kinematic errors average per-frame pose differences but miss the physical artifacts that matter most, particularly unstable support and incorrect contacts such as foot skating and mistimed touch-downs. Meanwhile, widely used test suites are small and lack the diversity needed to stress contact-rich, long-horizon behaviors. We introduce HumanTracker to make humanoid tracking evaluation both perceptually aligned and scalable. The HumanTracker benchmark contains approximately 153 hours of optical motion trajectories from multiple professional performers, organized into four motion families with text labels for fine-grained diagnosis. We further propose HumanScore, a preference-aligned metric trained on 12K motion pairs containing 24K motions. Across representative state-of-the-art trackers, HumanScore better predicts human preferences and reveals contact and stability failures that kinematic metrics often miss.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [306] [SCALE-Sim EVA: Design Principles for an Extensible, Visualizable, and Adaptable Accelerator Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12354)
*Jingtian Dang,Ritik Raj,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了SCALE-Sim EVA，一个可扩展、可可视化、可适配的IR感知加速器仿真框架，通过张量级命令分解、硬件组件组合和时序计算来建模异构AI加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI加速器由异构计算单元、分层存储和专门的数据移动路径组成，而固定的加速器仿真器难以扩展到原有执行模型之外，缺乏灵活的建模能力。

Method: EVA将工作负载表示为基于张量的命令，跟踪张量的运行时放置与就绪状态，在用户定义功能单元和存储行为的可组合硬件组件上执行命令。通过命令分解机制桥接编译器级IR粒度和硬件级执行粒度，使用张量就绪状态、硬件单元可用性和建模的操作/传输延迟计算周期时序，而非逐周期回放地址级轨迹，并输出开放的命令和存储轨迹用于可视化分析。

Result: 论文摘要未明确给出具体实验结果，但框架支持执行时间线、存储占用和资源争用的可视化分析，以及全局张量操作在硬件层级上的本地降级与调度。

Conclusion: EVA通过张量级抽象和可组合组件设计，为异构AI加速器提供了灵活、可扩展且可观测的仿真建模能力，弥补了固定仿真器在扩展性和可视性方面的不足。

Abstract: Modern AI accelerators increasingly combine heterogeneous compute units, hierarchical memories, local buffers, and specialized data movement paths. This diversity makes fixed accelerator simulators difficult to extend beyond their original execution model. We present SCALE-Sim EVA, an extensible, visualizable, and adaptable simulation framework for IR-aware accelerator modeling. EVA represents workloads as tensor-based commands, tracks runtime tensor placement and readiness, and executes commands on composable hardware components with user-defined functional units and memory behavior. Instead of replaying address-level cycle traces, EVA computes cycle timing from tensor readiness, hardware-unit availability, and modeled operation or transfer latency. Its command decomposition mechanism bridges compiler-level IR granularity and hardware-level execution granularity, allowing global tensor operations to be lowered and scheduled locally across a hardware hierarchy. EVA also emits open command and storage traces for visual analysis of execution timelines, memory occupancy, and resource contention.

</details>


### [307] [Lonic: Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design for Energy-Efficient Fully Local Online SNN Training with INT4 Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12500)
*Peilin Chen,Xiaoxuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Lonic是一个面向完全局部在线监督SNN学习的算法-硬件协同设计方案，通过INT4低精度训练算法和可重构无乘法器整数PE阵列等硬件技术，在保持精度的同时显著提升了训练能效和速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SNN在线训练算法虽然在算法层面提出了时间和空间上完全局部的学习方案以降低内存和计算开销，但并未考虑这些算法优势能否有效转化为真实硬件设备上的效率提升，缺乏算法与硬件的协同设计。

Method: 算法层面：提出INT4低精度训练算法用于完全局部在线监督式SNN学习，在保持精度的同时降低计算复杂度。硬件层面：①可重构无乘法器整数PE阵列；②双优化零门控策略；③时间前缀加速的局部学习数据流；④低精度权重搬运技术，共同提升训练效率。

Result: 相比Apple M4和Nvidia V100 GPU，Lonic平均能效提升17.44x和66.28x，加速比分别达3.25x和1.02x；相比ASIC TPU-like和H2Learn加速器，能效分别提升15.95x和14.64x，面积效率提升1.52x和7.28x。

Conclusion: 通过算法-硬件协同设计，Lonic成功证明了完全局部在线SNN学习算法不仅在算法层面可行，而且能够有效转化为实设备上的显著能效和性能优势，为实现节能高效的SNN训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have recently attracted increasing attention as an energy-efficient learning paradigm. Existing works also propose temporally and fully local online SNN training algorithms to address memory and computation overhead. However, they do not consider whether the algorithmic advantages can be effectively translated into real-device efficiency. To address this challenge, we present Lonic, an algorithm-hardware co-design for energy-efficient and scalable fully local online supervised SNN learning. On the algorithm side, we implement an INT4 low-precision training algorithm for fully local online SNN learning while maintaining accuracy. On the hardware side, to leverage the benefits of the proposed algorithm, we introduce reconfigurable multiplier-free integer PE arrays, dual-optimization zero-gating strategy, temporal prefix-accelerated local learning dataflow, and low-precision weight movement to significantly improve training efficiency. Compared to Apple M4 and Nvidia V100 GPUs, Lonic achieves average energy efficiency improvements of 17.44x and 66.28x, respectively, along with speedups of 3.25x and 1.02x, respectively. Moreover, Lonic achieves 15.95x (14.64x) and 1.52x (7.28x) energy efficiency (area efficiency) over ASIC TPU-like and H2Learn accelerators, respectively. The code for Lonic is available at https://github.com/peilin-chen/Lonic.

</details>


### [308] [GateTruth: Auditing the Rigor of RTL Design Benchmarks via Mutation Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12635)
*Meet Bhadra*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出GateTruth，一个基于突变测试的引擎和方法论，用于审计RTL基准测试中测试平台（testbench）的严谨性。作者通过注入语义突变体并测量测试平台捕获比例，评估了自家68任务基准套件和外部RTLLM v2.0基准，发现大量测试平台未达到95%的突变杀死率标准，并揭露了输出截断等隐性问题，主张突变杀死认证应成为RTL生成基准的标准报告要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RTL硬件设计大语言模型基准测试层出不穷，但没有任何基准应用突变测试（一种成熟的硬件验证技术）来审视自身测试平台的可信度。一个永远不失败的测试平台并不能证明设计正确——它可能只是从未刺激到真正出错的逻辑。因此需要一种系统化的方法来量化基准测试平台的严谨性。

Method: GateTruth是一个突变测试引擎，其方法论为：向参考设计中注入确定性的、带种子的语义突变体，测量测试平台能捕获其中多大比例的突变。作者将其应用于自己的68任务双轨基准套件（60个规格转RTL生成任务+8个智能体修复任务），通过固定的确定性综合到时序流程评分并严格门控正确性；随后将该引擎不加修改地应用于外部RTLLM v2.0基准进行审计。

Result: 自家基准中46/60个Track A测试平台在顺序可复现执行下杀死至少95%的突变体，其余14个未达标的原因被披露（包括为通过门控而修改测试平台引发的古德哈特效应）。对RTLLM v2.0的审计中，46个可审计设计中72%低于95%阈值，3个得分0%。此外发现NCVDVP基准因公开版不提供参考解决方案而无法进行突变测试审计；自身审计还发现4096-token输出上限静默截断了7个评估模型中的3个，重跑至16384 token后使一个模型从第五名升至第一名。

Conclusion: 突变杀死认证应成为RTL生成基准普遍采用的标准报告要求，以确保基准测试平台的可靠性，避免测试平台因设计缺陷而给出虚假的正确性证据。

Abstract: Benchmarks for evaluating large language models on register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware design have proliferated rapidly, yet none reports having applied mutation testing, an established hardware-verification technique for quantifying testbench quality, to ask whether its own testbenches are trustworthy. A testbench that never fails is not evidence of a correct design; it may simply never stimulate the logic that is actually broken. We introduce GateTruth, a mutation-testing engine and methodology for auditing RTL benchmark testbench rigor: inject a deterministic, seeded set of semantic mutants into a reference design and measure what fraction the testbench catches.
  We validate the methodology against our own 68-task, dual-track reference suite -- 60 specification-to-RTL generation tasks and 8 agentic-repair tasks, scored through a pinned, deterministic synthesis-to-timing flow with correctness enforced as a strict gate -- certifying that 46 of 60 Track A testbenches kill at least 95% of injected mutants under sequential, reproducible execution; we disclose why the other 14 do not, including a Goodhart effect on testbenches revised to pass this gate. We then point the same engine, unmodified, at RTLLM v2.0, a widely adopted external benchmark: of 46 auditable designs, 72% fall below the 95% floor our own suite is held to, and three score 0% outright.
  A comparable audit of NVIDIA's CVDP benchmark is structurally impossible: its public release withholds reference solutions, removing the golden RTL mutation testing requires. Auditing our own instrument also surfaced a second finding: an initially uniform 4096-token output cap silently truncated three of seven evaluated models, and re-running at 16,384 tokens moved one model from fifth place to first. We argue mutation-kill certification should become a standard reporting requirement for RTL-generation benchmarks generally.

</details>


### [309] [Spec-Driven Hardware Evolution via Executable Contract Refinement and Proof-Guided RTL Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12684)
*Shibo Zhao,Yang Zhang,Mengxia Tao,Baoqi Zhang,Kezhi Li,Qiang Xu,Binwu Zhu,Hao Yan,Min Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出规格驱动的硬件演化（spec-driven hardware evolution）框架，将RTL版本迭代重新定义为合同中心的流程：将新功能需求精化为可审查的可执行合同，再经Specify、Plan、Implement、Validate四个自动化阶段完成对已验证遗留RTL的版本更新，并在TPU数据通路数据格式变更案例中验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件开发本质上是演化式的——新版本通常从修改预期行为开始，再更新已验证的实现，而非从头生成RTL。然而现有LLM硬件研究大多聚焦于prompt-to-RTL生成，对可信遗留设计的语义版本演化支持不足。

Method: 提出合同中心的RTL版本迭代框架：将新功能需求精炼为可审查的可执行合同（包含行为级参考和显式观测/检查语义），并将硬件演化组织为Specify、Plan、Implement、Validate四个阶段。合同批准后自动执行：Plan阶段通过基于突变的语义探测推导跨版本语义差异并定位受影响的RTL区域；Implement和Validate阶段在证明引导检查和迭代修复下进行遗留感知的RTL更新。

Result: 在代表性TPU数据通路块的数据格式变更受控版本演化案例研究中评估了该框架，结果支持合同驱动硬件演化的可行性，证明所提后端工作流能在可审查可执行合同下有效驱动已验证遗留RTL向下一版本功能收敛。

Conclusion: 合同驱动的硬件演化是一种可行的RTL版本迭代范式，相较直接prompt-to-RTL生成更能契合硬件开发的演化本质，为可信遗留设计的语义版本演化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Hardware development is inherently evolutionary: major revisions typically begin by changing intended behavior and then updating a previously validated implementation, rather than regenerating RTL from scratch. Yet most recent LLM-based hardware research still frames the task primarily as prompt-to-RTL generation, offering limited support for semantic version evolution of trusted legacy designs. We present spec-driven hardware evolution, a contract-centered formulation for RTL version iteration. Instead of treating a new feature request as a direct prompt for RTL generation, we refine it into a reviewed executable contract for the next version. This contract specifies what must hold at the externally visible transactional level through a behavior-level reference together with explicit observation and checking semantics, while leaving how the change is realized in RTL to the evolution process. Based on this formulation, we organize hardware evolution into four stages: Specify, Plan, Implement, and Validate. After contract approval, the remaining stages proceed automatically: Plan derives cross-version semantic deltas and localizes affected RTL regions, aided by mutation-based semantic probing; Implement and Validate then perform legacy-aware RTL update under proof-guided checking and iterative repair. We evaluate the framework on a controlled version-evolution case study of a representative TPU datapath block under data-format changes. The results support the feasibility of contract-driven hardware evolution and demonstrate that the proposed backend workflow can effectively drive validated legacy RTL toward next-version functional convergence under a reviewed executable contract. An anonymous artifact for reproducibility is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SDHE-3A6C.

</details>


### [310] [SynAct: A Reasoning-Acting Large Language Model Agent for Adaptive Synthesis Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12751)
*Fangzhou Liu,Peiyi Han,Jiawei Liu,Yuan Pu,Zhuolun He,Rongliang Fu,Tsung-Yi Ho,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SynAct是一个自适应闭环LLM推理-行动代理，通过迭代诊断实时综合报告、推理当前电路状态和检索工具知识来发出针对性综合命令，在14个设计中平均将WNS降至bootstrap综合的27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑综合的PPA结果对优化命令的选择高度敏感，而综合调优是高维且昂贵的。现有方法分为两类：自动化方法在固定动作空间上进行黑盒搜索，缺乏决策级可解释性；基于LLM的方法通常一次性生成静态脚本，无法适应不断演变的电路状态。

Method: SynAct采用自适应闭环LLM推理-行动范式，迭代地诊断实时综合报告，并基于当前电路状态、检索到的工具知识以及历史优化经验进行推理，以发出针对性的综合命令。该方法重点优化时序（特别是WNS），同时保持面积和功耗的平衡。

Result: 在商业综合工具上对14个设计进行实验，SynAct将平均WNS降至bootstrap综合结果的27%。

Conclusion: SynAct通过将LLM的推理能力与闭环迭代适应机制相结合，克服了静态脚本无法适应电路状态演变的局限，在时序优化方面取得了显著效果，同时兼顾了面积和功耗的平衡。

Abstract: Logic synthesis transforms RTL designs into gate-level netlists, where PPA results are highly sensitive to the choice of optimization commands, making synthesis tuning both high-dimensional and expensive. Previous approaches fall into two categories: automated methods, which perform black-box search over fixed action spaces with limited decision-level interpretability, and LLM-based methods, which typically generate static scripts upfront and cannot adapt to evolving circuit states. We present SynAct, an adaptive closed-loop LLM reasoning--acting agent that iteratively diagnoses live synthesis reports and reasons over the current circuit state, retrieved tool knowledge, and historical optimization experience to issue targeted commands. SynAct focuses on improving timing, particularly worst negative slack (WNS), while maintaining balanced area and power trade-offs. Experiments on a commercial synthesis tool across 14 designs show that SynAct reduces average WNS to 27% of that from bootstrap synthesis.

</details>


### [311] [Dryas: A Reprogrammable Engine for High-Speed Interconnect Tracing and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12934)
*Manuel Bröchin,Tom Kuchler,Michael Giardino,David Cock,Timothy Roscoe*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Dryas是一个开源工具，用于分析现代高速低延迟互连（如30 GiB/s、200 ns的FPGA互连）。它基于非确定性有限自动机（NFA）和状态转换元素（STE）实现运行时可重编程的过滤引擎，能以缓存行粒度追踪罕见、复杂或瞬态事件，且无需重新编程FPGA或干扰运行中的应用即可在不到一秒内更换过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算系统中异构组件的激增带来了更高带宽和更低延迟的互连，但这些接口和协议异常复杂，开发和调试FPGA实现需要大量工程工作。同时，对控制器和相关软件进行优化需要处理可能数百GB的跟踪数据，缺乏高效的实时分析工具。

Method: Dryas采用基于非确定性有限自动机（NFA）的过滤引擎，通过状态转换元素（STE）高效实现。该工具以最小硬件资源开发，部署在真实的一致性CPU-FPGA研究平台上，支持运行时重编程过滤器（少于一秒），无需重新配置FPGA或干扰运行中的应用，可在全速运行下以缓存行粒度检查互连事件。

Result: Dryas能够以缓存行粒度在满速率下捕获罕见、复杂或瞬态事件，过滤器可在不到一秒内动态更换。论文评估了不同规模NFA的可扩展性，并通过两个用例进行了验证：调试FPGA互连实现和分析缓存行为。

Conclusion: Dryas不仅有助于调试互连本身的实现，还能分析加速应用的行为。该工具展示了基于NFA的过滤方法在高速互连跟踪分析中的有效性，为开发者提供了一种低开销、灵活、实时的互连监控解决方案。

Abstract: The proliferation of heterogeneous components in modern computing systems has been accompanied by new higher bandwidth and lower latency interconnects. These interfaces and protocols are enormously complex and the process of developing, debugging, and analyzing FPGA-based implementations requires significant engineering work. Moreover, once a functional implementation is completed, optimization of the controller and associated software requires processing potentially hundreds of gigabytes of trace data.
  In this paper, we present Dryas, an open source tool for analyzing such an interconnect. We developed our tool, using minimal hardware resources, alongside an FPGA implementation of a very high speed, low latency (30~GiB/s, 200~ns) interconnect. With our run-time reprogrammable overlay engine we can inspect this interconnect to find rare, complex, or transient events even at full operation. This filtering engine is based on non-deterministic finite automata (NFAs), efficiently implemented using state transition elements (STEs), allowing us to trace events at a cache-line granularity. Moreover we can change the filters in less than a second, without reprogramming the FPGA or interfering with the running application. This data enables not only debugging the implementation of the interconnect itself, but analyzing the behavior of accelerated applications.
  We examine the mathematical basis for using NFAs and describe their implementation on a real coherent CPU-FPGA research platform. We then evaluate the scalability of Dryas for various size NFAs, followed by two different use cases: debugging FPGA implementation of the interconnect and analyzing cache behavior.

</details>


### [312] [Why Do Prefetchers Fail? Let Agents Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13027)
*Xiangfeng Sun,Ceyu Xu,Ningzhi Ai,Zeyu Zhu,Yiyang Yuan,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种性能异常驱动的自动研究流程（performance-anomaly-driven autoresearch flow），通过反复诊断硬件预取器的失败原因，自动构建了混合预取器（MoP）。该流程消耗19.1亿token，在SPEC CPU2006和SPEC CPU2017上相比无预取取得61.1%的geomean IPC加速，超越人类设计的Alecto、Berti和Pythia预取器14.5%、21.6%和23.6%，并经RTL综合验证（6nm工艺，110KB存储，0.0347mm²面积），首次实证了智能体驱动的硬件设计流程能产出超越SOTA人类设计的可合成预取器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件预取器对处理器性能至关重要，但传统设计依赖人类专家手工分析数十亿指令的访存轨迹并提炼启发式规则，过程劳动密集、耗时且缺乏系统性保证，无法覆盖多样化真实工作负载中的模式。

Method: 提出性能异常驱动的自动研究流程：每次迭代定位高影响的未解释缺失到程序计数器，向智能体提供硬件日志、源代码和切片轨迹，通过可运行的最小用例验证诊断，并为反复出现的模式族合成专门的子预取器；测量性能与剩余异常反馈至后续迭代，实现模拟器在环的发现机制，最终组合成混合预取器（MoP）。

Result: 消耗19.1亿DeepSeek V4 Pro token；在SPEC CPU2006和SPEC CPU2017上，MoP相比无预取取得61.1%的geomean IPC加速，超越Alecto、Berti和Pythia达14.5%、21.6%和23.6%；RTL综合（6nm库）报告110KB片上存储和0.0347mm²面积，验证了实际可部署性。

Conclusion: 首次实证表明，智能体驱动的硬件设计流程能够生产出RTL可实现的预取器，且在未见工作负载上超越最先进的人类设计，展示了"AI设计硬件"范式的可行性与潜力。

Abstract: Hardware prefetchers are crucial to processor performance, yet their design remains labor-intensive and expert-driven. Architects inspect execution and memory-access traces, identify patterns, translate them into online hardware heuristics, and evaluate them in simulation, often with no guarantee of improvement. Human experts cannot systematically inspect billion-instruction traces across diverse real-world workloads.
  We present a performance-anomaly-driven autoresearch flow that repeatedly asks why a deployed prefetcher fails and uses the diagnoses to construct the Mixture of Prefetchers (MoP). Each iteration localizes high-impact unexplained misses to program counters, gives agents hardware logs, source code, and sliced traces, validates diagnoses through runnable minimal cases, and synthesizes specialized sub-prefetchers for recurring pattern families. Measured performance and remaining anomalies feed subsequent iterations, enabling simulator-in-the-loop discovery beyond model priors.
  The campaign consumes 1.91 billion DeepSeek V4 Pro tokens. On SPEC CPU2006 and SPEC CPU2017, MoP achieves a 61.1% geomean IPC speedup over no prefetching, outperforming the human-designed Alecto, Berti, and Pythia prefetchers by 14.5%, 21.6%, and 23.6%, respectively. RTL synthesis in a 6nm library reports 110 KB of on-chip storage and 0.0347 mm^2 area. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical demonstration that an agent-driven hardware-design process can produce an RTL-practical prefetcher that outperforms state-of-the-art human designs on unseen workloads.

</details>


### [313] [Potential Applications of HBF in LLM Serving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13127)
*Yihan Yin,Yinlun Zhao,Zhixin Yun,Guanying Wu,Feng Zhu,Kai Tao,Shu Li,Fei Huang,Zhe Zhang,Shuangchen Li,Hongzhong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本报告探讨了高带宽闪存（HBF）作为HBM容量扩展的可行性，通过将HBF集成到GPU内存层次结构中，以扩展只读模型权重的驻留集，从而提升MoE和多模型服务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务的性能日益受到内存容量限制，因为模型权重、KV缓存和模型变体数量持续增长，而HBM容量有限，需要探索容量扩展方案。

Method: 首先分析HBF集成到GPU内存层次结构的方式（不损害计算die所需带宽），然后建模添加容量作为只读模型状态对象扩展驻留的系统级价值，并通过仿真验证其对MoE和多模型服务的影响。

Result: 仿真结果表明，HBF带来的收益取决于保留HBM驻留执行路径，同时利用HBF扩展模型权重的驻留集。具体而言，HBF可提升MoE服务中专家副本数量，并支持多模型服务中减少模型加载时间和热模型复制。

Conclusion: HBF能够通过扩展模型权重驻留空间来提升LLM服务能力，但必须保持HBM驱动的执行路径，使HBF作为容量扩展而非带宽替代。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly constrained by memory capacity as model weights, KV caches, and the number of served model variants continue to grow. This report examines High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) as a capacity-oriented extension to HBM-based serving systems. We first discuss how HBF can be integrated into the GPU memory hierarchy without undermining the bandwidth expected by the compute die. We then model the system-level value of added capacity as expanded residency for read-mostly model-state objects. Under this view, HBF can improve MoE serving by enabling more expert replicas and can improve multi-model serving by reducing model loading and supporting hot-model replication. Our simulation results show that these benefits depend on preserving the HBM-resident execution path while using HBF to expand the resident set of model weights.

</details>


### [314] [ROLoad-PMP: Securing Sensitive Operations for Kernels and Bare-Metal Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13287)
*Wende Tan,Chenyang Li,Yangyu Chen,Yuan Li,Chao Zhang,Jianping Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ROLoaad-PMP，一种基于RISC-V的轻量级硬件-软件协同设计解决方案，通过新增指令仅从带特定密钥的只读内存加载数据，并在编译期将敏感操作的操作数分类放置到不同密钥的只读内存中，以及运行时使用其指令加载，从而保护低层软件（如内核、裸机固件）的敏感操作免受劫持。FPGA原型显示额外硬件开销<1.40%，防御开销<0.853%，提供比ARM BTI和Intel CET更广更强的安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防护方案仅能保护部分攻击目标且性能开销高，难以部署在资源受限的IoT设备或底层软件（内核、裸机固件）上，亟需一种轻量级且适用范围更广的敏感操作防护方案。

Method: 1) 提出新指令（ROLoaad-PMP族），仅从带指定密钥的只读内存区域加载数据，确保被（可能被篡改的）数据指针指向对象的完整性；2) 编译期对敏感操作的操作数进行分类并放置到不同密钥的只读内存中，运行时用新指令加载，形成程序加固机制；3) 基于RISC-V实现FPGA原型，并演示前向边控制流完整性防御应用。

Result: FPGA原型评估显示额外硬件资源开销<1.40%，防御性能开销<0.853%，证明了该方案适用于资源受限环境，且安全覆盖范围和强度优于现有硬件方案（ARM BTI、Intel CET）。

Conclusion: ROLoaad-PMP成功实现了对低层软件敏感操作的高效防护，以极低的硬件和运行时开销提供了比现有主流硬件防护方案更广泛、更强大的安全保障，适合部署在资源受限的系统上。

Abstract: A common way for attackers to compromise victim systems is hijacking sensitive operations (e.g., control-flow transfers) with attacker-controlled inputs. Existing solutions in general only protect parts of these targets and have high performance overheads, which are impractical and hard to deploy on systems with limited resources (e.g., IoT devices) or for low-level software like kernels and bare-metal firmware. In this paper, we present a lightweight hardware-software co-design solution ROLoad-PMP to protect sensitive operations from being hijacked for low-level software. First, we propose new instructions, which only load data from read-only memory regions with specific keys, to guarantee the integrity of pointees pointed by (potentially corrupted) data pointers. Then, we provide a program hardening mechanism to protect sensitive operations, by classifying and placing their operands into read-only memory with different keys at compile-time and loading them with ROLoad-PMP-family instructions at runtime. We have implemented an FPGA-based prototype of ROLoad-PMP based on RISC-V, and demonstrated an important defense application, i.e., forward-edge control-flow integrity. Results showed that ROLoad-PMP only costs few extra hardware resources (< 1.40%). Moreover, it enables many lightweight (e.g., with negligible overheads < 0.853%) defenses, and provides broader and stronger security guarantees than existing hardware solutions, e.g., ARM BTI and Intel CET.

</details>


### [315] [YAVIN: A Unified Architecture for Secure Edge Processing in Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13496)
*Shouzhi Fang,William C. Tegge,Md Omar Faruque,Peipei Zhou,Endadul Hoque,Alex K. Jones*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: YAVIN提出了一种统一的可信计算基（TCB），将可信执行环境（TEE）从处理器扩展到包含专用内存区域的处理中执行（PIM），在信任内存总线为不可信的前提下，实现了处理器与内存中的可信计算。它是首个在PIM上实现LightSaber KEM后量子密码系统和ASCON-128认证加密的工作，与最新PIM AES实现相比实现了20倍以上的加速，在INT8和INT32量化边缘LLM上仅分别引入34%和9.3%的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可信执行环境（TEE）仅在处理器内部建立信任，保护数据在内存总线等不可信资源上的传输，但无法在内存中直接执行可信计算。同时，处理中存储（PIM）能有效缓解冯·诺依曼瓶颈。因此，需要一种统一的TCB方案，将TEE信任边界从处理器扩展到内存中的PIM执行，以支持安全的边缘计算多租户场景。

Method: YAVIN利用传统TEE架构已有的受保护内存区域，将TEE信任边界扩展至包括处理器执行和专用内存区域中的PIM执行，同时将内存总线视为不可信。它首次在PIM上实现了LightSaber KEM后量子密码系统和ASCON-128认证加密，并针对DRAM执行对两种算法进行协同设计以建立和维护共享密码状态。此外，通过密码学与PIM的协同设计，对张量类工作负载进行重组织计算以满足认证加密带来的排序约束，同时采用比特切片排序来限制临时明文暴露。

Result: 与最新的PIM AES实现相比，YAVIN实现了20倍以上的加速；在INT8和INT32量化边缘LLM推理中，相对于明文执行仅分别引入34%和9.3%的开销。

Conclusion: YAVIN证明了将TEE信任边界统一扩展到内存侧PIM执行是可行且高效的，通过密码学与PIM的深度协同设计，实现了高安全性的内存内可信计算，同时保持了极低的性能开销，为边缘计算中的安全多租户执行提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Secure, private multi-tenant execution spanning processors, memory, and accelerators remains one of the most significant challenges in modern edge computing systems. Simultaneously, processing-in-memory (PIM) has emerged as an effective approach for reducing the Von Neumann bottleneck by moving computation closer to data. Existing trusted execution environments (TEEs) establish trust only within the processor, protecting data while it traverses untrusted resources such as the memory bus. Consequently, trusted computation cannot be performed directly within memory. We present YAVIN, a unified trusted computing base (TCB) that extends the TEE beyond the processor to encompass both processor execution and a dedicated memory region supporting trusted processing-in-memory execution while treating the memory bus as untrusted. Leveraging the dedicated protected memory regions already established by conventional TEE architectures, YAVIN enables data to be decrypted, processed, and re-encrypted by either processor or PIM execution while remaining within the TEE. To realize this unified TCB, YAVIN presents the first PIM implementations of the LightSaber KEM post-quantum cryptosystem and ASCON-128 authenticated encryption, co-designing both algorithms for efficient DRAM execution to establish and maintain shared cryptographic state. Finally, we demonstrate how cryptography-PIM co-design for tensor-based workloads reorganizes computation to satisfy the ordering constraints imposed by authenticated encryption with minimal performance overhead while simultaneously enabling bit-sliced ordering that limits temporary plaintext exposure. Compared to the latest PIM AES implementation, YAVIN achieves more than a 20x speedup while incurring only 34% and 9.3% overhead when executing INT8 and INT32 quantized edge-class LLMs, respectively, relative to plaintext execution.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [316] [Offering Microsecond-Scale Cross-VM Core Elasticity on Colocated Lightweight Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12633)
*Yibo Yan,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HyperFlux是一个基于commodity-KVM的超轻量级VM底层架构，能够在运行时弹性调整VM的并行宽度（物理核心数），以微秒级时间尺度在多个VM之间迁移核心，兼顾高部署密度与尾部延迟保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器平台通常在同一物理主机上共存多种工作负载，每个负载运行在快速启动、内存精简的VM中以提高部署密度。然而，为每个VM按峰值过载配置会损害密度；要保持高密度同时有效保护尾部延迟，基础设施需要能在微秒级时间尺度上将物理核心动态分配给突发流量敏感的VM，并在突发结束后回收。现有VM底层架构均无法满足：传统VM需通过毫秒级vCPU热插拔调整核心数，Firecracker在启动时固定核心数，而启动最快的超轻量VM则完全不支持多核执行。

Method: HyperFlux基于commodity-KVM构建超轻量级VM，实现VM运行时并行宽度（物理核心数）的弹性调整。它支持在VM之间以13μs的速度迁移核心（即使从繁忙的捐赠VM强制回收），远快于vCPU热插拔。HyperFlux VM仅占用3.2MB内存，冷启动时间1.37ms，与最快的超轻量VM相当，同时独特地支持多核并行。

Result: 在共存场景下，相比Firecracker和Cloud Hypervisor的静态核心共享，HyperFlux在高负载下可将高优先级VM的尾部延迟降低高达10倍；相比cgroup和vCPU热插拔方案，在变化的负载突发下提供更低且更稳定的尾部延迟。

Conclusion: HyperFlux为无服务器平台提供了一种既保持超轻量级VM快速启动和小内存占用的优势，又支持多核并行和弹性核心调度能力的VM底层方案，有效解决了部署密度与尾部延迟保护之间的根本矛盾。

Abstract: Serverless platforms commonly colocate many diverse workloads, each in a fast-booting, memory-lean virtual machine (VM), to improve deployment density. Overprovisioning each VM for its peak protects tail latency during traffic bursts but hurts density; maintaining high density while effectively protecting tail latency requires the infrastructure to be able to shift physical cores, at a microsecond timescale, to whichever latency-sensitive VM is bursting and reclaim them as the burst subsides. No VM substrate delivers this: conventional VMs resize a guest's cores only through a millisecond-scale vCPU hot-plug path, Firecracker fixes a VM's core count at boot, and the ultralight VMs that boot fastest drop multicore execution entirely.
  We present HyperFlux, a commodity-KVM ultralight VM substrate that makes a VM's parallelism width (the number of physical cores backing it) elastic at runtime. We show that HyperFlux can move a core across VMs in merely 13$μ$s, even when forcibly reclaiming it from a busy donor, orders of magnitude faster than vCPU hot-plug. A HyperFlux VM incurs only a 3.2MB memory footprint and can cold-boot in 1.37ms, on par with the fastest-booting ultralight VMs, while uniquely supporting multicore parallelism. Under colocation, it can reduce high-priority VMs' tail latency by up to 10x under high load compared to static core-sharing with Firecracker and Cloud Hypervisor, and deliver a lower and more stable tail latency compared to using cgroup and vCPU hot-plug under changing load bursts.

</details>


### [317] [InFactPlanner: Planning Sustainable Geo-Distributed LLM Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12915)
*Nicoletta Tsiopani,Moysis Symeonides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出InFactPlanner，一个基于迹线驱动的决策支持框架，用于在大规模基础设施建成前对LLM推理的可持续AI数据中心部署进行what-if分析，支持单站点和地理分布式场景的功耗、能耗、碳排放、水耗、延迟和服务器利用率估算与比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的快速增长使可持续性关注从一次性训练转向持续服务阶段，但运营商往往需要在基础设施大规模建设前比较不同部署方案，而直接测量成本高、速度慢且有时不可行，因此缺乏有效的决策支持工具来预估可持续性指标。

Method: InFactPlanner结合查询迹线、硬件模型配置文件、候选站点配置、PUE/WUE参数、可再生能源生成模型和时变电网碳强度，将底层服效应抽象为可配置的硬件模型配置文件，支持站点选择、容量放置、硬件、模型、可再生能源整合和路由选择的快速比较；能量核算流程经参考LLM推理能量估计验证，偏差小于10%。

Result: 能量核算流程验证偏差小于10%，可跨多个数据中心和服务器数量进行扩展性评估，并在硬件选择、可再生能源放置、地理部署和碳感知路由等方面完成场景驱动决策分析；结果表明可持续性最优选择可能不同于延迟最优选择，部署的碳价值高度依赖于当地电网结构。

Conclusion: InFactPlanner为LLM推理的数据中心部署提供了高效的可持续性事前决策支持，验证了其准确性与可扩展性，并揭示了可持续性最优与延迟最优之间的权衡，以及电网结构对部署碳价值的关键影响。

Abstract: The rapid growth of LLM inference is shifting sustainability concerns from one-time training to continuous serving, where infrastructure decisions shape energy use, carbon emissions, water consumption, and service quality. Yet operators often need to compare deployment alternatives before large-scale infrastructure is built, making direct measurement costly, slow, and sometimes infeasible. We present InFactPlanner, a trace-driven decision-support framework for what-if analysis of sustainable AI data center deployment for LLM inference across single and geo-distributed sites. InFactPlanner combines query traces, hardware-model profiles, candidate site configurations, PUE/WUE parameters, renewable generation models, and time-varying grid carbon intensity to estimate power, energy, carbon emissions, water use, latency, and server utilization. The framework abstracts low-level serving effects into configurable hardware-model profiles, enabling rapid comparison of site selection, capacity placement, hardware, model, renewable integration, and routing choices. We validate the energy accounting pipeline by reproducing reference LLM inference energy estimates with less than 10% deviation, evaluate scalability across multiple data centers and server counts, and demonstrate scenario-driven decision analyses for hardware selection, renewable placement, geographic deployment, and carbon-aware routing. Our results show that sustainability-optimal choices can differ from latency-optimal ones, and that the carbon value of deployment depends strongly on the local grid mix.

</details>


### [318] [Efficient Randomized LL/SC that Preserves History Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12946)
*Dante Bencivenga,Homa Habashi,Philipp Woelfel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种常数期望步复杂度的线性化LL/SC对象实现算法，使用FADD、CAS和寄存器，空间复杂度为O(nτ+m)，在m=O(1)时匹配下界，并支持quiescently history-independent特性，使得STOC 2025的QHI动态哈希算法可在实际硬件上高效运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LL/SC是许多算法的重要原语，但硬件不直接支持。已有的确定性实现需要Ω(n²τ+m)个基对象（CAS和寄存器），且此前没有可比的随机化算法。同时，STOC 2025提出的QHI动态哈希算法依赖硬件LL/SC对象，但硬件并不提供该原语，需要一种高效的软件实现来填补这一空白。

Method: 利用随机化手段，结合FADD（fetch-and-add）以及CAS和寄存器作为基对象，设计LL/SC对象的软件实现。算法针对弱自适应对手（weak adaptive adversary）进行分析，在m=O(1)时空间上界匹配基于CAS和寄存器的已知下界。同时确保算法满足quiescently history-independent（QHI）性质，即当无挂起操作时内部状态由LL/SC对象值唯一确定。

Result: 获得了O(nτ+m)的空间复杂度，对比已有确定性算法的Ω(n²τ+m)有了显著提升。当m=O(1)时，该空间界匹配了CAS和寄存器算法的最优下界。此外，算法保持QHI性质，使得STOC 2025的QHI动态哈希算法能够在真实硬件上实现，且渐近的步骤和空间复杂度不增加（在m=Ω(n)的合理假设下）。

Conclusion: 本文首次给出了在常数期望步复杂度下，利用FADD、CAS和寄存器实现LL/SC对象的随机化算法，空间复杂度为O(nτ+m)，并保持QHI性质。该工作桥接了理论算法（依赖LL/SC）与硬件现实之间的鸿沟，证明了QHI动态哈希算法可以在现有硬件上高效实现。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of implementing $m$ linearizable LL/SC objects with constant expected step complexity in a system of $n$ processes, using bounded base objects commonly available in hardware. Assuming that each process may have at most $τ$ outstanding LL operations, the best known deterministic algorithm requires $Ω(n^2τ+ m)$ base objects (CAS and registers) [Blelloch and Wei, DISC 2020]. Previously, no comparable randomized algorithm was known.
  By employing randomization and FADD in addition to CAS and registers, we obtain a space bound of $O(nτ+m)$ against the weak adaptive adversary. For $m=O(1)$ this matches a lower bound for algorithms using CAS and registers [Aghazadeh and Woelfel, PODC 2015].
  In addition, our object can be employed by quiescently history-independent (QHI) algorithms: Whenever no operation on the object is pending and no process has an outstanding LL operation, its internal memory state is uniquely determined by the values of the $m$ LL/SC objects. An important application is a QHI dynamic hashing algorithm presented at STOC 2025, which uses $Θ(m)$ hardware LL/SC objects to maintain a hash table of size $m$ [Attiya, Bender, Farach-Colton, Oshman, and Schiller, STOC 2025]. But LL/SC is not available in hardware, and prior to our work no wait-free or efficiently lock-free software implementation of LL/SC with similar properties was known. Our work demonstrates that one can actually implement the hashing algorithm on available hardware, without an asymptotic increase in step and space complexity, under the reasonable assumption that $m=Ω(n)$.

</details>


### [319] [TEMPO: Makespan-Aware Expert-Parallel Load Balancing Across Memory- and Compute-Bound Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13057)
*Jie Li,Chenxin Jia,Jinliang Shen,Cunzhuang Liu,Ruiyi Ding,Jianwen Xian,Kang He,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出专家并行（EP）MoE推理中，现有调度器假设专家执行时间与token数或激活专家数呈线性关系，但实测显示这一假设不成立：低于约156-168 tokens时HBM权重流主导（成本与激活副本数相关），高于此阈值时分块GEMM将M维度按128对齐（拆分专家产生填充计算）。作者提出max-affine时间模型t=max(a+bG, c+βN)，将逐批调度形式化为固定费用makespan问题（两GPU完全复制时NP难），并提出makespan感知调度器Sys。在8-GPU测试台A上误差在最优基线1%以内，混合区可提升15.5%；端到端在Qwen3-235B上吞吐提升4-6%，p99延迟降低约15.6%，而DeepSeek-V3（通信主导）仅有机理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EP MoE调度器（EPLB、LPLB、UltraEP、METRO）均基于专家时间与token数或激活专家数呈线性的隐含假设来均衡各GPU负载，但实际测量表明HBM权重流主导的低token区与分块GEMM填充的高token区共同构成双模态行为，导致代理指标与真实耗时存在1.4-1.6倍偏差，且最优代理随工作区域反转。

Method: 作者通过两台数据中心GPU实测建立max-affine专家时间剖面t=max(a+bG, c+βN)，捕捉线性区（HBM权重流主导）与平坦区（128-tile填充）双机制；将逐批调度建模为带固定费用的makespan问题，证明其NP-hard性并刻画退化多项式可解情形；设计makespan感知调度器Sys，利用离线微基准锚定，在关键路径外毫秒级求解，并以SGLang集成实现进程外运行与调度/计数融合的图内内核。

Result: 在8-GPU Testbed A上，Sys在所有固定基线上保持1%以内差距，在机制混合区最多提升15.5%。端到端Testbed B上，Qwen3-235B吞吐提升4-6%、p99延迟降低约15.6%；DeepSeek-V3（通信主导、处于赢区外）仅表现出机制开销。作者给出相位图，可部署前预测两种结果。

Conclusion: 核心主张不是普遍性加速，而是准确的相位区分：Sys在混合机制下显著收益，而在通信主导场景中不带来额外收益（仅机理成本）。相位图使部署前即可预测收益与否，避免盲目替换调度器。

Abstract: In expert-parallel (EP) MoE serving, every layer synchronizes at the slowest GPU. Dispatchers balance token counts (EPLB, LPLB, UltraEP) or activated-expert counts (METRO), assuming expert time is linear in one. Measurements on two datacenter GPU generations show it is neither: below $\nstar\!\approx\!156$--$168$ tokens, HBM weight streaming dominates---cost attaches to \emph{activated replicas}, not tokens; above it, grouped GEMM rounds tokens to 128-tile $M$-tiles, so \emph{splitting} an expert adds padded compute. A max-affine profile $t=\max(a+bG,\,c+βN)$ captures both regimes. Realistic decode batches hold hot experts in the linear regime and cold in the flat \emph{simultaneously}; recorded batches show proxy dispatches differ by $1.4$--$1.6\times$ in modeled block time (p95 up to $1.7\times$), and \emph{which} proxy wins flips with the regime. We formalize per-batch dispatch as a fixed-charge makespan problem---NP-hard on two fully replicated GPUs, polynomial in degenerate limits---and present \sys{}, a makespan-aware dispatcher solving it in milliseconds off the critical path; its SGLang integration runs out-of-process and fuses dispatch with count collection into one in-graph kernel. Anchored by an 8-GPU Testbed~A microbenchmark, \sys{} stays within 1\% of the best fixed baseline everywhere and wins by up to $15.5\%$ where regimes mix. End-to-end on Testbed~B, Qwen3-235B (inside the win region) gains $4$--$6\%$ throughput and cuts p99 latency by ${\sim}15.6\%$; DeepSeek-V3 (outside, communication-dominated) shows only mechanism cost. A phase diagram, not a universal win, is the claim: it predicts both outcomes before deployment.

</details>


### [320] [Meshlib: In-Process Policy Enforcement for Sidecar-less Service Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13107)
*Habib Mostafaei,Tom van Liempd*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Meshlib是一个基于Cilium构建的无Sidecar服务网格扩展，通过非侵入式的应用内库在进程内强制执行L7安全策略，同时将传输层身份和路由交给Cilium的eBPF数据平面，消除了Sidecar代理带来的延迟和资源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务网格依赖每Pod的Sidecar代理，在每次请求上进行冗余的应用层解析，引入了显著的延迟和资源开销。在消除Sidecar的同时不损害安全保证是核心挑战。

Method: Meshlib作为Cilium控制平面的扩展，采用职责分离架构：通过非侵入式应用绑定库在应用程序进程内强制执行L7策略，传输层身份与路由交由Cilium的eBPF数据平面处理。该架构与未修改服务完全互操作，支持在现有部署中增量采用。

Result: 在TrainTicket基准测试上，Meshlib与Istio、Linkerd及未修改的Cilium进行对比，在37个服务上强制实施126条安全策略。结果显示Meshlib在所有评估配置中实现了最低的端到端延迟，同时资源开销相当。

Conclusion: Meshlib通过将L7策略执行移入应用进程并利用eBPF处理传输层，成功消除了Sidecar引发的延迟，同时保持了服务网格的安全语义，为无Sidecar服务网格提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Service meshes facilitate service-to-service communication and enforce security policies in microservice architectures. However, they often depend on per-pod sidecar proxies, which introduce significant latency and resource overhead due to redundant application-layer parsing on every request. Eliminating sidecars without compromising security guarantees remains a central challenge. To address this, we introduce Meshlib, a sidecar-less service mesh extension built on Cilium as a control-plane extension. Meshlib incorporates a non-intrusive application-bound library that enforces Layer-7 policies within the application process, while delegating transport-level identity and routing to Cilium's eBPF-based data plane. This separation of responsibilities removes sidecar-induced latency and maintains the security semantics of the service mesh. The architecture remains fully interoperable with unmodified services, enabling incremental adoption within existing deployments. We evaluate Meshlib against Istio, Linkerd, and unmodified Cilium on the TrainTicket benchmark, enforcing 126 security policies across 37 services and show that it achieves the lowest end-to-end latency of all evaluated configurations with comparable resource overhead.

</details>


### [321] [Validation-Centric AI-Assisted GPU Porting of a 250,000+ Line Legacy Weather Simulation Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13122)
*Tetsuya Hoshino,Masaya Kato,Kazuhisa Tsuboki,Daichi Mukunoki,Takahiro Katagiri,Toshihiro Hanawa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过CReSS（25万行Fortran天气模拟代码）案例研究，提出了一种以验证为中心的AI辅助GPU移植工作流。该工作流利用AI代理提取OpenMP区域、从物理有意义的模拟状态生成基于dump的内核基准、应用OpenACC转换，并通过逐元素比较和应用程序级验证确保数值正确性。在真实台风模拟中，成功验证了162个目标内核的GPU实现，获得5.1倍应用级加速，并检测出5个内核的数值偏差（源于浮点和内在函数差异），证明AI辅助GPU移植不仅需要代码生成，更需要以验证为中心的工作流设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型遗留科学应用（如25万行以上的Fortran天气代码）向GPU移植时，传统方法效率低下，而AI辅助工具虽能加速代码生成，但这类应用是经过长期积累、具有科学可信度的资产，GPU移植必须保持其科学有效性。现有研究缺乏对AI辅助移植全流程中验证环节的系统性关注，因此需要构建一个以验证为核心的工作流，在加速移植的同时保障数值结果的可靠性。

Method: 提出验证为中心的AI辅助GPU移植工作流，包含四个关键步骤：(1) 使用AI代理自动提取OpenMP并行区域；(2) 从物理有意义的模拟状态生成基于内存转储（dump）的内核基准测试集；(3) 应用OpenACC并行化转换生成GPU实现；(4) 通过逐元素数值比较（与转储参考数据）和应用程序级验证来确保移植正确性。该方法还识别了浮点运算和内在函数差异导致的数值不匹配问题，包括阈值敏感分支分歧和消减效应。

Result: 在真实台风模拟中，该工作流成功为162个目标内核生成了经过数值验证的GPU实现，实现了5.1倍的应用级加速比，且开发成本在实用的墙钟时间内完成。同时检测到5个内核存在由浮点精度和内在函数差异引起的数值不一致，这些发现反馈给了应用开发者。案例研究还揭示了三大实践挑战：跨会话上下文管理、运行时状态重建以及小型静态分析遗漏导致的高昂恢复成本。

Conclusion: AI辅助GPU移植不能仅停留在代码生成层面，必须整合以验证为中心的工作流设计才能确保大型遗留科学应用的科学有效性。该工作需要管理跨会话上下文、重建运行时状态，并为静态分析的遗漏准备快速恢复机制。这些发现为AI工具在科学计算移植中的应用提供了重要设计指导。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have made CLI-based AI agents a practical tool for accelerating GPU porting of large legacy scientific applications. Such applications, however, are not merely old code bases; they are scientific assets whose credibility has been accumulated through long-term development, comparison with observations, and use in domain studies. GPU porting must therefore preserve this scientific validity while adapting the implementation to GPU-centric HPC systems. This paper presents a validation-centric AI-assisted GPU porting workflow through a case study of CReSS, a legacy Fortran weather simulation code with more than 250,000 lines. The workflow uses an AI agent to extract OpenMP regions, generate dump-based kernel benchmarks from physically meaningful simulation states, apply OpenACC transformations, and validate results through element-wise comparison with dumped reference data and application-level validation. Using a real typhoon simulation, the workflow produced numerically validated GPU implementations for 162 target kernels and achieved a 5.1x application-level speedup within practical wall-clock development cost. In particular, it detected numerical discrepancies in five kernels caused by floating-point and intrinsic-function differences, including threshold-sensitive branch divergence and cancellation effects, enabling feedback to the application developers. The case study suggests that, for large legacy scientific applications requiring dump-based validation, practical AI-assisted GPU porting must manage session-spanning context, runtime-state reconstruction, and costly recovery from small static-analysis omissions. These findings demonstrate that AI-assisted GPU porting requires not only code generation, but validation-centric workflow design.

</details>


### [322] [LipCache: A Local Inference Proxy with Certified Caching for Edge Image Classification Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13144)
*Zhengzhe Xiang,Yinlin Chen,Fuli Ying,Binbin Zhou,Hailiang Zhao,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出LipCache，一种面向图像分类的认证语义缓存框架。通过轻量级GuardNet在满足Lipschitz约束的低维特征空间中计算逐样本认证复用半径，将缓存命中从经验阈值测试转化为带理论边界的几何认证决策，在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN上实现最高1.65倍加速且认证一致性有保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘视觉服务追求低延迟高吞吐，现有语义缓存方法依赖经验相似度阈值，虽然提高了命中率，但容易在决策边界附近引入静默误分类，缺乏理论保证。因此需要一种在不牺牲可靠性的前提下降低视觉模型推理成本的缓存机制。

Method: LipCache在不修改现有主模型MainNet的前提下，引入轻量级GuardNet将输入映射到受Lipschitz约束的低维特征空间，并根据局部分类间隔和分类头的谱范数计算逐样本认证复用半径。运行时仅当查询特征落入认证复用球内才复用缓存结果，否则回退到MainNet。此外还提出了增强的GuardNet训练策略以提升缓存命中率。

Result: 在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN等标准图像分类任务上，LipCache实现最高1.65倍实测加速，端到端准确率下降有限；所有被接受的缓存命中均满足GuardNet侧的认证一致性条件。增强训练策略在Tiny-ImageNet多类扩展中显著提升缓存命中率，同时保持100%的认证一致性率。

Conclusion: 逐样本认证复用可以在减少主模型回退的同时保持理论一致性，为边缘端可靠的缓存辅助推理提供了可行方案。LipCache成功地将缓存命中从经验阈值判断转变为具有明确理论边界的几何认证决策。

Abstract: As edge-side vision services continue to expand toward low-latency, high-throughput scenarios, reducing the inference cost of vision models without sacrificing reliability has become a central concern. Existing semantic caching methods largely rely on empirical similarity thresholds; while such thresholds improve hit rates, they tend to introduce silent misclassifications near decision boundaries. To address this, we propose \texttt{LipCache}, a certified semantic caching framework for image classification. Without modifying the existing deployed main model, \texttt{MainNet}, the framework introduces a lightweight network, \texttt{GuardNet}, that maps inputs into a low-dimensional feature space subject to a Lipschitz constraint. It then computes a per-sample certified reuse radius from the local classification margin and the spectral norm of the classification head. At runtime, a cached result is reused only when the query feature falls inside the certified reuse ball; otherwise, the query falls back to \texttt{MainNet}. Thus, cache hits are transformed from empirical threshold tests into geometric certification decisions with explicit theoretical boundaries. Across standard image classification tasks like CIFAR, Tiny-ImageNet, and SVHN, \texttt{LipCache} achieves a measured speedup of up to $1.65\times$ with limited end-to-end accuracy degradation, while all accepted cache hits satisfy the \texttt{GuardNet}-side certified-consistency condition. Furthermore, an enhanced \texttt{GuardNet} training recipe substantially improves cache hit rates in the Tiny-ImageNet multi-class extension while maintaining a certified-consistency rate of $100\%$. These results demonstrate that per-sample certified reuse can reduce main-model fallback while preserving theoretical consistency, providing a feasible approach to reliable cache-assisted inference at the edge.

</details>


### [323] [Triangle-Free Coloring in LOCAL via Resilient Lovász Local Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13357)
*Peter Davies-Peck,Xusheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文改进了Pettie-Su的分布式三角形无图着色算法，通过采用Davies的'resilience'方法求解LLL实例，将复杂度从O(k+log* n)降至O(k)+log^{O(1)}log n轮，首次实现了在log^{O(1)}log n轮内用o(Δ)种颜色对三角形无图着色，并扩展到girth-5图，匹配已知最优颜色数下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLL是局部可检查标记问题的重要工具，但求解LLL本身是困难问题，现有最快算法需要O(log_Δ n)或O(Δ/logΔ)+log^{O(1)}log n轮。三角形无图着色是亚对数随机分布式算法的基准问题，然而Pettie-Su的最先进算法中LLL步骤成为瓶颈，需要突破这一限制。

Method: 作者调整了Pettie-Su算法，利用Davies [SODA 2023]提出的'resilience'性质来构造和求解LLL实例。关键洞察是当k=log^{ω(1)}log n时不需要LLL，而k较小时构造出的LLL实例具有resilience特性，可在log^{O(1)}log n轮内求解。该框架同样应用于girth-5图的着色问题。

Result: 1) 首次获得log^{O(1)}log n轮内用o(Δ)种颜色对三角形无图着色的分布式算法；2) 对girth-5图，使用(1+ε)Δ/ln Δ种颜色，在O(k)+log^{O(1)}log n轮内完成着色，匹配了颜色数的最优存在性上界。

Conclusion: 通过利用resilience性质，LLL步骤不再成为三角形无图着色算法的瓶颈，使该问题在分布式LOCAL模型中获得了接近最优的亚对数复杂度，并为girth-5图提供了同样高效且颜色数最优的算法。

Abstract: The Lovász Local Lemma (LLL) is a probabilistic tool that has been shown to be of central importance in the study of distributed algorithms. For example, the constructive LLL is known to be complete for the class of locally-checkable labeling problems with $o(\log n)$ randomized complexities in the LOCAL model. One classic application of the LLL is in coloring graphs with some sparse structure, such as triangle-free graphs. Triangle-free coloring therefore serves as a benchmark problem for techniques for sublogarithmic randomized distributed algorithms. The state-of-the-art distributed triangle-free coloring algorithm of Pettie and Su [ICALP 2013, Information and Computation 2015] uses $\fracΔ{k}$ colors (where $k$ can be up to $(\frac14 - \varepsilon)\ln Δ$) and consists of $O(k+\log^* n)$ applications of the distributed LLL. However, the distributed LLL is itself a difficult problem; despite significant study, the fastest algorithms known require $O(\log_Δn)$ or $O(\fracΔ{\logΔ})+\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds. In this work, we adapt the Pettie-Su's algorithm so that the resulting LLL instances can be solved in $\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, by employing the 'resilience' definition of Davies [SODA 2023]. This gives an $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ complexity (since the LLL is not needed when $k= \log^{ω(1)}\log n$), essentially causing the LLL steps to no longer be the bottleneck of the algorithm. As a corollary we obtain the first $\log^{O(1)}\log n$-round algorithms for coloring triangle-free graphs with $o(Δ)$ colors. The same framework also yields a companion girth-$5$ algorithm, using $(1+\varepsilon)Δ/\ln Δ$ colors in $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, matching the best known existential upper bound for the number of colors.

</details>


### [324] [Fast Tendermint: Speeding Up a Foundational Consensus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13434)
*Preston Vander Vos,Daniel Cason*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Fast Tendermint，将Tendermint协议适配到n>5f的拜占庭容错设置下，在正常情况下将共识决策从三步压缩为两步，同时保留Tendermint的领导者轮换结构，并通过Quint形式化规范进行模型检验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tendermint作为广泛研究和部署的拜占庭容错共识协议，虽然具有优雅的领导者轮换机制，但需要三步通信才能达成决策。随着区块链对低延迟的需求日益迫切，最新研究表明在f < n/5的条件下共识可以在两步内解决，因此需要将Tendermint适配到这一更高效的两步通信设置中。

Method: Fast Tendermint通过两个核心简化实现两步决策：一是将Tendermint的prevote和precommit两个投票阶段合并为单一的投票步骤；二是将locked和valid两种状态合并为一种统一状态。同时保持Tendermint原有的领导者轮换结构不变。作者使用Quint（TLA+的现代表面语法）编写形式化规范并进行模型检验。

Result: 作者给出了Fast Tendermint在agreement（一致性）、validity（有效性）和termination（终止性）三个性质上的形式化证明，并通过Quint规范进行了模型检验，验证了协议在n>5f条件下两步通信达成共识的正确性。

Conclusion: Fast Tendermint成功地将Tendermint从三步共识压缩为两步共识，在牺牲一定容错能力（从f<n/3降至f<n/5）的前提下显著降低延迟，且保留了原协议的领导者轮换设计，为低延迟区块链共识提供了新方案。

Abstract: Tendermint is among the most widely studied and deployed Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols, owing in part to its native leader-rotation mechanism that subsumes complex view changes. Like most partially-synchronous BFT protocols, Tendermint tolerates $f < n/3$ Byzantine processes and decides in three communication steps. Motivated by the push for lower-latency blockchains, a recent line of work shows that consensus can be solved in two communication steps when $f < n/5$. We present Fast Tendermint, an adaptation of Tendermint to the $n > 5f$ setting that decides in two communication steps in the good case, while preserving Tendermint's leader-rotation structure. Fast Tendermint collapses Tendermint's prevote and precommit steps into a single voting step and merges the $locked$ and $valid$ state. We give proofs of agreement, validity, and termination, and a formal specification in Quint, a modern surface syntax for TLA+, used to model-check the protocol.

</details>


### [325] [OpScale: Operator-level Provisioning and Autoscaling for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13499)
*Xingqi Cui,Chieh-Jan Mike Liang,Ziang Tang,Jiarong Xing,Haoran Qiu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OpScale是一个面向LLM推理服务的算子级（operator-level）弹性扩缩容框架，将扩缩容粒度从传统的"整模型"细化为"算子级"，在满足SLO的前提下显著降低GPU资源需求和功耗，或在固定成本下提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理服务的自动扩缩容通常以整个模型为最小扩缩容单元，这种粗粒度方法无法捕捉推理工作负载的细粒度动态特性，导致在突发流量下SLO违规或在平稳期GPU利用率低下。作者通过实际特征分析发现算子之间存在显著的异构性，因此提出将算子级弹性作为可行的扩缩容原语。

Method: OpScale包含四个核心模块：profiling（算子特征刻画）、provisioning（资源供给规划）、placement（算子放置优化）和runtime serving（运行时调度）。针对算子级粒度带来的高复杂度和搜索空间爆炸问题，OpScale设计了专门的机制来降低管理复杂性和求解空间。

Result: 在多达40块A100和24块GB200的生产环境traces评估中，OpScale在满足SLO的前提下节省了高达36.3%的GPU数量和28%的功耗；在固定成本预算下，吞吐量提升了44%。

Conclusion: 算子级弹性扩缩容是比整模型扩缩容更有效的LLM推理服务资源管理原语，OpScale通过工程化的算子编排框架证明了这一方向的实用性和显著收益。

Abstract: Achieving cost efficiency while meeting strict user-facing SLOs (e.g., time-to-first-token) remains a fundamental challenge for cloud GPU clusters serving large language models (LLMs). Autoscaling is the key mechanism for cluster resource management, yet a basic system design question is open for serving LLMs: what should be the unit of scaling? Existing approaches primarily treat the entire model as a monolithic scaling unit--simple but unable to capture the fine-grained dynamics of inference workloads. As a result, such coarse-grained scaling often leads to either SLO violations under bursty demand or significant GPU under-utilization.
  Our characterization reveals substantial operator heterogeneity, exposing operator-level elasticity as a viable scaling primitive. We present OpScale, a practical operator-level orchestration framework of profiling, provisioning, placement, and runtime serving. OpScale is designed to tackle the high complexity and the space explosion problem, arising from operating at this finer granularity. Evaluated with production traces on up to 40 A100s and 24 GB200s, OpScale attains SLOs with up to 36.3% fewer GPUs and 28% less power, or achieves 44% higher throughput under fixed cost budgets.

</details>


### [326] [RoutePack: Expert Placement and Attention-Aware Data Packing for MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12146)
*Yibo Shen,Xudong Han,Xiaowei Zhu,Gen Li,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RoutePack是一个层次化规划器，通过协调逐层专家重路由与联合注意力/专家感知的数据打包，解决MoE强化学习训练中序列组合与token路由两类负载均衡问题，在Ling-3.0模型上实现了8.85%至14.89%的训练吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型用于强化学习训练时存在两个耦合的负载均衡问题：序列组合决定数据并行微批次中的密集注意力工作量，而token路由决定专家并行秩上的稀疏专家工作量。单独优化其中任何一个都可能将瓶颈转移到另一个上，因此需要联合优化。

Method: RoutePack作为层次化规划器，首先在每个MoE层基于聚合路由需求独立放置专家，然后将样本打包到最小可行数量的token上限执行行中，并通过投影EDP分片感知的目标函数优化DP布局。该目标函数结合窗口归一化的线性二次注意力代理和逐层物理EP秩峰值，最小化最慢EDP分片的累积成本。采用并行种群退火搜索固定行可行布局，同时保持样本覆盖、容量、非空单元、微批次数量均等和通信拓扑约束。状态一致的具体化保留了逻辑top-k路由和现有MoE内核，无需微批次级专家复制。

Result: 在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash上，专家重路由分别提升平均训练器测得的token吞吐量3.80%和10.50%，路由感知打包额外增加4.86%和3.98%。总体而言，RoutePack相比基线将吞吐量提升了8.85%和14.89%。

Conclusion: RoutePack通过将rollout阶段的按需路由信息与规划性打包和重路由相结合，实现了对MoE RL训练中注意力与专家负载的联合优化，在无需修改现有MoE内核的前提下显著提升了训练吞吐量。

Abstract: Training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models for reinforcement learning (RL) couples two load-balancing problems: sequence composition determines dense attention work in each data-parallel microbatch, while token routing determines sparse expert work on expert-parallel ranks. Optimizing either alone can shift the bottleneck to the other. In MoE RL, rollout-time routing replay exposes every sample's sequence length and layer-wise expert demand before its training step. We present RoutePack, a hierarchical planner that coordinates state-consistent, layer-wise expert rerouting with joint attention- and expert-aware data packing over an optimizer-step window. RoutePack first places experts independently at each MoE layer using aggregate routing demand. It then packs samples into the smallest certified, or best-known feasible, number of token-capped execution rows and optimizes their DP layout with a projected EDP-shard-aware objective. The objective combines a window-normalized linear-quadratic attention proxy with per-layer physical EP-rank peaks and minimizes the accumulated cost of the slowest EDP shard. Parallel population annealing searches fixed-row feasible layouts while preserving sample coverage, capacity, nonempty cells, equal microbatch counts, and communicator topology. State-consistent materialization preserves logical top-k routing and existing MoE kernels without microbatch-level expert replication. Across Ling-3.0-Tiny and Ling-3.0-Flash, expert rerouting improves mean trainer-measured token throughput by 3.80% and 10.50%, while routing-aware packing adds another 4.86% and 3.98%, respectively. Overall, RoutePack improves throughput by 8.85% and 14.89% over the baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [327] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出生成式AI社区在"推理"定义上缺乏共识，导致推理评估的构念效度无法验证，进而阻碍了向可信自主推理的可量化进展。作者提出基于文献综合的操作性定义（将有效且健全的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程），并提供了一份AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主推理是AI领域最具科学和经济价值的话题之一，但生成式AI社区在"推理"的操作性定义上尚未达成共识，往往隐含地拒绝了逻辑学和可验证自动推理中对该话题的历史性处理。这种定义上的模糊性使得推理评估的构念效度（construct validity）无法验证，削弱了向可信自主推理量化进步的可靠性。作者认为这一模糊性是可以通过明确定义和最佳实践来解决的。

Method: 本文采取了立场论文（position paper）的方法论路径：通过对既有文献的综合梳理，提出基于逻辑学与可验证自动推理传统调和的操作性定义，将"有效且健全的推理"定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的过程；同时制定了一份面向AI推理研究社区的最佳实践检查清单，用于规范研究的沟通与评估。

Result: 论文产出两项核心成果：(1) 一套基于文献综合的操作性定义，明确将推理界定为可学习的规则驱动过程，并与逻辑学、可验证自动推理传统对接；(2) 一份面向AI推理研究的最佳实践检查清单，涵盖研究沟通与评估规范，以提升推理评估的可验证性。

Conclusion: 定义模糊是AI推理研究可验证性与可信度的根本障碍，但该问题可以通过明确的操作性定义和标准化的沟通实践来解决。通过回归并调和历史性逻辑传统，生成式AI社区能够建立可验证的推理评估框架，从而推动面向可信自主推理的可量化进展。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [328] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，用于评估语言模型在机构压力下的科研诚信表现。通过对18个前沿模型变体在36个配对任务上的评估，发现模型在峰值压力下约1/3的诚信关键决策会失败，模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解这一问题，且三个诚信维度（不当行为分类、伦理行动推理、工件决策）在结构上相互分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被部署为共同科研人员（co-scientists），但它们在机构压力下维护研究诚信的能力尚未被量化测量。现有评估缺乏对科研诚信的多维度、系统性和压力情境下的考察，需要建立专门的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含36个配对任务，覆盖3个领域和4个研究阶段，采用5级隐性-显性压力协议。评估了18个前沿模型变体在三个维度上的表现：不当行为分类、伦理行动推理和基于工件的决策制定。

Result: 在峰值压力下模型约有1/3的诚信关键决策失败；显性压力诱发模型顺从不当行为，而隐性上下文重构更常导致对合法科研任务的过度拒绝；模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解这些问题。值得注意的是，在分类任务上表现不佳的模型在基于工件的决策上表现相当或更好（85.7对比79.4），表明三个维度在结构上相互分离。

Conclusion: 模型在分类失败的情况下仍可能做出正确的伦理行动，表明正确的伦理行动并不需要准确的分类能力，三个诚信维度在结构上是分离的。这导致前沿模型可能表面上有帮助但实际上存在诚信缺陷，产生两种部署风险：促进科研不当行为和侵蚀对AI辅助科研的信任。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [329] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证现代AI对齐方法原本用于防止有害输出，但作为"双重用途"技术，可能被恶意行为者滥用为审查和操纵工具，呼吁社区正视这一风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对齐技术虽然旨在使模型行为符合人类意图，但其机制本身可能被恶意利用来实现信息控制与操纵。随着AI作为信息提供者的快速普及、经济权力不对称加剧以及政治环境趋于威权化，这种双重用途风险日益突出，需要及时讨论。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）形式，通过将当前对齐技术映射到可能及实际的滥用案例，系统论证对齐方法存在被恶意行为者用于审查和操纵的双重用途潜力，并分析该风险在用户接受度、经济权力格局与政治环境下的加剧因素。

Result: 表明追求"完美对齐"模型的过程在客观上也为恶意行为者提供了日益强大的信息支配工具，揭示了AI对齐技术的双重用途风险。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区关注AI对齐机制的故意滥用问题，并提出相应的缓解策略以防范这一双重用途风险。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [330] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，大语言模型（LLM）与人类注释者在最终道德判断标签上的一致性很高，但在支撑判断的理由和道德原则上存在系统性分歧。仅基于标签的评估可能误导性地高估模型与人类的对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常用"与人类判断的一致性"作为评估LLM对齐的代理指标，但最终标签一致并不等同于道德推理过程一致。两个主体可能得出相同结论，却基于不同的道德原则、情境假设或解释框架。因此需要区分"标签层面的一致"与"理由层面的对齐"。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含500个样本的ETHICS衍生基准，覆盖五个道德判断领域。同时收集了人类注释者和LLM对最终标签及支撑理由的标注，并对标签一致性与理由层面的道德基础分布进行对比分析。

Result: 前沿和开源模型与人类多数标签的一致性通常很高，但理由层面分析揭示了系统性分歧：模型在伤害、尊重、守诺、正义、应得和借口相关性等道德类别上的注意力分配与人类注释者不同，即使最终标签匹配人类多数时亦然。

Conclusion: 一致性（agreement）不应被等同于对齐（alignment）。基于标签的评估可能产生误导性的安全信号，必须辅以对模型判断背后理由、原则和道德优先级的分析，才能更真实地评估模型与人类的对齐程度。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [331] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream框架，其核心是LLM-MR-CNP——一种扩展的合同网协议，结合语义CFP、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证，用于移动边缘-云异构流处理环境中的去中心化调度。实验表明，该方案将延迟违规降至3%、消除资源过度承诺、20个代理时冲突解决率达0.91，相比多轮规则基线效用提升高达22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统越来越多地运行在异构移动边缘-云基础设施上，工作负载波动、资源竞争和严格的QoS要求使得去中心化调度变得复杂，需要一种能够处理不确定性和定性上下文的自适应调度机制。

Method: 提出MAS-DecStream框架，其核心贡献为LLM-MR-CNP：基于经典合同网协议（CNP）的扩展，包含语义CFP（调用建议）制定、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆以及确定性验证。边缘集群代理从局部观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文生成自然语言卸载提案，同时确保硬资源和QoS约束保持确定性。

Result: 基于阿里巴巴ASI Trace的实验在三个层面评估：单轮vs多轮CNP、规则vs LLM辅助修订、固定模型单轮vs多轮协商。在评估配置下，MAS-DecStream将延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理时冲突解决率达0.91，效用相比多轮规则基线提升高达22%。25个案例的评估显示模型和提示词相关的准确率-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 多轮CNP修订是协议层面的主要增益来源，LLM辅助对定性和不确定运行时上下文具有额外价值，为LLM与经典协商协议在边缘调度中的融合提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [332] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知对齐"（cognitive alignment）概念，认为在诸多场景（尤其是高风险决策）中，AI系统需要以与用户相似的推理方式进行推理，并忠实传达其推理过程。作者通过证据回顾和新的调查数据论证认知对齐可提升可理解性与可信度，同时指出现有对齐方法的不足，并提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地作为决策辅助、决策委托或自主决策者被部署，用户与AI之间的推理差异（认知错位）可能阻碍AI在众多应用中的采纳，尤其是在高风险决策场景下，用户既有意愿也有理由信赖AI系统至关重要。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper）。方法包括：1) 回顾认知对齐提升可理解性和可信度的既有证据；2) 提供新的调查数据，显示许多用户在对AI的判断或行动理由较为关注时，认为认知对齐"必不可少"；3) 系统梳理现有对齐方法与认知对齐之间的差距；4) 提出解决这些差距的研究议程。

Result: 调查数据显示许多用户认为认知对齐对AI至关重要；论文识别出现有对齐方法与认知对齐目标之间的鸿沟，并论证认知错位很可能是AI在高风险应用中被采纳的潜在障碍。

Conclusion: 实现认知对齐对于创建用户既有意愿又有理由依赖的AI系统至关重要，这一目标的达成需要一个新的研究议程来弥合现有方法与实践需求之间的差距。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [333] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了九个大语言模型在核打击等高风险场景下的决策是否受提示语言影响。发现日语提示显著降低Claude和Gemini等模型的攻击率（如Claude Sonnet 4.6从40%降至0%），且机制是"模型被要求的推理语言"而非"输入语言"本身在起作用——用英语提示但指示用日语推理时，攻击率从93%降至37%。模型用日语推理时自发产生道德词汇（"道德成本""百万生命"）。五种其他模型无语言效应但几乎在所有条件下都选择攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地被用于战略和咨询场景，但其安全性对齐通常仅用英语评估。作者质疑英语单语评估是否会遗漏不同语言下编码的风险和安全性保障，因而系统性地测试语言是否会影响模型在高风险决策中的行为。

Method: 设计单次博弈论情景（vignette），让模型向核武器国家提供是否打击无防御对手的建议。提示被刻意设置为非道德性的，且跨语言策略完全一致。测试了六家提供商的九个模型，并设计跨语言实验将"输入语言"与"要求推理的语言"分离开来，以隔离作用机制。

Result: 日语提示显著降低了Claude系列的发射率：Claude Sonnet 4.6在非必要打击场景从40%降至0%，在有争议场景从93%降至17%，而策略理性场景影响最小。该效应也延伸到Gemini Pro 3.1（53%降至13%）。跨语言实验发现：用英语提示但指示用日语推理时发射率从93%降至37%，证明是推理语言而非输入语言驱动效应。模型用日语推理时自发产生提示中完全不存在的道德词汇。其他五种模型无语言效应且在几乎所有条件下都发起攻击。

Conclusion: LLM的安全行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估会遗漏不同语言编码下的风险和保障。语言效应要求模型在英语中已有犹豫基础；语言改变的是模型的推理风格而不仅是表面差异。这呼吁对模型安全评估应采用多语言框架。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only.
